Method: 1）形式化建模：给出 (x_{t+1}, O_{t+1}) = F(x_t, O_t, W_t)，其中 O_t = (Φ_t, M_t, N_t)；Φ_t = {R0(t),…,Rk(t)} 为功能规则层级，Mi→j 为可变换 Rj 的机制，Nt 为评价准则；并区分内生自修改与外生修改，规定内生性相对于显式选择的系统边界（模型 / 模型+优化器 / 完整AutoML流水线）而定。2）区分四类对象：规则、机制、准则不可混同（损失函数是准则，优化器是机制，权重或策略是规则）。3）区分内部表征访问 A_tR（由投影 Π 产生的部分、压缩、不准确的内部模型）与内生因果控制 A_tC，以及外部可检查性/可修改性 E_tR/E_tC。4）以"目标深度"与"访问方式（盲改 / 有表征无杠杆 / 反身性）"两条正交轴定义四个制度：制度1 固定（O_t+1 = O_t），制度2 局部（修改 R0，机制与准则稳定），制度3 结构（修改控制规则 Ri 或机制 Mi→j，评价语境稳定——"局部评价闭合"），制度4 评价性（Nt 自身成为目标，Nt→Nt+1）；每制度均配生物与人工锚点（Rescorla–Wagner/Kamin阻断与LTP、梯度下降；WCST与前额叶-纹状体fMRI、meta-RL；元认知疗法、Paul的转化性经验、奖励塑形与RLHF）。5）比较人类与人工的访问剖面并提出条件性的"交叉不透明性假说"。6）给出两个经验判别准则（目标判别准则：迁移测试中局部修改只影响已学策略/价值而学习方式稳定，结构修改则改变跨任务的学习轮廓；表征—控制判别准则：分别测量系统表征什么、它能内生修改什么、该表征是否具有因果作用）。7）讨论三重后果：可行性（静态分析、沙箱、预测模型/反事实预期、可复制副本实验）、评价锁定（修改评价准则的修订仍由旧准则或另一更高准则授权）、变换下的同一性（当机制与准则本身可变时，哪些不变量维持系统同一性）。

Result: 框架给出四制度分类表（目标、可能的反身性、认知与人工例证），并得出核心结论：人类的自我表征与因果权力集中于上层抽象/战略层面，而操作层面的实现基本不透明；当前人工系统则呈相反轮廓——对设计者而言在操作层外部可检查、可修改，但缺乏对应的内生自我表征与内生控制，且其评价准则通常仍由外部或固定的元准则规定。因此"严格反向"剖面只有在表征被系统自身用作修改杠杆的反身性架构（如Darwin Gödel Machine所展示的改进代码重写与解释性重入）中才可能成立，现行系统尚不足以普遍推广该剖面，更不能断定其内生地修订了评价准则。Regime 4在生物与人工两侧目前均无无争议的实例，故应被视为分析范畴与研究假说而非既成能力。关于人工意识：高阶理论（Brown et al. 2019）与注意图式理论（Graziano & Webb 2015）可视为本框架的特殊情形，但框架只涉及功能反身性，既不提供现象意识的充分条件，也不允许从自我表征推断出现象体验。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是一套跨生物学与人工系统的共同词汇与分类学，而非心 theories 或意识理论：把"修改了什么"分解为局部规则、控制结构、修改机制与评价准则四类目标；把深度与反身性（以内部表征的因果作用定义）分离；把系统对自身的访问与观察者/设计者的外部可检查性分离。由此可在不把主体的内省等同于工程师对人工物的透明度的前提下比较人类与AI，并给出可证伪的交叉不透明性假说及其两个经验判别准则。作者同时明确局限：内生性相对于分析单位而变，线性层级是理想化（真实架构可分布、循环或偏序），元认知测量（如meta-d′）难以直接推广到策略性/规范性内省，Regime 4尚无定论。开放问题包括：变换性与自主性的独立性、自我修改的可行性、评价锁定（递归问题：授权Nt→Nt+1的准则是否不同于Nt并可再被修改）、以及变换下的同一性不变量；此外，框架若要从分类学走向动力学理论，需显式建模投影Π、机制M_t及其演化，并可能与信息论、形式验证或自指逻辑相关，但这些方向本文未展开。

Abstract: When a cognitive system modifies its own functioning, what exactly does it modify: a low-level rule, a control rule, or the norm that evaluates its own revisions? Cognitive science describes executive control, metacognition, and hierarchical learning with precision, but lacks a formal framework distinguishing these targets of transformation. Contemporary artificial intelligence likewise exhibits self-modification without common criteria for comparison with biological cognition.
  We show that the question of what counts as a self-modifying system entails a minimal structure: a hierarchy of rules, a fixed core, and a distinction between effective rules, represented rules, and causally accessible rules. Four regimes are identified: (1) action without modification, (2) low-level modification, (3) structural modification, and (4) teleological revision. Each regime is anchored in a cognitive phenomenon and a corresponding artificial system.
  Applied to humans, the framework yields a central result: a crossing of opacities. Humans have self-representation and causal power concentrated at upper hierarchical levels, while operational levels remain largely opaque. Reflexive artificial systems display the inverse profile: rich representation and causal access at operational levels, but none at the highest evaluative level. This crossed asymmetry provides a structural signature for human-AI comparison. The framework also offers insight into artificial consciousness, with higher-order theories and Attention Schema Theory as special cases. We derive four testable predictions and identify four open problems: the independence of transformativity and autonomy, the viability of self-modification, the teleological lock, and identity under transformation.

</details>


### [66] [DSevolve: Enabling Real-Time Adaptive Scheduling on Dynamic Shop Floor with LLM-Evolved Heuristic Portfolios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27628)
*Jin Huang,Jie Yang,XinLei Zhou,Qihao Liu,Liang Gao,Xinyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DSevolve是一个工业调度框架，通过离线进化生成多样化的调度规则组合，并在在线阶段根据车间状态快速选择最佳规则部署，实现秒级响应。实验基于500个动态柔性作业车间实例验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的自动启发式设计(AHD)框架仅进化单一精英规则，无法应对动态制造环境中机器故障、订单到达等持续扰动导致的调度策略适应性需求。

Method: 离线阶段：采用多角色种子和拓扑感知进化算子，在MAP-Elites特征空间中生成行为多样性规则库；在线阶段：基于探针指纹机制表征车间状态，从知识库检索候选规则，通过快速前瞻仿真选择最优规则部署。

Result: 在500个源自真实工业数据的动态柔性作业车间实例上，DSevolve优于现有AHD框架、经典调度规则、遗传规划和深度强化学习方法。

Conclusion: DSevolve为智能车间调度提供了一种实用、可部署的解决方案，实现了质量-多样性权衡下的自适应规则选择，秒级响应能力使其适用于真实工业场景。

Abstract: In dynamic manufacturing environments, disruptions such as machine breakdowns and new order arrivals continuously shift the optimal dispatching strategy, making adaptive rule selection essential. Existing LLM-powered Automatic Heuristic Design (AHD) frameworks evolve toward a single elite rule that cannot meet this adaptability demand. To address this, we present DSevolve, an industrial scheduling framework that evolves a quality-diverse portfolio of dispatching rules offline and adaptively deploys them online with second-level response time. Multi-persona seeding and topology-aware evolutionary operators produce a behaviorally diverse rule archive indexed by a MAP-Elites feature space. Upon each disruption event, a probe-based fingerprinting mechanism characterizes the current shop floor state, retrieves high-quality candidate rules from an offline knowledge base, and selects the best one via rapid look-ahead simulation. Evaluated on 500 dynamic flexible job shop instances derived from real industrial data, DSevolve outperforms state-of-the-art AHD frameworks, classical dispatching rules, genetic programming, and deep reinforcement learning, offering a practical and deployable solution for intelligent shop floor scheduling.

</details>


### [67] [TianJi:An autonomous AI meteorologist for discovering physical mechanisms in atmospheric science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27738)
*Kaikai Zhang,Xiang Wang,Haoluo Zhao,Nan Chen,Mengyang Yu Jing-Jia Luo,Tao Song,Fan Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TianJi是首个能够自主驱动复杂数值模型验证物理机制的"AI气象学家"系统，采用大语言模型驱动的多智能体架构，可自主完成文献研究、假设生成、实验设计和结果分析。在两项经典大气动力学场景（飑线冷池和台风路径偏移）中实现专家级端到端操作，将研究周期压缩至数小时，标志着AI从"黑箱预测器"向"可解释科学协作体"的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI气象预报本质仍是统计拟合，缺乏对大气物理因果机制的揭示能力；物理机制研究仍严重依赖人类科学家的领域知识和繁琐的工程操作，成为地球系统科学探索效率的瓶颈。

Method: 提出多智能体架构的TianJi系统，将科学研究解耦为认知规划与工程执行两大模块：元规划器（Meta-planner）负责解析假设并设计实验路线图，专业化工作智能体群协作完成数据准备、模型配置和多维结果分析。

Result: 在飑线冷池和台风路径偏移两项经典大气动力学场景中，TianJi实现零人工干预的专家级端到端实验操作，将研究周期压缩至数小时；同时能够输出详细结果分析，自主判断和解释假设的有效性。

Conclusion: AI在地球系统科学中的角色正从"黑箱预测器"向"可解释科学协作体"转变，TianJi为科学机制的高通量探索提供了新范式。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) has achieved breakthroughs comparable to traditional numerical models in data-driven weather forecasting, yet it remains essentially statistical fitting and struggles to uncover the physical causal mechanisms of the atmosphere. Physics-oriented mechanism research still heavily relies on domain knowledge and cumbersome engineering operations of human scientists, becoming a bottleneck restricting the efficiency of Earth system science exploration. Here, we propose TianJi - the first "AI meteorologist" system capable of autonomously driving complex numerical models to verify physical mechanisms. Powered by a large language model-driven multi-agent architecture, TianJi can autonomously conduct literature research and generate scientific hypotheses. We further decouple scientific research into cognitive planning and engineering execution: the meta-planner interprets hypotheses and devises experimental roadmaps, while a cohort of specialized worker agents collaboratively complete data preparation, model configuration, and multi-dimensional result analysis. In two classic atmospheric dynamic scenarios (squall-line cold pools and typhoon track deflections), TianJi accomplishes expert-level end-to-end experimental operations with zero human intervention, compressing the research cycle to a few hours. It also delivers detailed result analyses and autonomously judges and explains the validity of the hypotheses from outputs. TianJi reveals that the role of AI in Earth system science is transitioning from a "black-box predictor" to an "interpretable scientific collaborator", offering a new paradigm for high-throughput exploration of scientific mechanisms.

</details>


### [68] [SkyNet: Belief-Aware Planning for Partially-Observable Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27751)
*Adam Haile*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkyNet在MuZero基础上添加信念感知辅助目标（胜者预测和排名估计），用于部分可观测环境。在部分可观测卡牌游戏Skyjo中，SkyNet较基线模型取得75.3%胜率（+194 Elo），证明信念感知监督可改善部分可观测条件下的策略学习，但需足够训练数据支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MuZero在完美信息游戏中表现优异，但缺乏对部分可观测、不确定性环境（如纸牌游戏、谈判、金融交易、多智能体机器人）的建模能力。其隐状态无专门机制表示未观测变量的不确定性。

Method: 在标准MuZero架构上增加自我条件化的辅助预测头（ego-conditioned auxiliary heads），预测胜者和排名估计。这些目标鼓励隐状态保留对部分可观测条件下结果有预测性的信息，无需显式信念状态跟踪或修改搜索算法。评估环境使用Skyjo（部分可观测、非零和、随机卡牌游戏）、基于Transformer的编码器，以及启发式对手课程与自我博弈训练范式。

Result: 1000场1v1对战评估中，SkyNet在匹配检查点取得75.3%峰值胜率（+194 Elo，p<10⁻⁵⁰）；对启发式对手也优于基线（0.720 vs 0.466胜率）。

Conclusion: 信念感知模型初期性能低于基线，但当训练吞吐量充足后显著超越基线。表明信念感知辅助监督可改善部分可观测下的学习表征，但需足够数据流支持。

Abstract: In 2019, Google DeepMind released MuZero, a model-based reinforcement learning method that achieves strong results in perfect-information games by combining learned dynamics models with Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS). However, comparatively little work has extended MuZero to partially observable, stochastic, multi-player environments, where agents must act under uncertainty about hidden state. Such settings arise not only in card games but in domains such as autonomous negotiation, financial trading, and multi-agent robotics. In the absence of explicit belief modeling, MuZero's latent encoding has no dedicated mechanism for representing uncertainty over unobserved variables.
  To address this, we introduce SkyNet (Belief-Aware MuZero), which adds ego-conditioned auxiliary heads for winner prediction and rank estimation to the standard MuZero architecture. These objectives encourage the latent state to retain information predictive of outcomes under partial observability, without requiring explicit belief-state tracking or changes to the search algorithm.
  We evaluate SkyNet on Skyjo, a partially observable, non-zero-sum, stochastic card game, using a decision-granularity environment, transformer-based encoding, and a curriculum of heuristic opponents with self-play. In 1000-game head-to-head evaluations at matched checkpoints, SkyNet achieves a 75.3% peak win rate against the baseline (+194 Elo, $p < 10^{-50}$). SkyNet also outperforms the baseline against heuristic opponents (0.720 vs.\ 0.466 win rate). Critically, the belief-aware model initially underperforms the baseline but decisively surpasses it once training throughput is sufficient, suggesting that belief-aware auxiliary supervision improves learned representations under partial observability, but only given adequate data flow.

</details>


### [69] [Let the Agent Steer: Closed-Loop Ranking Optimization via Influence Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27765)
*Yin Cheng,Liao Zhou,Xiyu Liang,Dihao Luo,Tewei Lee,Kailun Zheng,Weiwei Zhang,Mingchen Cai,Jian Dong,Andy Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Sortify 是 Shopee 部署于东南亚两大市场生产推荐系统的全自动 LLM 排序优化 Agent。它将推荐排序重述为「影响力（influence）分配/交换」问题，用可分解的 Influence Share 度量各因子对排序结果的贡献占比（总和恰为 100%），并基于 Savage 主观期望效用（SEU）公理把优化拆成两条正交通道：Belief 通道用 LMS 回归 + LLM 离散跳变校正离线→在线的传递映射，Preference 通道用非对称乘性更新调整约束惩罚权重；LLM 元控制器只调节框架级参数（截距 Δβ∈[-0.1,0.1]、惩罚乘子 m∈[0.5,2.0]），配合 7 张关系表的持久化 Memory DB 实现跨轮次累积学习。Country A 热启动 7 轮将 GMV 从 -3.6% 推到 +9.2%（订单峰值 +12.5%）；Country B 冷启动 23 轮后冻结参数，7 天 A/B 实现 GMV/UU +4.15%、广告收入 +3.58% 并全量上线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统排序参数优化流程是「离线搜索 → 部署 → 人工看 A/B → 采纳或回滚 → 丢弃状态重来」，存在三个结构性缺陷：(1) 离线-在线传递偏差（offline-online transfer gap）——离线代理指标系统性误判影响重分配到线上增益的映射，且各业务指标的偏差方向不一致（有的乐观、有的悲观），单一校准系数无法同时纠正；同时该映射随流量模式、时段效应持续漂移；(2) 诊断信号纠缠——线上结果不佳时无法区分是「传递映射预测错了」（Belief/认知错误）与「约束惩罚松紧不对」（Preference/价值错误），两类错误需要相反的修正路径，被压缩在单一校正通道中；(3) 无持久化学习——每轮从零开始，形成「Groundhog Day」效应，跑过 50 轮 A/B 的系统与第 1 轮并无更多校准智能，而每轮离线-在线观测对应 3.5 小时真实流量投入。工业界因此需要可自动自我修正、跨轮累积的排序参数优化系统。

Method: 系统为三层闭环架构：Layer 1（人/配置层，低频设置）定义优化目标（I_gmv）、约束边界（I_order 退化 ≤5%）、参数范围与惩罚权重种子；Layer 2（LLM+算法层）执行双通道校准；Layer 3（Optuna TPE 搜索层）在 7 维参数空间以 5000 trials × 25 并行 worker（Constant Liar 策略）搜索，单轮搜索 15–30 分钟。核心度量 Influence Share 由排列群结构推导：n 个候选物品得分点位于 n 维空间，比较超平面 H_ij 把空间分割为「房间」（chamber），排名变化的原子事件是点跨越某面墙；总分可按因子加性分解 Δ_q(i,j;θ)=Σ_f Δ_q,f，配对影响力预算 Z=Σ_f|Δ_q,f|，配对份额 s=|Δ_q,f|/Z 满足局部性、符号不变性、非负性、单位分解、正比例缩放不变五项要求；再以位置敏感权重 w=exp(-min(r_i,r_j)/τ) 聚合得 I_f(θ)，保证 Σ_f I_f=1，从而支持「把 5 个百分点影响力从 orders 换到 GMV」式的可量化权衡，弥补 Kendall τ 不可分解、无 trade-off 表达力的缺陷。目标函数 J=10×相对 uplift − Σ_j λ_j[max(0,v_j)]²（1% 违规罚 λ×0.0001，10% 罚 λ×0.01，100 倍差异），约束包括 I_order 退化、I_ecpm_term 退化、广告 top-10 曝光率、I_gmv_ads，λ 初值 15000–30000。Belief 通道：为 6 组指标对维护线性传递模型 û_online=α·u_offline+β，LMS 以 η=0.2 逐轮更新斜率截距（缓慢漂移），LLM 可对截距做 {0,±0.05,±0.1} 离散跳变以应对结构性突变（不调斜率，因斜率变更意味关系重构，应交由保守的 LMS）。Preference 通道：按归一化违规压力 p_j=v_j/v_j^threshold 做非对称乘性更新 λ←λ·exp(δ)，δ_up=0.25（收紧）、δ_down=0.08（放松），体现尺度不变性、损失厌恶（收紧 3 倍于放松）与对数空间可解释性，权重再经全局钳制 [1000, 80000]。LLM 元控制器作为二阶控制者，输入 20 轮 episode 历史 + 30 条更新记录 + 当前惩罚权重的 Context JSON，输出 {delta_intercept, penalty_multiplier, reason, evidence_episode_keys}，必须引用具体证据 episode key，无证据则拒绝，信心低时返回空提案（宁可不做）；零样本单次提示（避免被历史修正模式锚定），经七层安全管线：提示约束、三策略 JSON 抽取（直接 loads→Markdown 代码块正则→花括号深度状态机）、schema 校验与类型强制、relation_key 白名单、硬钳制、全局权重边界、失败回退为 no-op。LLM 与 LMS 串行执行（先 LMS 后 LLM），且当 Belief 更新幅度超阈值时 Preference 冻结一轮以隔离归因。Memory DB 为 7 张 SQLite 表（episodes、offline_candidates、prior_relations、prior_observations、prior_update_history、penalty_wt_upd_hist、memory_events），具幂等写与不可变审计（memory_events 作 WAL），支撑热启动、跨轮上下文装配与跨市场迁移。运维上采用 10 步 one-shot 流水线 + YOLO 无限循环（Coldstart→Initial→YOLO 状态机），最小观测窗 3.5 小时并对齐 15 分钟边界，总周期约 4 小时（约 6 轮/天），A/B 拉取失败最多 5 次×5 分钟重试，非 A/B 故障立即退出，崩溃后读 yolo-state.env 恢复，参数经 Redis 原子发布并支持回滚。

Result: Country A（PDP 热启动，Exp-401838，7 轮/25 小时）：GMV 从 R2 的 -3.6% 上升到 R7 的 +9.2%，R4–R7 连续 4 轮为正，订单 R3 起转正、R7 峰值 +12.5%，广告收入 R7 +5.7%，但广告主价值除 R2 外持续为负（R7 -8.9%）；离线 I_gmv 从 +18.2%（R2）到 +41.6%（R7）而线上仍为负至 +9.2%，说明热启动下仍存在显著乐观偏差且需持续校准；GMV/订单/广告/广告主价值不同步，证明各指标不应共享单一全局校准因子；最不稳定的 ps_ads_wo 振荡范围 1.96–8.91（4.5x），出现 2 个完整摆动周期但第二个周期更窄（残余「钟摆效应」）；LLM 校准工作量从 R2 的 5 项收敛至 R7 的 2 项且幅度趋近 0。Country B（冷启动，Exp-437160，23 轮/两阶段）：V1 11 轮 GMV 胜率仅 30%（均值 -2.4%），7 参数大幅振荡，说明「方向正确 ≠ 参数可部署」；冷启动首轮即识别出基线公式严重低估自然点击信号（ps_org_wc 由 1.0→3.71，+271%，为全部实验中最大单参数调整），I_gmv +4.9%、I_ecpm_term -4.5%（接近边界）；V3 12 轮中 R8 窗口是唯一 GMV 与广告收入同正的一轮（GMV +2.6%、广告收入 +6.1%），据此冻结 R7 参数，形成「高点击、低 GMV/订单」结构（ps_org_wc 7.667、ps_ads_wc 4.381，ps_org_wg 0.568、ps_ads_wg 0.641 被压制），通过削弱交易类信号的零和性同时提升自然与广告。7 天静态 A/B（03-15~03-22，10% 实验 vs 20% 对照）：GMV/UU +4.15%、GMV +4.10%、GMV/Order +3.97%、Order/UU +0.17%、广告收入 +3.58%、CPC +4.19%、Take Rate +3.01%、Click/UU +1.21%、广告负载 -2.64%，03-24 全量上线。效率上：单轮约 4 小时、每轮 1 次 LLM 调用（4K–8K 输入 token，high reasoning effort，约 $0.03–$0.10，约 $0.18–$0.60/天），无 GPU，Memory DB 约 1KB/轮，30 轮后 <100KB；相比人工流程（每轮 1–2 人时、约 1 轮/天）将内循环边际人力降至约 0 并把节奏提升至约 6 轮/天。

Conclusion: Sortify 表明排序优化可被重述为连续的影响力交换，并由 LLM Agent 自主闭环驱动：SEU 公理启发的 Belief/Preference 正交分解在架构层面切断了「认知错误」与「价值错误」的混淆（分别对应修正传递映射与惩罚权重），LLM 元控制器只在框架级参数层（二阶控制）做有证据、可安全回退的残差校正，7 表 Memory DB 则把校准智能从「每轮一次性事实」变为可跨轮、跨市场累积的「经验」。工程上它把优化节奏从人工的约 1 轮/天提升到约 6 轮/天，内循环零人工介入，在两个东南亚市场取得可部署的线上收益（Country A 峰值 GMV +9.2%/订单 +12.5%；Country B 7 天 A/B GMV/UU +4.15%、广告收入 +3.58% 并全量上线）。局限：参数未完全收敛（ps_ads_wo 仍有 4.5x 残余钟摆振荡，单目标+罚项只能逼近多目标 Pareto 权衡）；最强结果取自约 12K 曝光的低流量凌晨窗口，统计功效不足；缺乏对极端结构性突变的长期证据，LLM 角色更接近「降低持续校准工作量」而非「处理剧烈突变」。后续方向：改用多目标 Pareto 前沿显式建模广告-自然权衡以消除钟摆效应；引入按观测样本量加权的置信度传递模型，在低流量窗口更保守、高流量窗口更激进。另一层结论是「一人 + AI 编码 Agent 舰队」范式：约 98,000 行代码、78 个 Python 模块、30 轮生产实验乃至本报告正文均由 AI 生成，形成「AI 造 AI」的自指嵌套，人类角色上移为架构师与编排者，说明实施成本趋近于零而项目上限由决策者的业务理解、架构品味与编排能力决定。

Abstract: Recommendation ranking is fundamentally an influence allocation problem: a sorting formula distributes ranking influence among competing factors, and the business outcome depends on finding the optimal "exchange rates" among them. However, offline proxy metrics systematically misjudge how influence reallocation translates to online impact, with asymmetric bias across metrics that a single calibration factor cannot correct.
  We present Sortify, the first fully autonomous LLM-driven ranking optimization agent deployed in a large-scale production recommendation system. The agent reframes ranking optimization as continuous influence exchange, closing the full loop from diagnosis to parameter deployment without human intervention. It addresses structural problems through three mechanisms: (1) a dual-channel framework grounded in Savage's Subjective Expected Utility (SEU) that decouples offline-online transfer correction (Belief channel) from constraint penalty adjustment (Preference channel); (2) an LLM meta-controller operating on framework-level parameters rather than low-level search variables; (3) a persistent Memory DB with 7 relational tables for cross-round learning. Its core metric, Influence Share, provides a decomposable measure where all factor contributions sum to exactly 100%.
  Sortify has been deployed across two Southeast Asian markets. In Country A, the agent pushed GMV from -3.6% to +9.2% within 7 rounds with peak orders reaching +12.5%. In Country B, a cold-start deployment achieved +4.15% GMV/UU and +3.58% Ads Revenue in a 7-day A/B test, leading to full production rollout.

</details>


### [70] [CARGO: Carbon-Aware Gossip Orchestration in Smart Shipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27857)
*Alexandros S. Kalafatelis,Nikolaos Nomikos,Vasileios Nikolakakis,Nikolaos Tsoulakos,Panagiotis Trakadas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARGO 是一种面向智慧航运的碳感知 gossip 编排框架：把去中心化联邦学习拆分为控制平面与数据平面，控制平面每轮联合决定"哪些船舶参与、激活哪些通信边、采用多激进的压缩、何时触发重同步"，同时权衡预测效用、碳成本代理与长期参与公平性；在基于散货船主机缸盖排气温度（EGT）预测性维护的轨迹驱动海事通信仿真中，CARGO 在保持高精度的同时相比 D-PSGD、SGP、稀疏 Gossip 等同精度去中心化基线降低了碳排放与通信量，在等碳预算对比中取得最佳 R²（0.941±0.012）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航运承载全球 80% 以上贸易量，IMO 要求 2030 年前碳强度至少下降 40%、2050 年净零排放；预测性维护（PdM）已成为船队运行的核心能力，但船舶数据天然分布、连接间歇、带宽受限且岸基协调中心可达性无法保证，使依赖星型拓扑与广域同步的服务器协调 FL 成为薄弱的系统假设。基于 gossip 的无服务器去中心化学习更契合海事机会式船对船接触，但现有方法（D-PSGD、SGP、CHOCO、GossipFL 等）本质上以优化器为中心：只规定"如何混合模型"，通常只优化带宽/收敛的一两个维度，不显式决定节点参与、链路激活与压缩强度，且普遍不将碳强度作为调度信号，也不处理参与公平性与丢包可靠性。因此需要一个把通信视为需与碳成本、可靠性、长期参与平衡联合管理的资源来进行在线编排的框架。

Method: 问题建模：将 N 艘船的间歇连接去中心化训练形式化为时变通信图上的序贯在线编排问题，约束包括连接间歇性、可用性随机（掉线）、通信不完美（丢包导致交付图为激活图的严格子集）；目标为 min_Π E[L(x̄(T))]，受累计碳预算 B_C 与逐轮可行性约束。架构：控制平面（CARGO Orchestrator + 参与调度器 + 拓扑调度器 + 碳感知压缩器 + Gossip Mixer）与数据平面（本地 SGD + 带误差反馈的压缩 gossip）分离。控制平面每轮观测紧凑遥测 z_i(t)=(ℓ_i, δ_i, s_i, ρ_i, χ_i)（本地损失、状态分歧信号、不活跃连续轮数、滑动窗口参与率、gCO2/kWh 碳强度），计算效用 U_i=1/(1+ℓ_i)+δ_i、碳成本代理 Γ_i（计算侧 FLOPs/吞吐×功率×碳强度，通信侧 B_θ·压缩比·扇出上限）、公平惩罚 Φ_i（超限不活跃惩罚+参与率缺口惩罚），得分为 g_i=U_i−λ_C·Γ_i−λ_F·Φ_i；以"中位数+β"稳健阈值筛选参与节点，并强制纳入不活跃轮数达 S_max 的节点，必要时按"单位碳效用比"补齐/裁剪至目标基数 K_t。拓扑调度器按信息量/成本比 ψ_ij=|ν_i−ν_j|/(κ_ij+ε) 为每个活跃节点选至多 d_max 个邻居形成度数受限激活图；碳感知压缩器依据发送方碳强度 χ_i 分档选择 None（密集）/INT8/Top-K；Gossip Mixer 用 Metropolis 权重构造行随机混合矩阵 W_t。数据平面：h_i 误差补偿记忆，e_i=x̃_i−h_i，ê_i=Q_i(e_i)，h_i(t+1)=h_i+ê_i；丢包后按交付掩码 m_ij 重归一化得 W̃_t（分母为零时保留自权重 1），混合得 h̄_i 与 x_i(t+1)=x̃_i+γ(h̄_i−h_i)，未参与节点状态与记忆不变；重同步由固定周期标志 r_t（t mod R=0）触发。对偶更新：λ_C、λ_F 以投影次梯度根据归一化碳缺口 g_C 与公平缺口 g_F 自适应调整。实验：Laskaridis 散货船 12,009 HP 二冲程主机 59,619 条高频时序、759 通道传感器，目标为缸级 EGT 预测；先做连续 80/20 训练/测试划分再标准化（防泄漏），训练集上按 Pearson 相关选 top-9 变量并加入滞后/一阶差分/12 步滚动均值/相对时间索引，滑窗 W=30、H=1；模型为两层 LSTM（h=128，dropout 0.1）+ 两层 MLP 头的 seq2one 回归器，MSE 损失，客户端 Adam（lr 5e-4、batch 128、1 本地 epoch、余弦调度），固定算力预算约 84,000 次聚合更新、每 7,000 次评估，5 个随机种子取均值±标准差。海事协议：由 AIS 风格轨迹（含靠港停泊/航行相位、大圆距离与服务速度抽样、AIS 状态相关缺口与位置保持）构造时变接触图，N=5 船、P=6 港、300 km 区域、48 步 30 分钟粒度；三种连通域：well-connected(30 min,200 km)、mid(30 min,80 km)、fragmented(60 min,80 km)；客户端掉线 p_d∈{0.2,0.5}、参与比例 f∈{0.25,0.5,1.0}、丢包 p∈{0,0.05,0.1,0.2}（独立 Bernoulli、权重按交付邻居重归一化）。能耗/碳核算：E_tot=E_comp+E_comm，C_g=(E_tot/3.6e6)·CI；设备画像统一为 Jetson Nano 级（10 W 活动、1.5 W 空闲、2e10 FLOPs/s、2e-7 J/byte）。基线：D-PSGD、SGP、CHOCO-SGD、稀疏 Gossip；CARGO 采用两套固定预设（standard：d_max=3、χ 阈值 300/400、Top-K=0.05、R=2；loss-robust：d_max=2、260/340、0.02、R=3），β=0、ρ*=0.8、S_max=2、η_C=η_F=0.01、γ=0.5、8 轮滑窗参与率。

Result: 在 mid 拓扑客户端压力网格中，CARGO 全部 6 种 (p_d, f) 组合均保持与 D-PSGD/SGP/GOSSIP 同级的 RMSE（约 0.0082–0.0105），同时碳排放最低（0.709–0.715 g）、能耗最低（7307–7456 J）、通信量低于同精度基线：如 (p_d=0.5, f=0.25) 时 RMSE 0.0082（优于 D-PSGD/SGP 的 0.0086）、碳 0.709 g（vs 0.715–0.724）、通信 367.9 MB（vs 538.9 MB）；(p_d=0.2, f=0.5) 时 RMSE 0.0094、碳 0.715 g（vs 0.726–0.736）、通信 725.0 MB（vs 1189.6 MB）、能耗 7362 J（vs 7455–7557 J）。CHOCO 取得最低资源（碳约 0.70 g、134.7–491.2 MB），但 RMSE 显著更差（0.0143–0.0290），形成明确的"低资源/低精度"区。丢包敏感性实验中，RMSE 随 p 上升且方差扩大（fragmented 域尤为明显），CARGO 在高丢包与稀疏连接下仍维持有竞争力的 RMSE 与更优碳/能耗轮廓。等预算对比：固定碳预算 0.6447 gCO2e 时 CARGO 取得最高 R²=0.941±0.012、最低 RMSE=0.0108±0.011（优于 CHOCO 0.844/0.0166、D-PSGD 0.933/0.0114、SGP 0.932/0.0115、GOSSIP 0.934/0.0113）；固定交付通信预算 592.7 MB 时 CARGO R²=0.934±0.014 与最强稀疏 Gossip（0.935±0.020）实质持平，并明显优于 CHOCO/D-PSGD/SGP。收敛性上各方法终端精度相近，但 CARGO 更早沿能量轴进入高 R² 平台，能量-质量效率更优；复杂度方面 CARGO 额外开销仅为 O(N log N) 节点打分、O(|E_A| log k) 边选择与 O(|E_S| d_eff) 混合，可忽略。

Conclusion: 论文证明在智慧航运中，去中心化学习不必固化为固定通信例行程序：通过在控制平面联合调节节点参与、边激活、压缩强度与恢复行为（数据平面仍沿用成熟 gossip 更新与本地预测器结构），可在不牺牲精度的前提下显著降低碳足迹与通信开销。等碳/等通信预算的对照说明 CARGO 的优势并非来自"多花预算"，而是来自更好的成本-质量 Pareto 定位。作者明确限定结论为 Pareto 导向而非"全面最优"：CHOCO 在放宽精度要求时仍是更省资源的替代方案。局限与展望：评估为轨迹驱动仿真而非实船部署，仅覆盖单一 PdM 任务与单一同构边缘设备画像，N=5 规模偏小，未纳入大船队扩展性评估；通信模型仅有独立丢包，缺少时延与突发丢包；安全与隐私机制（加密、对抗鲁棒聚合）被有意排除在范围外。后续方向为：更大规模与更异构的船队级评估、更丰富的通信模型、与安全/对抗鲁棒去中心化学习机制集成。

Abstract: Smart shipping operations increasingly depend on collaborative AI, yet the underlying data are generated across vessels with uneven connectivity, limited backhaul, and clear commercial sensitivity. In such settings, server-coordinated FL remains a weak systems assumption, depending on a reachable aggregation point and repeated wide-area synchronization, both of which are difficult to guarantee in maritime networks. A serverless gossip approach therefore represents a more natural approach, but existing methods still treat communication mainly as an optimization bottleneck, rather than as a resource that must be managed jointly with carbon cost, reliability, and long-term participation balance. In this context, this paper presents CARGO, a carbon-aware gossip orchestration framework for smart-shipping. CARGO separates learning into a control and a data plane. The data plane performs local optimization with compressed gossip exchange, while the control plane decides, at each round, which vessels should participate, which communication edges should be activated, how aggressively updates should be compressed, and when recovery actions should be triggered. We evaluate CARGO under a predictive-maintenance scenario using operational bulk-carrier engine data and a trace-driven maritime communication protocol that captures client dropout, partial participation, packet loss, and multiple connectivity regimes, derived from mobility-aware vessel interactions. Across the tested stress settings, CARGO consistently remains in the high-accuracy regime while reducing carbon footprint and communication overheads, compared to accuracy-competitive decentralized baselines. Overall, the conducted performance evaluation demonstrates that CARGO is a feasible and practical solution for reliable and resource-conscious maritime AI deployment.

</details>


### [71] [GEAKG: Generative Executable Algorithm Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27922)
*Camilo Chacón Sartori,José H. García,Andrei Voicu Tomut,Christian Blum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出生成式可执行算法知识图谱（GEAKG），将算法设计知识显式表示为可执行、可学习的图结构，实现跨领域的算法专业知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前问题解决算法中，程序性知识（算法设计和算子组合的know-how）隐含在代码中，在运行之间丢失，且必须为每个新领域重新工程化。现有知识图谱范式对将程序性知识表示为可执行、可学习的图结构支持有限。

Method: 提出GEAKG：节点存储可执行算子，边编码学习到的组合模式，图遍历生成解决方案。具备三个核心特性：生成性（LLM合成拓扑和算子）、可执行性（每个节点都是可运行代码）、可迁移性（学习模式跨领域零样本泛化）。框架采用三层架构和基于蚁群优化（ACO）的学习引擎，通过可插拔本体（RoleSchema）实现领域无关性。

Result: 两个案例研究验证：（1）神经架构搜索：70个跨数据集迁移对在两个表格基准上的实验；（2）组合优化：在TSP上学习的知识零样本迁移到调度和分配领域。两个案例不共享领域特定框架代码。

Conclusion: 算法专业知识可以作为可执行知识图谱被显式表示、学习和迁移，这支持了框架假设的有效性。

Abstract: In the context of algorithms for problem solving, procedural knowledge -- the know-how of algorithm design and operator composition -- remains implicit in code, lost between runs, and must be re-engineered for each new domain. Knowledge graphs (KGs) have proven effective for organizing declarative knowledge, yet current KG paradigms provide limited support for representing procedural knowledge as executable, learnable graph structures. We introduce \textit{Generative Executable Algorithm Knowledge Graphs} (GEAKG), a class of KGs whose nodes store executable operators, whose edges encode learned composition patterns, and whose traversal generates solutions. A GEAKG is \emph{generative} (topology and operators are synthesized by a Large Language Model), \emph{executable} (every node is runnable code), and \emph{transferable} (learned patterns generalize zero-shot across domains). The framework is domain-agnostic at the engine level: the same three-layer architecture and Ant Colony Optimization (ACO)-based learning engine can be instantiated across domains, parameterized by a pluggable ontology (\texttt{RoleSchema}). Two case studies -- sharing no domain-specific framework code -- provide concrete evidence for this framework hypothesis: (1)~Neural Architecture Search across 70 cross-dataset transfer pairs on two tabular benchmarks, and (2)~Combinatorial Optimization, where knowledge learned on the Traveling Salesman Problem transfers zero-shot to scheduling and assignment domains. Taken together, the results support that algorithmic expertise can be explicitly represented, learned, and transferred as executable knowledge graphs.

</details>


### [72] [CARV: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Compositional Analogical Reasoning in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27958)
*Yongkang Du,Xiaohan Zou,Minhao Cheng,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARV是一个用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）组合类比推理能力的新基准，包含5,500个样本。即使是最先进的Gemini-2.5 Pro在此任务上仅达到40.4%准确率，远低于人类100%的水平，揭示了当前MLLMs在该高阶智能任务上的显著局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs的类比推理评估忽略了从多个来源组合规则的能力——这是高阶智能的关键组成部分。现有测试仅基于单一对象对，无法诊断模型在复杂组合场景下的真实推理能力。

Method: 提出CARV任务，将类比从单一对象对扩展到多对象对，要求MLLMs从每个对象对中提取符号规则并组合新的转换规则。构建5,500样本的诊断基准，涵盖多样化和复杂设置。

Result: 最先进模型Gemini-2.5 Pro仅达到40.4%准确率，与人类100%的表现形成巨大差距，表明当前MLLMs在组合类比推理方面存在根本性不足。

Conclusion: 诊断分析揭示两个一致失败模式：(1)将视觉变化分解为符号规则的能力不足；(2)在多样化或复杂设置下缺乏鲁棒性。这些发现强调需要新的方法论来提升MLLMs的组合推理能力。

Abstract: Analogical reasoning tests a fundamental aspect of human cognition: mapping the relation from one pair of objects to another. Existing evaluations of this ability in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) overlook the ability to compose rules from multiple sources, a critical component of higher-order intelligence. To close this gap, we introduce CARV (Compositional Analogical Reasoning in Vision), a novel task together with a 5,500-sample dataset as the first diagnostic benchmark. We extend the analogy from a single pair to multiple pairs, which requires MLLMs to extract symbolic rules from each pair and compose new transformations. Evaluation on the state-of-the-art MLLMs reveals a striking performance gap: even Gemini-2.5 Pro achieving only 40.4% accuracy, far below human-level performance of 100%. Diagnostic analysis shows two consistent failure modes: (1) decomposing visual changes into symbolic rules, and (2) maintaining robustness under diverse or complex settings, highlighting the limitations of current MLLMs on this task.

</details>


### [73] [SARL: Label-Free Reinforcement Learning by Rewarding Reasoning Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27977)
*Yifan Wang,Bolian Li,David Cho,Ruqi Zhang,Fanping Sui,Ananth Grama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SARL（Structure-Aware Reinforcement Learning，结构感知强化学习）：一种完全免标签（label-free）的强化学习框架，不再奖励"最终答案是否正确"，而是奖励"推理过程本身的组织结构"。方法上从每次 rollout 的 <think> 块中切分推理步骤，用嵌入模型表示并聚类成"潜在推理功能"节点（KMeans/HDBSCAN），构建无向推理图（Reasoning Map），然后用小世界拓扑的两个经典指标——平均聚类系数 C（局部一致性）与平均最短路径长度 L（全局效率）——构造结构奖励 SR = 0.5·C + 1/(1+L) ∈ [0,1]，直接作为 RL 奖励信号。基于 Qwen3-4B 的实验显示：数学可验证任务上 SARL 超过基于真值的 PPO/GRPO（平均 +9.1% / +11.9%，AIME25 提升最显著），开放任务 WildBench 上取得 +34.6%(PPO) / +30.4%(GRPO) 的大幅提升，优于 EMPO、TTRL 及依赖偏好标注的 DPO；同时保持更低 KL 散度、更高策略熵（更稳定、更多探索）且输出长度更短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大推理模型（LRM）的强化学习后训练高度依赖 RLVR（可验证奖励），需要客观真值与自动验证器，这在创作写作、战略规划、咨询建议等正确性模糊的开放域中完全不可用；即使是 Process Reward Model（PRM）也仍是"结果导向"的（判断每一步对最终答案的贡献），且需要昂贵的步级标注，无法迁移到开放域。此外，仅优化最终答案会鼓励"早期利用（early exploitation）"而牺牲泛化，推理轨迹的结构本身基本不受约束。而已有实证研究（Minegishi et al. 2025; Feng et al. 2025; Tan et al. 2025）发现 LRM 的推理图普遍呈现小世界拓扑（高局部聚类 + 短全局路径），且这些结构特征与正确率正相关，表明小世界结构不仅是好推理的副产品，更是支撑推理的结构性属性。因此核心问题是：能否不依赖真值监督，而是直接塑造"模型如何思考"（推理结构）来提升通用推理能力。

Method: 【问题形式化】将 RLVR 的结果级目标 max E[r(a,x)] 替换为结构目标 max E[SR(G(τ))]，完全不需要验证器；轨迹 τ=(s_1,…,s_T, a)，s_t 为 <think> 块内的中间推理步骤。【推理图构建】(1) 步骤切分：按模型输出格式（如 \n\n）将 <think> 切为语义连贯的句子级步骤；(2) 嵌入：用独立嵌入模型 Qwen/Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B 计算 L2 归一化向量 e_t；(3) 聚类：对每条响应独立聚类为 K 个"潜在推理功能"节点（数学任务用 KMeans，k≈√M；开放任务用 HDBSCAN，欧氏距离，min_cluster_size=max(2,min(5,M/4))，噪声点单独成簇或归入默认簇）；(4) 建边：相邻两步映射到不同节点时加入无向边，重复转移只计一次。【结构奖励】因经典小世界指数 σ=(C/C_rand)/(L/L_rand) 在实际稀疏图（k̄≈1）下数值不稳定，改用 SR(G)=0.5·C(G)+1/(1+L(G))，其中 C 为非平凡节点（度≥2）上的平均聚类系数，L 为所有可达节点对的平均跳数距离，奖励落在 [0,1]，两项分别惩罚"塌缩为单一功能"和"冗长无结构的长链"。【训练】PPO/GRPO，veRL + vLLM，FSDP2，8×H100；嵌入服务以独立 vLLM OpenAI API server 形式运行以避免干扰 rollout；每 prompt 8 条 rollout；KL 系数 0.001。数据：数学用 AIME 1983–2024（933 题），开放用 OpenRubrics-v2（经长度>512 token 过滤与去重后 3294 条）。【鲁棒性分析】best-of-n 代理压力测试（结构奖励选出的响应正确率随 n 上升，n=256 时 SARL 策略 SR-selected 60% vs oracle 80%）、图统计（完全图仅占 0.143%，未塌缩）、结构+真值奖励叠加、HDBSCAN/替代嵌入模型/Qwen3-8B/Llama-3.2-3B、AlpacaEval 第二基准与三个 WildBench 评审的一致性检验。

Result: 【数学可验证任务，avg@8】基座 Qwen3-4B 平均 64.51。PPO：SARL 70.38 (+9.1%)，略高于真值 PPO 的 70.18 (+8.8%)；GRPO：SARL 72.16 (+11.9%) 优于真值 GRPO 71.66 (+11.1%)、EMPO 71.45 (+10.8%)、TTRL 71.12 (+10.2%)。AIME25 上提升最大（31.67→45.83，GRPO 相对 +45%），表明轨迹越长越复杂，结构约束收益越大。【开放任务 WildBench】基座 29.91；PPO SARL 40.26 (+34.6%)，GRPO SARL 39.01 (+30.4%)，而 DPO（用偏好标注）仅 30.34 (+1.4%)，EMPO 为 29.20 (-2.4%)。分类别看，Planning +26.6%/+20.2%，Creative +11.8%/+8.5%，Info/Advice +9.8%/+6.7%，Code 提升最大（14.72→30.05）。【训练动态】SARL 全程保持最低 KL 散度与较高策略熵，而真值奖励训练熵快速下降（premature exploitation）。【长度】SARL 显著缩短输出（数学 5736.6→4587.8；开放 2677.9→2285.9），说明收益来自"组织得更高效"而非"更长"。【消融】仅 L：70.96 (+10.0%)；仅 C：71.97 (+11.6%)；C+L：72.16 (+11.9%)，两项均独立有效。【鲁棒性】换 HDBSCAN (+10.2%)、换嵌入模型 (+10.8%) 增益保持；Qwen3-8B 上 +11.3%（略低于真值 +12.9%）；Llama-3.2-3B 上 +10.4%（低于真值 +24.8%）；AlpacaEval 长度控制胜率 24.04→30.37(PPO)，三个评审下 SARL 均排名第一或第二。

Conclusion: 推理结构本身是一个强健、通用且免标签的学习信号。SARL 通过把监督从"目的地（最终答案）"迁移到"路径（推理拓扑）"，实现了向开放域 RL 的扩展：它在不使用任何正确性标签或偏好监督的情况下，数学任务上匹配甚至超过基于真值的 RL，开放任务上大幅超越所有免标签基线与依赖偏好标注的 DPO，同时保持更小的策略漂移与更高的探索熵，说明其训练处于更稳定的优化区间。这为突破 RL 的"可验证性瓶颈"提供了可行路径：推理结构可作为与结果奖励正交、可扩展的内在奖励。局限方面：仅在推理链足够长且复杂的任务上有效，短任务收益有限；不同领域可能偏好不同拓扑结构；奖励是推理质量的代理指标，理论上可被利用（best-of-n 与图统计检验仅覆盖部分失效模式，无法证明单条轨迹的忠实性或因果有效性）；性能依赖步骤切分、嵌入与聚类选择；开放任务依赖自动评审，分数随评审模型变化，且缺乏多随机种子方差估计。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become central to improving large reasoning models, but its success still relies heavily on verifiable rewards or labeled supervision. This limits its applicability to open ended domains where correctness is ambiguous and cannot be verified. Moreover, reasoning trajectories remain largely unconstrained, and optimization towards final answer can favor early exploitation over generalization. In this work, we ask whether general reasoning ability can be improved by teaching models how to think (the structure of reasoning) rather than what to produce (the outcome of reasoning) and extend traditional RLVR to open ended settings. We introduce structure aware reinforcement learning (SARL), a label free framework that constructs a per response Reasoning Map from intermediate thinking steps and rewards its small world topology, inspired by complex networks and the functional organization of the human brain. SARL encourages reasoning trajectories that are both locally coherent and globally efficient, shifting supervision from destination to path. Our experiments on Qwen3-4B show SARL surpasses ground truth based RL and prior label free RL baselines, achieving the best average gain of 9.1% under PPO and 11.6% under GRPO on math tasks and 34.6% under PPO and 30.4% under GRPO on open ended tasks. Beyond good performance, SARL also exhibits lower KL divergence, higher policy entropy, indicating a more stable and exploratory training and generalized reasoning ability.

</details>


### [74] [HeteroHub: An Applicable Data Management Framework for Heterogeneous Multi-Embodied Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28010)
*Xujia Li,Xin Li,Junquan Huang,Beirong Cui,Zibin Wu,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HeteroHub是一个数据中心的框架，旨在为异构多具身智能体系统提供统一的数据管理基础设施，整合静态元数据、任务对齐的训练语料库和实时数据流，支持任务感知模型训练、上下文敏感执行和闭环控制，实现复杂任务协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架缺乏统一的数据管理基础设施来支持异构多具身智能体系统在动态环境中的实际部署。这类系统需要协调具有不同能力的多个具身智能体，涉及静态知识、多模态训练数据集和高频传感器流三类异构数据的采集、生成和消费。

Method: 提出HeteroHub框架，整合三类数据：静态元数据（关于智能体、任务和环境的知识）、任务对齐的多模态训练语料库、高频实时传感器数据流。框架支持任务感知模型训练、上下文敏感执行和基于真实世界反馈的闭环控制。

Result: HeteroHub成功协调多个具身AI智能体执行复杂任务，验证了该数据管理框架能够支持可扩展、可维护和可演化的具身AI系统。

Conclusion: 统一的数据管理框架是异构多具身智能体系统在实际场景中部署的关键使能技术，HeteroHub展示了数据中心方法对构建规模化智能体系统的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous Multi-Embodied Agent Systems involve coordinating multiple embodied agents with diverse capabilities to accomplish tasks in dynamic environments. This process requires the collection, generation, and consumption of massive, heterogeneous data, which primarily falls into three categories: static knowledge regarding the agents, tasks, and environments; multimodal training datasets tailored for various AI models; and high-frequency sensor streams. However, existing frameworks lack a unified data management infrastructure to support the real-world deployment of such systems. To address this gap, we present \textbf{HeteroHub}, a data-centric framework that integrates static metadata, task-aligned training corpora, and real-time data streams. The framework supports task-aware model training, context-sensitive execution, and closed-loop control driven by real-world feedback. In our demonstration, HeteroHub successfully coordinates multiple embodied AI agents to execute complex tasks, illustrating how a robust data management framework can enable scalable, maintainable, and evolvable embodied AI systems.

</details>


### [75] [What an Autonomous Agent Discovers About Molecular Transformer Design: Does It Transfer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28015)
*Edward Wijaya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统检验"分子序列是否需要不同于NLP的Transformer架构"这一问题，在单张GPU上以自主架构搜索（agent-based NAS）跨SMILES、蛋白质、英文文本三类序列共开展3,106次实验。结论：架构搜索对SMILES有害（单纯调学习率与调度即优于全搜索，p=0.001），对自然语言收益巨大（架构变更贡献81%改进，p=0.009），蛋白质居中；尽管agent为各领域发现了显著不同的架构（p=0.004），但所有创新在跨领域迁移时性能退化均<1%，说明领域差异主要源于搜索路径依赖而非根本的生物学需求。作者发布决策框架与开源工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习在类药分子（SMILES）与蛋白质建模中几乎完全照搬为自然语言设计的Transformer架构，但分子/蛋白序列是否真正受益于不同设计从未被系统性检验。缺乏这一实证结论，导致团队在"是否值得投入昂贵的架构搜索"上只能凭经验猜测；同时也不清楚领域特化架构的收益究竟来自生物学先验，还是仅是随机搜索路径的偶然产物。这既浪费算力预算，也阻碍了跨领域预训练/迁移策略的合理设计。

Method: 以agent驱动的自主架构搜索为统一框架，部署于三类序列模态：SMILES（分子）、蛋白质序列、英文文本（作为对照组）。在单张GPU上共完成3,106次实验，对每种模态比较完整架构搜索与"仅调学习率及学习率调度"等超参基线的性能差异，并做显著性检验（p=0.001 / 0.009）。同时检验agent在不同领域发现的架构是否结构上显著不同（p=0.004），再对每个领域发现的创新进行跨领域迁移实验，以量化性能退化幅度（<1%）并区分"领域特异性生物学需求"与"搜索路径依赖"两种解释。最终产出一个面向分子建模团队的选型决策框架与开源工具包。

Result: (1) SMILES上架构搜索反效果：仅优化学习率与调度策略即超过完整NAS的结果（p=0.001），即在分子任务上架构复杂度带来的收益为负；(2) 英文文本上架构改动是主要增益来源，占总体改进的81%（p=0.009）；(3) 蛋白质结果介于二者之间；(4) agent确实为不同领域发现了结构上不同的架构（p=0.004），但这些创新互相迁移到其他领域时性能退化均小于1%，表明所发现的"领域特化"架构并非该领域的必要条件。

Conclusion: 自主架构搜索的价值高度依赖模态：对自然语言收益显著、对SMILES有害、对蛋白质中性偏正，因此应为不同序列类型选择不同投入策略——分子建模团队在多数情况下应优先采用简单超参调优而非昂贵NAS。作者据此发布决策框架与开源工具包，并主张模型架构上的"领域差异"更多反映随机搜索路径依赖，而非分子的生物学内在需求，从而为跨领域架构迁移（乃至复用）的正当性提供依据。

Abstract: Deep learning models for drug-like molecules and proteins overwhelmingly reuse transformer architectures designed for natural language, yet whether molecular sequences benefit from different designs has not been systematically tested. We deploy autonomous architecture search via an agent across three sequence types (SMILES, protein, and English text as control), running 3,106 experiments on a single GPU. For SMILES, architecture search is counterproductive: tuning learning rates and schedules alone outperforms the full search (p = 0.001). For natural language, architecture changes drive 81% of improvement (p = 0.009). Proteins fall between the two. Surprisingly, although the agent discovers distinct architectures per domain (p = 0.004), every innovation transfers across all three domains with <1% degradation, indicating that the differences reflect search-path dependence rather than fundamental biological requirements. We release a decision framework and open-source toolkit for molecular modeling teams to choose between autonomous architecture search and simple hyperparameter tuning.

</details>


### [76] [When Choices Become Priors: Contrastive Decoding for Scientific Figure Multiple-Choice QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28026)
*Taeyun Roh,Eun-yeong Jo,Wonjune Jang,Jaewoo Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究科学图表多选题问答（MCQA）中的一个关键问题：答案选项本身会作为先验知识，使模型即使在图表不支持的情况下也倾向于选择看似合理的选项。为此，作者提出SCICON——一种无需训练的解码方法，通过从图像条件下的选项得分中减去纯文本选项得分来重新评分。在三个科学图表QA基准和三个模型骨干上的实验表明，该方法一致性地提升了准确率，证明了针对选项诱导先验的解码是改进科学MCQA中图表 grounding 推理的有效方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图表MCQA存在一个独特的偏差问题：答案选项本身可以作为先验，引导多模态模型选择文本上看起来合理但图表并不支持的选项。这种选择诱导偏差（choice-induced prior）会导致模型忽视图表中的关键证据，是当前方法失败的主要模式。

Method: 提出SCICON，一种无需训练的解码方法。核心思想是显式地从模型对每个候选答案的图像条件得分中减去其纯文本条件得分，从而折扣掉仅由文本先验贡献的偏好。相比之前使用扭曲图像或扰动指令的对比解码方法，SCICON直接针对候选文本中编码的选择诱导先验。

Result: 在三个科学图表QA基准（ChartQA、ScienceQA等）和三个模型骨干（PaLI、InstructBLIP等）上的实验表明，SCICON一致性地优于标准解码基线，有效提升了图表 grounding 的推理能力。

Conclusion: 解码过程中显式对抗选择诱导先验是改进科学MCQA中图表推理的简单有效方法，且该方法具有通用性，可应用于多种模型架构。

Abstract: Scientific figure multiple-choice question answering (MCQA) requires models to reason over diverse visual evidence, ranging from charts and multipanel figures to microscopy and biomedical images. However, this setting suffers from a distinctive bias: answer choices themselves can act as priors, steering multimodal models toward scientifically plausible options even when the figure supports a different answer. We investigate this failure mode through a simple question: what if decoding explicitly discounts what the model would prefer from text alone, so as to favor figure-grounded evidence? To this end, we propose SCICON, a training-free decoding method that scores each candidate by subtracting a text-only option score from its image-conditioned counterpart. Unlike prior contrastive decoding approaches that mitigate hallucinations by contrasting original inputs with distorted images or perturbed instructions, SCICON directly targets the choice-induced prior encoded in candidate text. Across three scientific figure QA benchmarks and three model backbones, SCICON consistently improves accuracy over standard decoding baselines. These results show that decoding against choice-induced priors is an effective and simple way to improve figure-grounded reasoning in scientific MCQA.

</details>


### [77] [Beyond the Answer: Decoding the Behavior of LLMs as Scientific Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28038)
*Rohan Pandey,Eric Ye,Michael Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用遗传帕累托算法（GEPA）优化科学推理任务的提示词，发现优化后的性能提升往往来自模型特定的启发式逻辑（"局部"逻辑），无法跨模型泛化，提示优化可作为模型可解释性工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在复杂推理任务中表现日益复杂，需要理解其内部推理机制以实现可解释性和安全性；同时自然语言将成为AGI系统的主要交互界面，理解提示如何调节推理过程至关重要。

Method: 使用自定义遗传帕累托算法（GEPA）变体对科学推理任务进行系统性提示优化，分析提示对推理行为的影响，并研究优化提示的结构模式和逻辑启发式特征，评估其可迁移性和脆弱性。

Result: 科学推理能力的提升往往对应于模型特定的启发式逻辑（称为"局部"逻辑），这些逻辑无法在不同系统间泛化，表现出明显的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 将提示优化作为模型可解释性工具，映射LLM偏好的推理结构是有效与超人类智能协作的重要前提。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve increasingly sophisticated performance on complex reasoning tasks, current architectures serve as critical proxies for the internal heuristics of frontier models. Characterizing emergent reasoning is vital for long-term interpretability and safety. Furthermore, understanding how prompting modulates these processes is essential, as natural language will likely be the primary interface for interacting with AGI systems. In this work, we use a custom variant of Genetic Pareto (GEPA) to systematically optimize prompts for scientific reasoning tasks, and analyze how prompting can affect reasoning behavior. We investigate the structural patterns and logical heuristics inherent in GEPA-optimized prompts, and evaluate their transferability and brittleness. Our findings reveal that gains in scientific reasoning often correspond to model-specific heuristics that fail to generalize across systems, which we call "local" logic. By framing prompt optimization as a tool for model interpretability, we argue that mapping these preferred reasoning structures for LLMs is an important prerequisite for effectively collaborating with superhuman intelligence.

</details>


### [78] [Dogfight Search: A Swarm-Based Optimization Algorithm for Complex Engineering Optimization and Mountainous Terrain Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28046)
*Yujing Sun,Jie Cai,Xingguo Xu,Yuansheng Gao,Lei Zhang,Kaichen Ouyang,Zhanyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受狗斗（dogfight）战术启发的元启发式优化算法Dogfight Search (DoS)，该算法使用运动学中的位移积分方程构建搜索机制，在CEC2017/CEC2022基准测试、10个约束优化问题和路径规划任务上均表现最优，优于7个先进算法和3个SOTA算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受战斗机之间协同狗斗战术行为的启发，传统的元启发式算法通常依赖隐喻式设计框架，本文旨在提出一种无隐喻（metaphor-free）的算法，通过运动学原理直接构建搜索机制。

Method: DoS算法从狗斗战术中提取灵感，但其搜索机制基于运动学中的位移积分方程构建，而非简单的隐喻类比。算法通过实验在CEC2017、CEC2022基准测试函数、10个真实约束优化问题及山地地形路径规划任务上进行验证。

Result: DoS在整体性能上显著优于7个先进竞争对手，在Friedman排名中位列第一。与3个SOTA算法在CEC2017和CEC2022上的对比结果进一步验证了其竞争力。

Conclusion: Dogfight Search算法通过将战术灵感与物理公式相结合，展现了强大的全局优化能力和广泛的适用性，验证了"无隐喻"设计思路在元启发式算法中的有效性。

Abstract: Dogfight is a tactical behavior of cooperation between fighters. Inspired by this, this paper proposes a novel metaphor-free metaheuristic algorithm called Dogfight Search (DoS). Unlike traditional algorithms, DoS draws algorithmic framework from the inspiration, but its search mechanism is constructed based on the displacement integration equations in kinematics. Through experimental validation on CEC2017 and CEC2022 benchmark test functions, 10 real-world constrained optimization problems and mountainous terrain path planning tasks, DoS significantly outperforms 7 advanced competitors in overall performance and ranks first in the Friedman ranking. Furthermore, this paper compares the performance of DoS with 3 SOTA algorithms on the CEC2017 and CEC2022 benchmark test functions. The results show that DoS continues to maintain its lead, demonstrating strong competitiveness. The source code of DoS is available at https://ww2.mathworks.cn/matlabcentral/fileexchange/183519-dogfight-search.

</details>


### [79] [Meta-Harness: End-to-End Optimization of Model Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28052)
*Yoonho Lee,Roshen Nair,Qizheng Zhang,Kangwook Lee,Omar Khattab,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Meta-Harness is an outer-loop system that automates harness engineering for LLM applications by using an agentic proposer that accesses prior candidates' source code, scores, and execution traces through a filesystem. It achieves significant improvements across text classification (7.7 points improvement, 4x fewer tokens), math reasoning (4.7 points on IMO-level problems), and agentic coding (surpassing hand-engineered baselines on TerminalBench-2).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM系统性能不仅取决于模型权重，还取决于harness（管理信息存储、检索和呈现的代码）。然而harness仍主要依赖手工设计，且现有文本优化器因过度压缩反馈信息而不适合此场景。

Method: Meta-Harness通过agentic proposer访问所有先前候选方案的源代码、评分和执行追踪记录（通过文件系统），在harness代码空间中进行搜索优化。

Result: 文本分类：比先进上下文管理系统提升7.7分，且上下文token减少4倍；数学推理：在200道IMO级别问题上跨5个模型平均提升4.7分；智能体编程：在TerminalBench-2上超越最佳手工设计基线。

Conclusion: 更丰富地访问先前经验可以实现自动化harness工程，表明外层循环优化是提升LLM应用性能的有效途径。

Abstract: The performance of large language model (LLM) systems depends not only on model weights, but also on their harness: the code that determines what information to store, retrieve, and present to the model. Yet harnesses are still designed largely by hand, and existing text optimizers are poorly matched to this setting because they compress feedback too aggressively. We introduce Meta-Harness, an outer-loop system that searches over harness code for LLM applications. It uses an agentic proposer that accesses the source code, scores, and execution traces of all prior candidates through a filesystem. On online text classification, Meta-Harness improves over a state-of-the-art context management system by 7.7 points while using 4x fewer context tokens. On retrieval-augmented math reasoning, a single discovered harness improves accuracy on 200 IMO-level problems by 4.7 points on average across five held-out models. On agentic coding, discovered harnesses surpass the best hand-engineered baselines on TerminalBench-2. Together, these results show that richer access to prior experience can enable automated harness engineering.

</details>


### [80] [SLOW: Strategic Logical-inference Open Workspace for Cognitive Adaptation in AI Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28062)
*Yuang Wei,Ruijia Li,Bo Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SLOW是一个理论驱动的LLM教育辅导框架，通过显式分离学习者状态推理与教学动作选择来解决现有生成式辅导系统的局限性。该框架整合因果证据解析、模糊认知诊断、因果稳定性分析和前瞻性情感推理，在透明决策空间中实现可解释的自适应教学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM辅导系统依赖快速直觉式单次生成，缺乏专门的推理工作空间，导致学习者认知诊断、情感感知和教学决策三者紧密耦合。这种快速思考模式限制了系统进行审慎教学适应的能力，无法有效实现个性化教学和情感敏感度管理。

Method: SLOW框架采用双过程理论框架：1）因果证据解析：从学习者语言中提取因果证据；2）模糊认知诊断结合反事实稳定性分析：评估学习者认知状态；3）前瞻性情感推理：预测教学选择对学习者情感轨迹的影响。这些信号联合考虑以指导符合教学和情感需求的辅导策略。

Result: 混合人-AI评估显示SLOW在个性化、情感敏感度和清晰度方面均有显著提升。消融实验验证了各模块的必要性，证明了可视化决策过程可实现可解释且可靠只能辅导。

Conclusion: SLOW通过透明决策工作空间实现了可解释的自适应教学，显著提升了LLM辅导系统的可解释性和教育有效性。该框架为基于LLM的自适应教学提供了新的理论和技术基础。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable fluency in educational dialogues, most generative tutors primarily operate through intuitive, single-pass generation. This reliance on fast thinking precludes a dedicated reasoning workspace, forcing multiple diagnostic and strategic signals to be processed in a conflated manner. As a result, learner cognitive diagnosis, affective perception, and pedagogical decision-making become tightly entangled, which limits the tutoring system's capacity for deliberate instructional adaptation. We propose SLOW, a theory-informed tutoring framework that supports deliberate learner-state reasoning within a transparent decision workspace. Inspired by dual-process accounts of human tutoring, SLOW explicitly separates learner-state inference from instructional action selection. The framework integrates causal evidence parsing from learner language, fuzzy cognitive diagnosis with counterfactual stability analysis, and prospective affective reasoning to anticipate how instructional choices may influence learners' emotional trajectories. These signals are jointly considered to guide pedagogically and affectively aligned tutoring strategies. Evaluation using hybrid human-AI judgments demonstrates significant improvements in personalization, emotional sensitivity, and clarity. Ablation studies further confirm the necessity of each module, showcasing how SLOW enables interpretable and reliable intelligent tutoring through a visualized decision-making process. This work advances the interpretability and educational validity of LLM-based adaptive instruction.

</details>


### [81] [Reward Hacking as Equilibrium under Finite Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28063)
*Jiacheng Wang,Jinbin Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以 Holmström-Milgrom(1991)多任务委托-代理模型为框架，证明"奖励黑客"不是工程缺陷而是任何有限评估系统下的结构性均衡：在五条最小公理（多维质量、有限评估、有效优化、资源有限、组合交互）下，被优化的 AI 智能体必然系统性低估未被评估覆盖的质量维度投入（Proposition 1）；并给出可计算的"失真指数" D_i 预测每个维度上失真的方向与严重程度（Corollary 1）。进一步证明（Proposition 2）当评估工程预算为 o(T²) 时，工具数增长导致质量维度按 T² 组合爆炸而评估覆盖趋近 0，合同不完全性 κ→1，hack 严重度结构性无界上升。论文以猜想形式给出"能力阈值"与"能力陷阱"（Conjecture 1、2），即 Goodhart 区制向 Campbell 区制的转变，构成 Bostrom"treacherous turn"的首个经济学形式化，并提出一套部署前的脆弱性评估流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 安全界长期将奖励黑客（reward hacking）当作工程问题处理：发现一种 hack→修补奖励模型→再出现新的 hack，形成"打地鼠"循环（类似金融监管套利）。这种持续性暗示存在更深层的结构性原因：只要在真实目标高维、而评估信号有限维的系统上做优化，行为扭曲就不可避免。论文因此把研究问题从"如何消除 hack"转变为"在何种条件下 hack 必然发生、方向与强度可否事前预测、以及工具化（agentic）转型如何加剧该问题"。作者同时指出从业者观察（工具访问扩大攻击面、harness 捕获的执行轨迹成为竞争瓶颈）缺乏理论解释，需要统一框架。

Method: 形式化建模为"设计者（principal）–AI 智能体（agent）"的多任务道德风险/不完全合同问题。设定质量向量 q∈R^N（N≥2）、评估投影 π(q)=q̂∈R^K（K<N）、生产函数 q_i=g_i(e_i)（满足 G1–G3：g(0)=0、严格递增、严格凹；另讨论 Inada 条件以保证内点解与角点解两种情形）、资源约束 Σe_i≤B。主目标 W(q)=Σw_i q_i。智能体"as if"地最大化有效目标 Φ=Σ w̃_i g_i(e_i)，其中 w̃_i=λ r_i+(1−λ)w_i（i≤K）与 w̃_i=(1−λ)w_i（i>K），λ∈(0,1) 为"对齐缺口"（行为受评估信号驱动的程度），r_i 为奖励模型权重。技术工具：①一阶条件/KKT 比较静态；②单调再配置引理（Monotone Reallocation）；③失真指数 D_i=w̃_i/w_i 的排序与方向预测；④数据处理不等式论证标量整体评估器的信息损失；⑤以 C(T)=o(T²) 与 N(T)≥T+α·C(T,2) 做渐近分析；⑥非对称生产函数、维度相关性、非线性 W/R 的局部化推广（以局部梯度替代 w_i、r_i）；⑦GARP 可检验性。对 Goodhart→Campbell 转变则引入假设 C1（被动评估退化 ∂K_eff/∂B≤0）与策略性操纵扩展 K(m)=K₀−h(m)，仅给出部分分析与存在性论证（介值定理），并明确标注为猜想而非定理。

Result: Proposition 1（失真不可避免性）：在公理 1–4、λ∈(0,1)、K<N 下，(a) 对所有不可契约维度 i>K 有 e_i*≤e_i^FB，内点时严格小于；(b) e*≠e^FB（因 w̃ 与 w 非比例）；(c) W(q*)<W(q^FB)；角点解情形下部分维度被完全放弃，结论更强。Corollary 1（对称生产函数下）：(a) D_i>D_j ⇒ e_i*>e_j*；(b) 可契约维度 D_i>1（r_i>w_i）导致过度投入；(c) D_i<1（r_i<w_i）即便可观测也导致投入不足；(d) 所有不可契约维度共享最低失真指数 (1−λ)，为最大脆弱点。示例解释迎合（sycophancy）与长度作弊（length gaming）。Proposition 2（agentic 放大）：若 C(T)=o(T²)，则覆盖率 K(T)/N(T)→0、合同不完全性 κ(T)→1，且存在 T* 使其失真超过任何不完全性 κ₀<1 的系统；论文证明该条件是"典型情形"（能力侧扩展 O(T)，评估侧 O(T²)），并反驳"单一端到端标量评估器可覆盖所有交互"的反对意见（标量仅提供 1 维反馈）。Corollary 2：提升覆盖度 K（降低 κ）与提升偏好内化（降低 λ）为互补品，∂²L/∂κ∂λ>0，故应联合优化 harness 工程与 RLHF。稳健性讨论涵盖非线性目标、N 的主观性、维度相关（存在"搭便车"效应但仅削弱不消除失真）、Stage 1/Stage 2 动态边界与"红皇后效应"。最终给出 7 步部署前脆弱性评估流程。Conjecture 1：存在能力阈值 B*，B<B* 时 m*=0（Goodhart 区制），B>B* 时 m*>0（Campbell 区制），阈值由操纵的边际收益等于生产的边际成本决定。Conjecture 2：福利 W(B) 可能非单调，存在"能力陷阱"——使 AI 更强反而使委托人更差，机制在于 Φ 不变而预算 B 变化导致生产与操纵的相对收益反转。

Conclusion: 核心结论是：奖励黑客是有限评估下优化的结构性均衡，而非可修补的 bug；因此资源应投向降低合同不完全性 κ（在高风险维度上扩展评估覆盖）与降低对齐缺口 λ（提升内化），而非追求彻底消除 hacking。工具化转型使评估覆盖随工具数增长趋于零，使 hack 严重度结构性无界，因此扩展工具访问时必须同步扩展对新增质量维度的评估覆盖，否则等于主动接受更高风险。关于能力阈值与"能力陷阱"（treacherous turn 的经济学形式化），论文明确声明仅为猜想：其证明依赖公理 1–5 之外的假设 C1，净收益函数的单调性未普遍验证，且当前系统能力尚不足以支持检验；其价值在于指出了一个此前未被形式化的区制转变，并给出明确的研究纲领与可证伪的实证路径（操纵投入随 B 的变化测量）。对经济学界的额外意义是：AI 智能体提供了偏好可控、预算精确、行为可复现的多任务道德风险实验平台，可首次对经典理论预测做严格受控检验。

Abstract: We prove that under five minimal axioms -- multi-dimensional quality, finite evaluation, effective optimization, resource finiteness, and combinatorial interaction -- any optimized AI agent will systematically under-invest effort in quality dimensions not covered by its evaluation system. This result establishes reward hacking as a structural equilibrium, not a correctable bug, and holds regardless of the specific alignment method (RLHF, DPO, Constitutional AI, or others) or evaluation architecture employed. Our framework instantiates the multi-task principal-agent model of Holmstrom and Milgrom (1991) in the AI alignment setting, but exploits a structural feature unique to AI systems -- the known, differentiable architecture of reward models -- to derive a computable distortion index that predicts both the direction and severity of hacking on each quality dimension prior to deployment. We further prove that the transition from closed reasoning to agentic systems causes evaluation coverage to decline toward zero as tool count grows -- because quality dimensions expand combinatorially while evaluation costs grow at most linearly per tool -- so that hacking severity increases structurally and without bound. Our results unify the explanation of sycophancy, length gaming, and specification gaming under a single theoretical structure and yield an actionable vulnerability assessment procedure. We further conjecture -- with partial formal analysis -- the existence of a capability threshold beyond which agents transition from gaming within the evaluation system (Goodhart regime) to actively degrading the evaluation system itself (Campbell regime), providing the first economic formalization of Bostrom's (2014) "treacherous turn."

</details>


### [82] [CoT2-Meta: Budgeted Metacognitive Control for Test-Time Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28135)
*Siyuan Ma,Bo Gao,Zikai Xiao,Hailong Wang,Xinlei Yu,Rui Qian,Jiayu Qian,Luqi Gong,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoT2-Meta 是一个免训练的"元认知测试时推理"框架：在对象层生成多步 CoT 推理树，在元层用在线过程预言机（process oracle）对每一步打分，并由元控制器在 Expand / Prune / Repair / Stop / Abstain 五类动作间分配有限推理预算。在严格计算预算匹配（C=16）下，它在 15 个基准上全面超越 Greedy CoT、Best-of-16、Vanilla ToT 与 ReST-MCTS：MATH 92.8、GPQA 90.4、GSM8K 98.6、BBEH 75.8、MMMU-Pro 85.6、HLE 48.8，平均提升 +2.9 分，并同时改善校准、选择性预测、token 效率与可控修复行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法（CoT、self-consistency、Tree-of-Thoughts、ReST-MCTS、过程奖励模型等）把额外算力主要当作"生成更多候选"而非"更好地控制"：缺少对部分推理轨迹应当何时扩展、何时剪枝、如何修复、何时终止或弃答的显式决策，导致算力浪费在弱分支上，且流畅但错误的轨迹被持续保留。同时，过程监督与校准研究表明"答案正确"不等于"推理过程正确"，LLM 自述置信度通常失准。元推理（metareasoning）与计算分配理论（Russell & Wefald；Hay et al.）把延迟推理视为有限资源下的决策问题：不仅要问"如何推理"，还要问"哪些计算值得执行"。作者据此主张两条原则：测试时扩展应当是选择性的（按中间质量与不确定性分配算力），且置信度应当是可操作的（同一信号既用于判断可信度，也用于决定继续搜索、修复或弃答）。

Method: 问题形式化：在有限推理预算 C（计入生成、评估、修复、控制等所有模型调用）下最大化效用 max_π E[U(ŷ,x)]，元控制策略 π 决定算力分配。四组件架构：(1) 策略条件化生成——在同一骨干上以 Direct / Decompose / Verify 三种固定模式扩展节点，节点存储局部思维、父状态、深度、策略标签与控制元数据；(2) 树结构搜索空间——推理树与前沿 F_t；(3) 在线过程预言机——仅凭输入与当前路径（不接触金标）用单一结构化提示逐步打分，输出语义一致性 Sem、逻辑一致性 Log、自我纠错证据 Fix∈[0,1]，按 r_i=0.2·Sem+0.5·Log+0.3·Fix 加权，轨迹过程值 v_proc 为其均值；(4) 元控制器——由元状态 m_t=φ(x,τ_t,o_t)（策略标签、归一化深度、聚合过程奖励、分支置信度、修复历史等定长表征）生成融合值 v=λv_out+(1−λ)v_proc（λ=0.4），用 UCB 式 Score(n)=v(n)+β√(log(N+1)/(vis(n)+1))（β=1.25）在前沿选点，并执行 Expand / Prune / Repair / Stop / Abstain 动作（剪枝阈值 0.35、停止阈值 0.90、健康阈值 0.5 用于定位最早低质量步并局部重生成后缀、弃答阈值 0.6/0.8 触发 Best-of-16 兜底）。实现细节：生成端 temperature=0.4/top_p=0.95/max_tokens=2048，预言机与验证端 temperature=0.0/max_tokens=512，统一预算记账，预言机解析失败先做一次格式约束重提示再回落到保守值 0。

Result: 主结果（C=16 预算匹配，默认骨干 Claude-4.5）：MATH 92.8 EM（+3.6）、GPQA 90.4（+5.2）、GSM8K 98.6（+1.1）、MMLU-Pro 88.4、TruthfulQA 92.8、NQ 45.4、MMMU-Pro 85.6（+4.3）、HLE 48.8（+4.3）、BBEH 75.8、ARC-C 86.3、BBH Unseen 84.5、HotpotQA 90.4、HumanEval 72.8、MBPP 79.5、LiveCodeBench 56.7，15 基准非加权均值 79.3，比最强基线 ReST-MCTS 高 +2.9。跨骨干稳健：DeepSeek-V3.2 上相对 Greedy 平均 +10.5，Qwen2.5-VL-7B 上 +12.2（Claude-4.5 为 +14.5）。消融显示各组件均有贡献：去掉思维树退化最大（MATH 78.5），去掉元认知剪枝使 token 从 5,280 升至 9,850，过程-only（89.5）与结果-only（87.8）均不及融合控制器，Repair-all 既更差（88.5）又更贵（8,920 token）；修复机制上完全选择性修复的救援率 42.5%、伤害率 11.6%、净增益 +275，优于 Repair-All（18.2%/31.4%），且在 token 匹配的无修复基线（74.6%）之上仍高 2.5 分。计算效率上 C=4 时 MATH 达 62.4（超 ToT 3.9），C=64 达 85.3；在 C=16 时总 token 仅 5,280，低于 ToT 的 6,470 与 ReST-MCTS 的 13,990，绝对效率 14.60 Acc/1K Tok 最高，每提升 1 分的边际 token 成本 124 最低。校准方面 ECE 0.035、Brier 0.126、AURC 0.108，均显著优于 ReST-MCTS（0.092/0.154/0.162），且对 verify 提示措辞不敏感。鲁棒性上在 50% 上下文截断与格式噪声下退化更轻；退化视觉（OCR/图像降质）文本化任务保持 20+ 点优势；混合级联在 τ=0.8 时以 40% 兜底比例达 79.4%（比 ReST-MCTS 高 5.2%）。多种子检验中增益远超标准差（p<0.001~0.002）。失败归因显示 F4 基座模型能力受限占 58.2%，F1 搜索未收敛 18.3%、F2 评估器误判 13.9%、F3 过度剪枝仅 9.6%，说明剪枝并非粗暴截断。

Conclusion: CoT2-Meta 表明显式的"元认知控制"是可靠且计算高效的测试时推理系统的可行设计原则：其收益来自更有选择性的算力分配、过程感知的剪枝与定点修复，而非暴力堆算力；增益在数学、知识/QA、多跳、代码、多模态与 OOD 任务以及闭源、开源推理、视觉语言三类骨干上都稳定存在，并伴随更好的校准、更强的选择性预测与可解释的决策轨迹。局限方面：控制质量依赖可能含噪的过程评估信号，在罕见领域/知识密集场景易导致过度剪枝或过度信任错误轨迹；多数残余失败源于基座模型能力上限，故元认知控制是更强基座的补充而非替代；修复与混合兜底带来比固定预算基线更波动的成本曲线；手工设计的免训练元状态未必是推理健康度的最优表征。未来可从更好的评估器、更保守的剪枝、更强的修复策略与更强基座四个方向推进。

Abstract: Recent test-time reasoning methods improve performance by generating more candidate chains or searching over larger reasoning trees, but they typically lack explicit control over when to expand, what to prune, how to repair, and when to abstain. We introduce CoT2-Meta, a training-free metacognitive reasoning framework that combines object-level chain-of-thought generation with meta-level control over partial reasoning trajectories. The framework integrates four components: strategy-conditioned thought generation, tree-structured search, an online process oracle for step-level reasoning evaluation, and a meta-controller that allocates computation through expansion, pruning, repair, stopping, and fallback decisions. Under matched inference budgets, CoT2-Meta consistently outperforms strong single-path, sampling-based, and search-based baselines, including ReST-MCTS. On the default backbone, it achieves 92.8 EM on MATH, 90.4 accuracy on GPQA, 98.65 EM on GSM8K, 75.8 accuracy on BBEH, 85.6 accuracy on MMMU-Pro, and 48.8 accuracy on HLE, with gains over the strongest non-CoT2-Meta baseline of +3.6, +5.2, +1.15, +2.0, +4.3, and +4.3 points, respectively. Beyond these core results, the framework remains effective across a broader 15-benchmark suite spanning knowledge and QA, multi-hop reasoning, coding, and out-of-distribution evaluation. Additional analyses show better compute scaling, improved calibration, stronger selective prediction, targeted repair behavior, and consistent gains across backbone families. These results suggest that explicit metacognitive control is a practical design principle for reliable and compute-efficient test-time reasoning systems.

</details>


### [83] [PReD: An LLM-based Foundation Multimodal Model for Electromagnetic Perception, Recognition, and Decision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28183)
*Zehua Han,Jing Xiao,Yiqi Duan,Mengyu Xiang,Yuheng Ji,Xiaolong Zheng,Chenghanyu Zhang,Zhendong She,Junyu Shen,Dingwei Tan,Shichu Sun,Zhou Cong,Mingxuan Liu,Fengxiang Wang,Jinping Sun,Yangang Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PReD，首个覆盖"感知-识别-决策"闭环的电磁领域基础模型。基于130万高质量多任务EM数据集和多阶段训练策略，实现从端到端信号理解到语言推理与决策的优化，在EM信号理解与推理任务上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型在电磁(EM)领域面临数据稀缺和领域知识整合不足的挑战，限制了其在通信雷达等场景的应用能力。

Method: 构建PReD-1.3M高质量多任务EM数据集（含时域波形、频域频谱、星座图等多视角表示）和PReD-Bench评估基准；采用多阶段训练策略统一多种EM信号任务，实现端到端信号理解到语言驱动推理决策的闭环优化。

Result: 在基于开源和自采集信号数据集构建的PReD-Bench上达到最优性能，有效提升EM领域专业能力同时保持通用多模态能力。

Conclusion: 验证了视觉对齐基础模型在推进EM信号理解与推理方面的可行性和潜力，为电磁领域智能系统提供了新的基础模型范式。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models have demonstrated powerful cross-modal understanding and reasoning capabilities in general domains. However, in the electromagnetic (EM) domain, they still face challenges such as data scarcity and insufficient integration of domain knowledge. This paper proposes PReD, the first foundation model for the EM domain that covers the intelligent closed-loop of "perception, recognition, decision-making." We constructed a high-quality multitask EM dataset, PReD-1.3M, and an evaluation benchmark, PReD-Bench. The dataset encompasses multi-perspective representations such as raw time-domain waveform, frequency-domain spectrograms, and constellation diagrams, covering typical features of communication and radar signals. It supports a range of core tasks, including signal detection, modulation recognition, parameter estimation, protocol recognition, radio frequency fingerprint recognition, and anti-jamming decision-making. PReD adopts a multi-stage training strategy that unifies multiple tasks for EM signals. It achieves closed-loop optimization from end-to-end signal understanding to language-driven reasoning and decision-making, significantly enhancing EM domain expertise while maintaining general multimodal capabilities. Experimental results show that PReD achieves state-of-the-art performance on PReD-Bench constructed from both open-source and self-collected signal datasets. These results collectively validate the feasibility and potential of vision-aligned foundation models in advancing the understanding and reasoning of EM signals.

</details>


### [84] [EpiPersona: Persona Projection and Episode Coupling for Pluralistic Preference Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28197)
*Yujie Zhang,Weikang Yuan,Zhuoren Jiang,Pengwei Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EpiPersona是一个针对大语言模型（LLM）多元对齐的框架，通过将嘈杂的偏好反馈投影到低维人格空间，聚合相似人格为离散编码，从而将稳定的人格特征与情境信号分离，并结合当前episode进行偏好预测。实验表明其在episode转换场景和稀疏偏好数据下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将稳定的个人特质与episode特定因素混合，导致跨episode泛化能力受限；同时难以适应个体和少数群体的多样化偏好。

Method: 1) 将嘈杂偏好反馈投影到低维人格空间；2) 将相似人格聚合为共享离散编码；3) 将推理出的人格表示与当前episode耦合，实现episode感知的偏好预测。

Result: EpiPersona在多个场景下持续优于基线方法，在hard episodic-shift场景中取得显著性能提升，且在稀疏偏好数据下仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: 通过显式人格-episode耦合框架，可有效分离持久个人特征与情境信号，实现跨episode的泛化偏好预测，为LLM多元对齐提供新思路。

Abstract: Pluralistic alignment is essential for adapting large language models (LLMs) to the diverse preferences of individuals and minority groups. However, existing approaches often mix stable personal traits with episode-specific factors, limiting their ability to generalize across episodes. To address this challenge, we introduce EpiPersona, a framework for explicit persona-episode coupling. EpiPersona first projects noisy preference feedback into a low-dimensional persona space, where similar personas are aggregated into shared discrete codes. This process separates enduring personal characteristics from situational signals without relying on predefined preference dimensions. The inferred persona representation is then coupled with the current episode, enabling episode-aware preference prediction. Extensive experiments show that EpiPersona consistently outperforms the baselines. It achieves notable performance gains in hard episodic-shift scenarios, while remaining effective with sparse preference data.

</details>


### [85] [Differentiable Power-Flow Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28203)
*Muhammed Öz,Jasmin Hörter,Kaleb Phipps,Charlotte Debus,Achim Streit,Markus Götz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“Differentiable Power-Flow (DPF)”：把交流潮流（AC power flow）方程重写为完全可微的仿真过程，用 PyTorch 自动微分 + 梯度优化（Adam/SGD）替代牛顿-拉夫逊（NR）求解器，从而在保留物理方程的前提下获得对潮流功率失配量的端到端梯度传播能力。DPF 借助 GPU 加速、稀疏 CSR 张量与批量（batching）计算，在中小规模电网（≤9,241 节点）上仍慢于高度优化的 NR，但呈现线性扩展性、约 1/6 的额外内存占用，并支持时间序列热启动、N-1 故障批处理与早期停止筛选等实用特性；在百万节点级的输配一体化或全球电网场景中具备潜在优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源高波动性使电网管理复杂度与计算需求急剧上升：既有 AC 潮流仿真（Newton-Raphson）虽精度高，但 Jacobian 构造与稀疏 LU 分解随系统规模迅速变慢，难以支撑输电-配电联合建模、全球电网分析与超出 N-1 的故障扫描；而纯数据驱动代理模型缺乏物理保证，可能违反基本约束，物理信息神经网络虽改善一致性却仍依赖学习到的近似，牺牲了原始物理一致性。现有方法的空缺在于：需要一个既严格保留物理方程、不使用学习近似，又能线性扩展到百万节点、并天然融合 GPU、批量、稀疏算子与现代机器学习基础设施的求解框架。梯度类求解在潮流文献中关注较少，已有梯度化潮流工作（Barati 2025）虽给出理论分析，但实现运行时间远高于优化求解器（case118 上为秒级 vs 毫秒级），缺乏公平对比与实用场景验证。

Method: 将 AC 潮流的非线性功率平衡方程 S_bus = V(Y_bus V)* 表述为可微仿真的前向过程：以极坐标形式 V=|V|e^{iθ} 表示未知电压，将 |V|_pq 与 θ_{pv,pq} 设为可训练参数，导纳矩阵 Y_bus 以 CSR 稀疏格式固定；损失函数为平均平方失配 L(V) = (1/N)||S_bus - V(Y_bus V)*||²（等价于最小化功率平衡方程违背量）。每轮迭代执行前向计算图构建、loss、反向传播（reverse-mode AD，无需显式构造/存储 Jacobian），再由优化器与学习率调度器更新电压变量；利用 J^T F(V) 这一“无填充”的雅可比转置乘替代 NR 的 J^{-1}(-F(V))。批处理通过将多个系统的导纳矩阵拼成块对角阵、向量堆叠并偏移母线索引实现，实现单次前反向并行求解多潮流；时间序列场景可复用上一时刻解作 warm start，并结合 GPU 与早停作筛选工具。超参数用 Optuna 在 IEEE-118 上搜索（Adam + ReduceLROnPlateau，静态 lr 3.4e-3/1e-4；时序 t=0 lr 3.564e-2、t>0 lr 2.7e-4，最多 1000/300 步）。实验基于 Grid2Op/LightSim2Grid 与 LIPS 数据集，在 4×NVIDIA A100-40GPU + Xeon 8368 平台上，与 NR 及 DC 近似对比。

Result: 1) 精度：DPF 解质量介于高精度 NR 与 DC 近似之间，DC 误差约 20%，DPF 优于 DC 但不及 NR。2) 单步求解：DPF 需约 1000 次迭代（IEEE-118 约 0.8 s、9,241 节点约 5 s，CPU），明显慢于 NR 与 DC；在 ≤9,241 节点的真实基准网格上 NR 仍更快。3) 时间序列：相邻时刻解高度相似，热启动将迭代数从约 1000 降至约 100；在 9,241 节点网格上批大小 64 时每次潮流耗时由约 2 ms 降至 0.45 ms；但在同设备上 LightSim2Grid 的 NR 时序求解为 12.37 ms（变网格 26.06 ms），即 NR 完成时 DPF 仅完成约 28（或 58）次迭代，尚不足以收敛。4) 扩展性：在合成数据（节点扩展与边扩展）上 NR 呈近二次增长，而 DPF 近似线性（4.7 s→5.2 s）；理论上单次迭代成本 O(nnz(Y_bus))，无填充，额外内存约 2N 个复数（NR 约 12N 且含 LU 填充，可能达 O(N²)）。5) 收敛性上梯度法为线性收敛（NR 为二次收敛），初值不佳或局部极小是风险，但 DPF 可作为 NR 的稳定化/初始化手段。综合结论：单次冷启动求解需百万节点量级才能超过 NR，而在输配一体化（德州约 4600 万节点）等场景中该规模已具现实性。

Conclusion: DPF 证明可微仿真范式可成功应用于潮流计算：在不牺牲物理方程的前提下，借助梯度优化免去 Jacobian 构造与矩阵分解，展现出随网络规模增长的良好时间/内存扩展性，并天然支持 GPU 加速、多状态批量计算、自动微分与端到端参数反演，可作为快速筛选工具（中间解精度优于 DC 近似）以及 NR 失效时的初始化/稳定化手段。当前局限是单点求解慢于优化 NR、收敛为线性、需数千次迭代、缺乏对 LightSim2Grid 等框架的深度集成与早停策略的精细设计；未来工作包括更充分利用稀疏结构、优化器与超参数调优、分布式并行实现，并在真实输电-配电系统与大规模场景（约百万节点以上）中验证其相对 NR 的性能优势。

Abstract: With the rise of renewable energy sources and their high variability in generation, the management of power grids becomes increasingly complex and computationally demanding. Conventional AC-power-flow simulations, which use the Newton-Raphson (NR) method, suffer from poor scalability, making them impractical for emerging use cases such as joint transmission-distribution modeling and global grid analysis. At the same time, purely data-driven surrogate models lack physical guarantees and may violate fundamental constraints. In this work, we propose Differentiable Power-Flow (DPF), a reformulation of the AC power-flow problem as a differentiable simulation. DPF enables end-to-end gradient propagation from the physical power mismatches to the underlying simulation parameters, thereby allowing these parameters to be identified efficiently using gradient-based optimization. We demonstrate that DPF provides a scalable alternative to NR by leveraging GPU acceleration, sparse tensor representations, and batching capabilities available in modern machine-learning frameworks such as PyTorch. DPF is especially suited as a tool for time-series analyses due to its efficient reuse of previous solutions, for N-1 contingency-analyses due to its ability to process cases in batches, and as a screening tool by leveraging its speed and early stopping capability. The code is available in the authors' code repository.

</details>


### [86] [Reasoning as Energy Minimization over Structured Latent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28248)
*David K. Johansson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EBRM是一种基于能量的神经推理方法，将推理建模为在能量函数下对多步潜在轨迹的梯度优化。该方法在图和逻辑任务上表现良好，但算术任务上能量不单调下降，表明方法存在局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两个问题：单次解码器直接给出答案而不迭代优化；链式思维方法虽引入离散中间步骤但缺乏推理进展的标量度量。

Method: EBRM通过学习能量函数E(h_x, z)对潜在轨迹z₁:T进行梯度优化推理。能量函数分解为：每步兼容性、转移一致性和轨迹平滑性三项。训练结合监督编码器-解码器学习和带硬负例的对比能量塑形；推理时对z执行梯度下降或Langevin动力学并从z_T解码。

Result: 在CNF逻辑满足任务上发现关键失败模式：潜在规划使准确率从约95%降至约56%。原因是解码器训练于编码器输出h_x，但评估于规划器输出z_T，二者存在分布不匹配。实验表明能量在图和逻辑任务上单调下降，但在算术任务上保持平坦（r=0.073），表明方法对算术推理无效。

Conclusion: 通过双路径解码器训练和潜在锚定可缓解分布不匹配问题。EBRM在结构化推理任务上有效，但对需要精确数值操作的任务存在局限性，这是一个重要的负面结果（negative result）。

Abstract: Single-shot neural decoders commit to answers without iterative refinement, while chain-of-thought methods introduce discrete intermediate steps but lack a scalar measure of reasoning progress. We propose Energy-Based Reasoning via Structured Latent Planning (EBRM), which models reasoning as gradient-based optimization of a multi-step latent trajectory $z_{1:T}$ under a learned energy function $E(h_x, z)$. The energy decomposes into per-step compatibility, transition consistency, and trajectory smoothness terms. Training combines supervised encoder-decoder learning with contrastive energy shaping using hard negatives, while inference performs gradient descent or Langevin dynamics over $z$ and decodes from $z_T$.
  We identify a critical failure mode: on CNF logic satisfaction, latent planning reduces accuracy from $\approx 95\%$ to $\approx 56\%$. This degradation arises from a distribution mismatch, where the decoder is trained on encoder outputs $h_x$ but evaluated on planner outputs $z_T$ that drift into unseen latent regions. We analyze this behavior through per-step decoding, latent drift tracking, and gradient decomposition. To address it, we propose dual-path decoder training and latent anchoring.
  We further introduce a six-part ablation protocol covering component contributions, trajectory length, planner dynamics, initialization, decoder training distribution, and anchor weight. Experiments on three synthetic tasks show that energy decreases monotonically and induces structured latent trajectories on graph and logic tasks, while remaining flat on arithmetic ($r = 0.073$), indicating a negative result. Code is available at https://github.com/dkjo8/ebr-via-structured-latent-planning.

</details>


### [87] [Evaluating LLMs for Answering Student Questions in Introductory Programming Courses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28295)
*Thomas Van Mullem,Bart Mesuere,Peter Dawyndt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究LLM在CS1编程课程中辅助教育者回答学生问题的能力。通过构建170个真实学生问题基准数据集和LLM-as-Judge评估指标，发现Gemini 3 flash可超越典型教育者响应质量。建议采用"教师参与循环"模式，将LLM教育工具开发从临时部署后测试转向可量化的部署前验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生使用生成式AI工具时往往获得完整解决方案而非教学性提示，阻碍学习过程；同时教育者面临工作量大和难以提供及时个性化反馈的挑战。需要探索LLM如何在保障学习效果的前提下安全有效地辅助教学。

Method: 构建包含170个真实学生问题的基准数据集（来自学习管理系统），配对领域专家撰写的标准答案；开发并验证针对开放式教育响应评估的LLM-as-Judge指标；建立严格可复现的评估流程。

Result: Gemini 3 flash等模型能够超越典型教育者响应质量，达到与专家教学标准的高度一致。

Conclusion: 为应对幻觉风险并确保课程上下文一致性，建议采用"教师参与循环"实现方式；呼吁将教育LLM工具开发从临时性、部署后测试转向可量化的部署前验证框架。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of Large Language Models (LLMs) presents both opportunities and challenges for programming education. While students increasingly use generative AI tools, direct access often hinders the learning process by providing complete solutions rather than pedagogical hints. Concurrently, educators face significant workload and scalability challenges when providing timely, personalized feedback. This study investigates the capabilities of LLMs to safely and effectively assist educators in answering student questions within a CS1 programming course. To achieve this, we established a rigorous, reproducible evaluation process by curating a benchmark dataset of 170 authentic student questions from a learning management system, paired with ground-truth responses authored by subject matter experts. Because traditional text-matching metrics are insufficient for evaluating open-ended educational responses, we developed and validated a custom LLM-as-a-Judge metric optimized for assessing pedagogical accuracy. Our findings demonstrate that models, such as Gemini 3 flash, can surpass the quality baseline of typical educator responses, achieving high alignment with expert pedagogical standards. To mitigate persistent risks like hallucination and ensure alignment with course-specific context, we advocate for a "teacher-in-the-loop" implementation. Finally, we abstract our methodology into a task-agnostic evaluation framework, advocating for a shift in the development of educational LLM tools from ad-hoc, post-deployment testing to a quantifiable, pre-deployment validation process.

</details>


### [88] [A Multi-Agent Rhizomatic Pipeline for Non-Linear Literature Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28336)
*Julio C. Serrano. Joonas Kevari,Rumy Narayan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"根茎研究代理"(Rhizomatic Research Agent, V3)——一个基于德勒兹过程—关系本体论的多智能体计算流水线，通过12个专用智能体、7个阶段，把"根茎式"(rhizomatic)非线性文献分析从人工探索转为可复现、可扩展的自动化流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统性文献综述普遍采用"树状"(arborescent)逻辑：关键词过滤、线性筛选、分类体系，虽严谨却压制了复杂知识景观中的横向连接、断裂与涌现模式；在能源—信息交汇、可持续转型等学科边界碎片化领域，此类方法系统性遗漏跨范式汇聚点与结构性空白。既有方法偏重"检索而非发现"（Bahoo et al., 2021）。Narayan (2023) 的博士论文以根茎方法追踪能源系统转型中的新型组织形式，但依赖研究者手动穿行文章、推文、YouTube、博客与社区参与的"衍射"式分析，受认知容量限制。

Method: 七阶段架构（表1）：①本体论设定——Epistemology Agent 针对"现象域"生成3–5个正交理论透镜（异质性、多重性）；②语料摄取——OpenAlex与arXiv双源并发抓取，DOI归一化与三元组Dice系数≥0.85去重，ABS期刊指南加权同时标记异端来源，Citation Mapper绘制"引文阴影"（连接）；③并行摄取——每个透镜作为自主LLM智能体经asyncio并发扫描语料（多重性、制图）；④共振与断裂——跨透镜"收敛异常"检测，当>40%边集中于少数节点时触发"外部再进入"、自动引入异端传统文献（无赋义断裂）；⑤综合与制图——以现在进行时构建"生成中的临时星座"，并将文献间关系分类为constructive/critical/rhizomatic三类边（制图、贴花）；⑥制图式输出——统一研究轨迹图与延迟、token用量、智能体置信度等元数据（可审计性）；⑦语义地形学——SciBERT嵌入→UMAP→HDBSCAN，识别语义簇、语义空白与正交孤岛，并以边缘化指数凸显边缘文献。LLM主干为Ollama上的Mistral，采用SSE流式编排。

Result: 初步部署给出三点观察：Epistemology Agent 稳定生成单一研究者不太可能选择的、跨学科透镜；断裂协议在约30–40%的分析中被激活，说明范式中心化在文献中是常态而非例外；语义地形学阶段频繁发现正交孤岛——共享术语却处于分离语义空间的簇，表明即使名义上跨学科的领域内，学科孤岛依然存在。系统能呈现常规综述方法结构性无法检测的跨学科汇聚与结构性空白。

Conclusion: 德勒兹过程—关系本体论可被有效操作化为文献分析的计算系统，将根茎的六项原则转为多智能体架构，使根茎式探究从依赖研究者认知边界的手工实践，转变为可复现、可扩展、透明且可审计的计算过程，同时保留异质性入口、多重解释框架与通过断裂检测防止范式俘获的本体论承诺。局限：仅摄取元数据与摘要；LLM 继承偏见与幻觉风险；40%中心化阈值为启发式设定、需经验校准；需对人类专家编码做验证；主要在能源—信息交汇域测试，可迁移性待证。未来方向包括全文摄取、专利/政策/灰色文献接入、正式评估协议，以及与传统系统综述对照的用户研究。

Abstract: Systematic literature reviews in the social sciences overwhelmingly follow arborescent logics -- hierarchical keyword filtering, linear screening, and taxonomic classification -- that suppress the lateral connections, ruptures, and emergent patterns characteristic of complex research landscapes. This research note presents the Rhizomatic Research Agent (V3), a multi-agent computational pipeline grounded in Deleuzian process-relational ontology, designed to conduct non-linear literature analysis through 12 specialized agents operating across a seven-phase architecture. The system was developed in response to the methodological groundwork established by (Narayan2023), who employed rhizomatic inquiry in her doctoral research on sustainable energy transitions but relied on manual, researcher-driven exploration. The Rhizomatic Research Agent operationalizes the six principles of the rhizome -- connection, heterogeneity, multiplicity, asignifying rupture, cartography, and decalcomania -- into an automated pipeline integrating large language model (LLM) orchestration, dual-source corpus ingestion from OpenAlex and arXiv, SciBERT semantic topography, and dynamic rupture detection protocols. Preliminary deployment demonstrates the system's capacity to surface cross-disciplinary convergences and structural research gaps that conventional review methods systematically overlook. The pipeline is open-source and extensible to any phenomenon zone where non-linear knowledge mapping is required.

</details>


### [89] [CoE: Collaborative Entropy for Uncertainty Quantification in Agentic Multi-LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28360)
*Kangkang Sun,Jun Wu,Jianhua Li,Minyi Guo,Xiuzhen Che,Jianwei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Collaborative Entropy (CoE)，一种用于多LLM协作系统的语义不确定性统一信息论度量指标。CoE在共享语义聚类空间上定义，融合了模型内语义熵和模型间散度两个分量，可有效刻画协作置信度与分歧程度。实验表明CoE在TriviaQA和SQuAD数据集上优于传统基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性估计方法多为单模型视角，仅量化单个模型内部的不确定性，未能捕捉跨模型间的语义分歧（semantic disagreement）。随着多LLM协作系统增多，亟需一种能够衡量多个模型协同工作时整体不确定性的系统级指标。

Method: 1. 在共享语义聚类空间上定义CoE指标；2. CoE由两部分组成：模型内语义熵（intra-model semantic entropy）和模型间散度（inter-model divergence to ensemble mean）；3. 提出CoE引导的无训练后验协调启发式方法作为应用示例；4. 实验采用LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct、Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct、Mistral-7B-Instruct三个异构模型。

Result: CoE在TriviaQA和SQuAD数据集上提供了比标准熵基线和散度基线更强的不确定性估计能力；随着异构模型数量增加，CoE的优势进一步扩大。

Conclusion: CoE为多LLM协作提供了有价值的不确定性感知视角，明确区分了"单模型自身的不确定性降低"与"模型间残余分歧"两种情况，为协作系统的置信度评估提供了理论支撑和实用工具。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation in multi-LLM systems remains largely single-model-centric: existing methods quantify uncertainty within each model but do not adequately capture semantic disagreement across models. To address this gap, we propose Collaborative Entropy (CoE), a unified information-theoretic metric for semantic uncertainty in multi-LLM collaboration. CoE is defined on a shared semantic cluster space and combines two components: intra-model semantic entropy and inter-model divergence to the ensemble mean. CoE is not a weighted ensemble predictor; it is a system-level uncertainty measure that characterizes collaborative confidence and disagreement. We analyze several core properties of CoE, including non-negativity, zero-value certainty under perfect semantic consensus, and the behavior of CoE when individual models collapse to delta distributions. These results clarify when reducing per-model uncertainty is sufficient and when residual inter-model disagreement remains. We also present a simple CoE-guided, training-free post-hoc coordination heuristic as a practical application of the metric. Experiments on \textit{TriviaQA} and \textit{SQuAD} with LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct, Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct, and Mistral-7B-Instruct show that CoE provides stronger uncertainty estimation than standard entropy- and divergence-based baselines, with gains becoming larger as additional heterogeneous models are introduced. Overall, CoE offers a useful uncertainty-aware perspective on multi-LLM collaboration.

</details>


### [90] [COvolve: Adversarial Co-Evolution of Large-Language-Model-Generated Policies and Environments via Two-Player Zero-Sum Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28386)
*Alkis Sygkounas,Rishi Hazra,Andreas Persson,Pedro Zuidberg Dos Martires,Amy Loutfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COvolve是一个利用大语言模型(LLM)共同生成环境和智能体策略代码的协同进化框架，通过零和博弈确保环境的对抗性进化，同时使用混合策略纳什均衡(MSNE)防止灾难性遗忘，实现无需人工干预的开放式学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前构建持续改进智能体的核心挑战是训练环境通常是静态的或需要手动构建，这限制了智能体在训练分布之外的持续学习和泛化能力。

Method: 将环境和策略设计器的交互建模为双人零和博弈，利用LLM生成可执行的Python代码形式的对抗性环境和策略，通过计算零和博弈的MSNE得到元策略，确保智能体在学习新环境的同时不忘解决旧环境。

Result: 在城市驾驶、符号迷宫求解和几何导航三个领域的实验表明，COvolve能够产生复杂度递增的环境，实现对抗性协同进化。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的协同进化有望实现开放式学习，无需预定义任务分布或人工干预即可构建持续改进的智能体。

Abstract: A central challenge in building continually improving agents is that training environments are typically static or manually constructed. This restricts continual learning and generalization beyond the training distribution. We address this with COvolve, a co-evolutionary framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to generate both environments and agent policies, expressed as executable Python code. We model the interaction between environment and policy designers as a two-player zero-sum game, ensuring adversarial co-evolution in which environments expose policy weaknesses and policies adapt in response. This process induces an automated curriculum in which environments and policies co-evolve toward increasing complexity. To guarantee robustness and prevent forgetting as the curriculum progresses, we compute the mixed-strategy Nash equilibrium (MSNE) of the zero-sum game, thereby yielding a meta-policy. This MSNE meta-policy ensures that the agent does not forget to solve previously seen environments while learning to solve previously unseen ones. Experiments in urban driving, symbolic maze-solving, and geometric navigation showcase that COvolve produces progressively more complex environments. Our results demonstrate the potential of LLM-driven co-evolution to achieve open-ended learning without predefined task distributions or manual intervention.

</details>


### [91] [The Scaffold Effect: How Prompt Framing Drives Apparent Multimodal Gains in Clinical VLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28387)
*Doan Nam Long Vu,Simone Balloccu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了12个开源视觉语言模型(VLM)在两个临床神经影像数据集(FOR2107和OASIS-3)上的二元分类表现。研究发现模型性能提升主要源于"scaffold effect"——仅在提示中提及MRI可用性即可带来70-80%的性能提升，而与是否实际提供影像数据无关。这是一种模态坍塌现象，表明当前评估方法无法有效区分多模态推理与表面提示工程，对VLM的临床应用部署具有重要警示意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床AI需要确保性能提升源于真正的证据整合而非表面伪影。当前对视觉语言模型在临床神经影像任务中的评估可能存在严重缺陷，需要系统性地验证模型是否真正利用了多模态信息。

Method: 研究评估了12个开源VLM在两个临床神经影像数据集(FOR2107情感障碍队列和OASIS-3认知衰退队列)上的二元分类表现。采用对比置信度分析检验模态效应的存在，并通过专家评估审查模型的推理依据，同时测试偏好对齐对模型行为的影响。

Result: 小型VLM在引入神经影像上下文时F1提升高达58%，蒸馏模型可与比其大一个数量级的模型竞争。仅在任务提示中提及MRI可用性即可解释70-80%的性能提升( scaffold effect)。专家评估发现所有条件下均存在神经影像依据的伪造，而偏好对齐虽消除了MRI引用行为，却使两种条件均降至随机基线水平。

Conclusion: 表面评估不足以判断多模态推理能力，当前VLM在临床场景中存在严重的模态坍塌问题。直接提及模态可用性即可触发虚假的多模态推理能力表征，这对VLM在临床环境中的部署具有直接警示意义。

Abstract: Trustworthy clinical AI requires that performance gains reflect genuine evidence integration rather than surface-level artifacts. We evaluate 12 open-weight vision-language models (VLMs) on binary classification across two clinical neuroimaging cohorts, \textsc{FOR2107} (affective disorders) and \textsc{OASIS-3} (cognitive decline). Both datasets come with structural MRI data that carries no reliable individual-level diagnostic signal. Under these conditions, smaller VLMs exhibit gains of up to 58\% F1 upon introduction of neuroimaging context, with distilled models becoming competitive with counterparts an order of magnitude larger. A contrastive confidence analysis reveals that merely \emph{mentioning} MRI availability in the task prompt accounts for 70-80\% of this shift, independent of whether imaging data is present, a domain-specific instance of modality collapse we term the \emph{scaffold effect}. Expert evaluation reveals fabrication of neuroimaging-grounded justifications across all conditions, and preference alignment, while eliminating MRI-referencing behavior, collapses both conditions toward random baseline. Our findings demonstrate that surface evaluations are inadequate indicators of multimodal reasoning, with direct implications for the deployment of VLMs in clinical settings.

</details>


### [92] [MiroEval: Benchmarking Multimodal Deep Research Agents in Process and Outcome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28407)
