Method: 将多周期、多对多制造资源匹配问题建模为序贯决策过程；采用无模型深度强化学习方法避免显式建模转移动态；针对Q-learning中最大算子导致的问题，引入基于先验策略的领域知识惩罚和符合供需约束的不可行性惩罚；将改进方法嵌入DDPG算法，形成DKDDPG算法。

Result: 小规模问题可获得收敛性理论保证；大规模实验中，DKDDPG始终优于传统DDPG和其他RL算法，获得更高奖励，时间和轮次效率更高。

Conclusion: 通过引入领域知识惩罚和不可行性惩罚，有效解决了Q-learning的收敛问题，提升了DDPG在制造资源匹配问题上的性能，为实际制造资源调度提供了有效的强化学习解决方案。

Abstract: Matching plays an important role in the logical allocation of resources across a wide range of industries. The benefits of matching have been increasingly recognized in manufacturing industries. In particular, capacity sharing has received much attention recently. In this paper, we consider the problem of dynamically matching demand-capacity types of manufacturing resources. We formulate the multi-period, many-to-many manufacturing resource-matching problem as a sequential decision process. The formulated manufacturing resource-matching problem involves large state and action spaces, and it is not practical to accurately model the joint distribution of various types of demands. To address the curse of dimensionality and the difficulty of explicitly modeling the transition dynamics, we use a model-free deep reinforcement learning approach to find optimal matching policies. Moreover, to tackle the issue of infeasible actions and slow convergence due to initial biased estimates caused by the maximum operator in Q-learning, we introduce two penalties to the traditional Q-learning algorithm: a domain knowledge-based penalty based on a prior policy and an infeasibility penalty that conforms to the demand-supply constraints. We establish theoretical results on the convergence of our domain knowledge-informed Q-learning providing performance guarantee for small-size problems. For large-size problems, we further inject our modified approach into the deep deterministic policy gradient (DDPG) algorithm, which we refer to as domain knowledge-informed DDPG (DKDDPG). In our computational study, including small- and large-scale experiments, DKDDPG consistently outperformed traditional DDPG and other RL algorithms, yielding higher rewards and demonstrating greater efficiency in time and episodes.

</details>


### [144] [Hierarchy-Guided Topology Latent Flow for Molecular Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27113)
*Urvi Awasthi,Alexander Arjun Lobo,Leonid Zhukov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HLTF通过层级引导的潜在拓扑流生成3D分子，采用规划器-执行器架构解决传统方法中拓扑错误导致全局生成失败的问题。在QM9数据集上达到98.8%原子稳定性和92.9%有效唯一性，在GEOM-DRUGS数据集上达到85.5%有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物类分子的3D生成面临关键挑战：离散键拓扑中微小局部错误可导致全局失效（价态违规、断连、不可信环结构）。现有无条件3D生成器通常强调坐标生成后推断键或依赖后处理，对拓扑可行性控制较弱。

Method: 提出HLTF（层级引导潜在拓扑流），采用规划器-执行器模型生成带3D坐标的键图。核心设计包括：①潜在多尺度规划捕获全局上下文信息；②约束感知采样器抑制拓扑驱动的失效。通过显式拓扑生成减少"假有效"样本（通过RDKit sanitization但无法通过更严格检查）。

Result: QM9数据集：98.8%原子稳定性，92.9%有效-唯一性，PoseBusters有效性达94.0%（超越最强基线0.9点）。GEOM-DRUGS数据集：无需后处理达85.5%有效性/85.0%有效-唯一-新颖性，标准化弛豫后达92.2%有效性/91.2%有效-唯一-新颖性，接近最佳后处理基线（0.9点差距）。

Conclusion: HLTF通过显式拓扑生成和约束感知采样，有效解决了3D分子生成中的拓扑可行性问题，显著提升生成质量且减少对后处理的依赖，为药物发现中的3D分子生成提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Generating chemically valid 3D molecules is hindered by discrete bond topology: small local bond errors can cause global failures (valence violations, disconnections, implausible rings), especially for drug-like molecules with long-range constraints. Many unconditional 3D generators emphasize coordinates and then infer bonds or rely on post-processing, leaving topology feasibility weakly controlled. We propose Hierarchy-Guided Latent Topology Flow (HLTF), a planner-executor model that generates bond graphs with 3D coordinates, using a latent multi-scale plan for global context and a constraint-aware sampler to suppress topology-driven failures. On QM9, HLTF achieves 98.8% atom stability and 92.9% valid-and-unique, improving PoseBusters validity to 94.0% (+0.9 over the strongest reported baseline). On GEOM-DRUGS, HLTF attains 85.5%/85.0% validity/valid-unique-novel without post-processing and 92.2%/91.2% after standardized relaxation, within 0.9 points of the best post-processed baseline. Explicit topology generation also reduces "false-valid" samples that pass RDKit sanitization but fail stricter checks.

</details>


### [145] [Maximin Learning of Individualized Treatment Effect on Multi-Domain Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27114)
*Yuying Lu,Wenbo Fei,Yuanjia Wang,Molei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DRIFT框架，通过潜在因子表示和对抗学习，在高维项目级别数据上估计稳健的个体化治疗效果（ITE）。该方法利用广义因子分析学习潜在构念，构建锚定目标不确定性集以逼近潜在结果的超总体，优化最差情况性能，从而对未充分代表或未测量的临床域具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个体化治疗效果估计方法依赖单一汇总结局或特定症状集，对症状选择敏感，且无法泛化至未测量但临床相关的域。这限制了精准心理健康治疗的实施，因为患者症状表现具有异质性，涉及多个临床域。

Method: DRIFT采用三阶段方法：1）通过广义因子分析从高维项目数据学习潜在因子表示；2）构建锚定目标不确定性集，外推至观察测量之外的潜在结果超总体；3）利用对抗学习和闭合形式的最大最小解对该不确定性集进行最差情况优化，生成稳健ITE。

Result: 理论证明DRIFT对潜在因子的容许重参数化具有不变性，并提供识别和收敛性的理论保证。实证分析中，DRIFT在抑郁症随机对照试验（EMBARC）中表现优异，对外部多域结局（包括未参与训练的不良反应和自评症状）展现出更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DRIFT为精准心理健康提供了一种可证明鲁棒的ITE估计方法，能够处理高维项目数据并泛化至未测量的临床域，为多域症状异质性条件下的治疗决策提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Precision mental health requires treatment decisions that account for heterogeneous symptoms reflecting multiple clinical domains. However, existing methods for estimating individualized treatment effects (ITE) rely on a single summary outcome or a specific set of observed symptoms or measures, which are sensitive to symptom selection and limit generalizability to unmeasured yet clinically relevant domains. We propose DRIFT, a new maximin framework for estimating robust ITEs from high-dimensional item-level data by leveraging latent factor representations and adversarial learning. DRIFT learns latent constructs via generalized factor analysis, then constructs an anchored on-target uncertainty set that extrapolates beyond the observed measures to approximate the broader hyper-population of potential outcomes. By optimizing worst-case performance over this uncertainty set, DRIFT yields ITEs that are robust to underrepresented or unmeasured domains. We further show that DRIFT is invariant to admissible reparameterizations of the latent factors and admits a closed-form maximin solution, with theoretical guarantees for identification and convergence. In analyses of a randomized controlled trial for major depressive disorder (EMBARC), DRIFT demonstrates superior performance and improved generalizability to external multi-domain outcomes, including side effects and self-reported symptoms not used during training.

</details>


### [146] [Bayesian-Symbolic Integration for Uncertainty-Aware Parking Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27119)
*Alireza Nezhadettehad,Arkady Zaslavsky,Abdur Rakib,Seng W. Loke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种松耦合（loosely coupled）神经符号框架，将贝叶斯神经网络（BNN，变分推断+30%置信阈值选择预测）与基于决策树规则抽取的PLP符号推理结合，用于墨尔本CBD路内停车位占用率（5分类）预测。两种策略：Method 1 置信不足时回退至符号推理；Method 2 用符号约束裁剪BNN输出类别空间后重新预测，仍不足则用符号输出。Method 2 在全量、稀疏（90/50/10%）、噪声三种条件下、三个15分钟预测窗口的 Accuracy 与 Accuracy@1 上均全面优于 LSTM、BNN 与纯符号基线，改进幅度约 1–3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能停车系统虽借助IoT与深度学习提升了占用率预测精度，但在真实部署中常面临数据稀疏、传感器噪声、标注缺失与分布突变等挑战。现有深度模型（LSTM/GRU）缺少对预测不确定性的量化能力，也不支持符号推理与可解释决策，难以满足面向公众的城市基础设施对透明性与鲁棒性的要求。BNN虽可量化不确定性但缺乏逻辑一致性，符号规则系统虽可解释却表达能力受限。作者指出尚无工作将BNN作为神经骨干引入神经符号系统用于停车预测，因此需要一种既能输出概率化预测、又能依据领域规则进行校验与兜底的混合框架。

Method: 数据：墨尔本开放数据2019年路内停车位地磁传感器事件数据（约4270万条），清洗后按15分钟粒度在路段（两相邻路口间车位组）级别聚合，计算占用率比例；特征含当前/历史占用率、时段、星期、月份、节假日、天气类型、温度、风速、降雨；目标离散化为5类（Very Low/Low/Moderate/High/Very High，每档20%占用率）。BNN：变分推断建模权重后验，输出后验预测分布，取最大类概率与阈值比较，30%阈值为准确率与预测率最佳折中，超阈值视为有效预测。符号模块：用决策树归纳抽取可解释规则（如工作日12–14时且PrevOcc=High ⇒ VeryHigh；周末9–11时且PrevOcc=Low ⇒ Moderate；Rainfall>1.0且PrevOcc=VeryHigh ⇒ High），编码为概率逻辑程序(PLP)。Method 1（回退）：BNN置信度<30%时直接由PLP输出预测。Method 2（上下文精炼）：PLP先剔除上下文下不可能的类别（如周末9点前排除VeryHigh），BNN在剩余类别子集（如{Low,Moderate,High}）上重估置信度，超过阈值则接受精炼预测，否则回退到符号输出。实验：TensorFlow/TFP，NVIDIA GPU集群，Adam lr=0.001，batch 64，早停，80/10/10时序划分；LSTM基线三层[1500,1500,1000]；稀疏条件用90%/50%/10%随机子集训练；噪声条件对特征与标签注入合成噪声；指标为Accuracy与考虑序数相邻类的Accuracy@1；预测窗口PW1/PW2/PW3分别对应0–15、15–30、30–45分钟，独立评估。

Result: 全量数据：Method 2 PW1 Acc 57.2 / Acc@1 91.0，PW2 54.1/89.4，PW3 52.0/88.5，全面第一（LSTM 55.9/90.2、52.3/89.1、51.8/88.0；BNN 51.7/89.2 等；DT+PLP 47.4/81.3 等；Method 1 53.5/88.6 等）。稀疏条件：Method 2 仍居首，10%数据下PW1 49.5/83.6，优于LSTM 48.3/79.6、BNN 45.6/82.7、Method 1 47.2/81.5、符号 43.56/75.81。噪声条件：Method 2 PW1 52.6/89.1，仍最佳，优于LSTM 50.2/84.6、BNN 50.4/88.2与Method 1 49.1/83.4；符号模型最差(41.8/77.5)但降幅较小。BNN低置信委派比例：全量约40%，稀疏下46%–52%，噪声下约47%，说明符号模块介入频繁且必要。两种混合方法均优于纯符号推理；Method 2 的性能退化曲线最平缓。

Conclusion: 松耦合的神经符号集成可在数据稀疏与噪声条件下显著提升停车占用预测的准确性与鲁棒性，Method 2（符号约束精炼输出空间）在所有预测窗口与所有数据质量情形下均优于纯神经与纯符号基线，其优势源于在不确定样本上缩小决策空间并吸收噪声，而非仅修正低置信样本。框架还带来可解释性、可接入新政策/城市规则而无需重训网络的互操作性，对智能停车与智慧城市基础设施具有部署价值。不足与展望：提升幅度仅约1–3%且在PW3窗口优势收窄；未显式建模相邻路段的空间相关性；未来将探索端到端可微分等紧耦合策略与多模态交通实时部署。

Abstract: Accurate parking availability prediction is critical for intelligent transportation systems, but real-world deployments often face data sparsity, noise, and unpredictable changes. Addressing these challenges requires models that are not only accurate but also uncertainty-aware. In this work, we propose a loosely coupled neuro-symbolic framework that integrates Bayesian Neural Networks (BNNs) with symbolic reasoning to enhance robustness in uncertain environments. BNNs quantify predictive uncertainty, while symbolic knowledge extracted via decision trees and encoded using probabilistic logic programming is leveraged in two hybrid strategies: (1) using symbolic reasoning as a fallback when BNN confidence is low, and (2) refining output classes based on symbolic constraints before reapplying the BNN. We evaluate both strategies on real-world parking data under full, sparse, and noisy conditions. Results demonstrate that both hybrid methods outperform symbolic reasoning alone, and the context-refinement strategy consistently exceeds the performance of Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks and BNN baselines across all prediction windows. Our findings highlight the potential of modular neuro-symbolic integration in real-world, uncertainty-prone prediction tasks.

</details>


### [147] [Semantic Interaction Information mediates compositional generalization in latent space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27134)
*John Schwarcz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出「Factorization Regret（因子化遗憾，摘要旧版称 Semantic Interaction Information）」这一信息论度量，量化潜变量交互（Interaction Information）对任务性能的实际贡献；并构建最小化的「Cognitive Gridworld」（一个观测由多个带参数化交互的潜变量联合生成的平稳 POMDP，仅对单一目标变量提供反馈）作为理论可控的实验床。核心结论是：忽略潜变量交互的"朴素贝叶斯"是学习交互前的性能上限，全训练 RNN 达到联合贝叶斯最优，而 Echo State Network 恰好停滞在朴素贝叶斯水平；进一步用 JEPA 风格的 Representation Classification Chains（RCC）从自然化奖励中隐式学到变量交互与组合嵌入，从而对未见变量组合实现组合泛化与零样本控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题：即使所有与任务相关的潜变量都已知，泛化是否仍存在壁垒？作者认为障碍在于「如何把变量组合起来」——Interaction Information（Watanabe, 1960）在纯数学上度量三个及以上随机变量的联合信息，但其与生存相关的「语义信息」（Kolchinsky & Wolpert, 2018）之间的联系尚未建立：交互信息只有在影响目标/最优行动时才具语义价值。方法论上，现有基准（Alchemy、CompoSuite、ARC-AGI）的任务结构要么显式给出、要么需靠操纵世界（因果干预）来发现，无法把「学习交互」这一难度从「组合性」中剥离出来；而元学习任务通常定义在固定但未知的世界中，缺乏理论可分析性。此外，现有 reservoir computing 文献只观测到 ESN 表现次优，却缺乏对其原因的可计算解释。

Method: （1）环境：Cognitive Gridworld，平稳 POMDP。C 个相关潜变量各取 R 种实现（i.i.d. 均匀），观测 o_t 的每一维由参数化交互 Z 决定的似然 ℓ_{z^i}(r) 独立生成；交互 z^i_{cc'} = (k_c^i)^T q_{c'}^i（组合嵌入，Gram-Schmidt 正交化），经「压缩→扩张」构造：标量交互经正弦基相位平移得到向量 v^i_{cc'}，外积并广播到 C 维似然张量 ℓ ∈ [0,1]^{R^C}，sigmoid 归一化。每次 episode 随机选一个目标变量 g，智能体需推断 r_g 并选 a* = r_g。（2）度量：Factorization Regret_t = D_KL(B^Joint_{tg} ‖ B^Naive_{tg})，即联合贝叶斯观察者与朴素（边缘化似然）观察者对目标变量边缘信念的 KL 散度；理论上等于条件总相关 TC(o_{1:T}|r_g) 减去一个 KL 偏移，且 TC = T×(I(o;r_c) − I(o;r_c;r_g)) − I(o_{1:T};r_c|r_g)，因 r_g、r_c 先验独立故 I(o;r_c;r_g)≤0，TC 随时间线性增长。（3）实验一：Classifier（LSTM，输入 [o_t, Z]，输出 C×R 边缘 logit 增量，经累积+softmax 得信念状态）以对称 KL 损失对齐联合贝叶斯目标信念，α=0.5；对比 Fully Trained RNN 与 ESN（循环权重冻结）。附加分析包括 confidence–accuracy 校准、PCA 表征纠缠分析、以 Jeffreys 散度定义的 Entanglement 指标及 Participation Ratio 有效维度分析。（4）实验二：把 Z 变为待学参数（500 个潜变量，10% 为 testing 变量，其信念永不接收梯度）。RCC 含两个不共享梯度的模块：Classifier 由稀疏二值奖励（δ(a−a*)）以策略梯度式交叉熵训练；Generator 以估计实现 r̂（从 B_T 采样）与估计交互 Ẑ 为输入，用轨迹经验分布与自生成预测间的 Jeffreys 散度做自监督学习，梯度经 Ẑ 回传至组合嵌入；辅以可学习投影 f_K/f_Q、嵌入正则化、soft clip、奖励加权的生成器损失。（5）实验三：智能体控制潜变量本身，以偏好观测 Ω 定义内在奖励 R(r,Ω,ℓ) 形成「偏好地景」，用 A2C（Actor-Critic）在想象中训练控制器，比较 Online / Offline w/ Generator / Offline w/ Joint Bayes（oracle）三种条件。

Result: （1）FR 随交互变量数 C 与推断时长 T 单调增长，Joint 与 Naive 观察者的相对精度差随交互迅速拉开；C=1 时 FR=0，两者一致。（2）Fully Trained RNN 达到联合贝叶斯最优，ESN 精确停滞在朴素贝叶斯水平；当 C=1（FR=0）时两者差距消失；参数匹配、ESN 更新联合信念等对照下结论不变。FR 成为性能差距的定量解释，且网络间相对精度比等于两个贝叶斯观察者之比。（3）失败模式：Naive Bayes 与 ESN 出现「幻觉式」置信—准确解耦——对正确行动赋予的信念分布出现低于随机的第二峰，即错误解释使证据变得有害；随 C 增大过度自信加剧（accuracy/confidence 随 FR 单调下降）。机制上，PCA 显示单步观测后表征仍因子化，但 T=30 后 ESN/Naive 的边缘信念表征深度纠缠（无法区分 r_c 与 r_c'），而 RNN 与联合贝叶斯保持因子化；Entanglement 与 RNN 群体向量范数、跨成分分布的有效维度扩张相关，提示动态调节维度以维持解纠缠是可行机制。（4）RCC 能从自然化反馈学到组合嵌入并迁移到 testing 变量，但存在临界规模：|S| 太小（<500）时只学会训练变量，|S| 增至足够大时训练变慢后自发恢复并泛化；消融 Generator、让梯度仅从 Classifier 回传会显著损害学习稳定性。（5）学得交互后，用学得的生成模型在想象中离线训练控制器即可实现零样本控制：Offline w/ Generator 与使用真实似然的 oracle（Joint Bayes）表现无显著差异，在线交互训练并不优于离线。

Conclusion: 贡献是一个最小、理论有据的「有意义智能」评估框架：FR 作为环境固有属性可量化任一基准中「学习交互」所占难度比例；Naive Bayes 给出「有意义智能」的 principled 阈值——跨过它意味着智能体超越刺激绑定策略，仅使用刺激绑定策略者即 ESN 类；RCC 证明了组合潜结构可从自然化反馈中被学习，而交互一旦被学到，想象经验足以支撑零样本控制。作者亦提出神经学假说：HPC 巩固组合嵌入、OFC 负责检测与管理 Factorization Regret，二者协作构成零样本控制的全局认知地图。局限：观测的时空丰富性被完全抽象掉；RCC 仅在 C=2 小环境评估，可扩展性未知；最关键的限制是假设所有任务相关变量已知（需如 VASE 的潜变量发现算法）；未来方向包括用离散傅里叶级数构造更丰富的似然、对嵌入施加稀疏性、引入 Transformer 等序列模型。

Abstract: Are there still barriers to generalization once all relevant variables are known? We address this question via a framework that casts compositional generalization as a variational inference problem over latent variables with parametric interactions. To explore this, we develop the Cognitive Gridworld, a stationary Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) where observations are generated jointly by multiple latent variables, yet feedback is provided for only a single goal variable. This setting allows us to define Semantic Interaction Information (SII): a metric measuring the contribution of latent variable interactions to task performance. Using SII, we analyze Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) provided with these interactions, finding that SII explains the accuracy gap between Echo State and Fully Trained networks. Our analysis also uncovers a theoretically predicted failure mode where confidence decouples from accuracy, suggesting that utilizing interactions between relevant variables is a non-trivial capability.
  We then address a harder regime where the interactions must be learned by an embedding model. Learning how latent variables interact requires accurate inference, yet accurate inference depends on knowing those interactions. The Cognitive Gridworld reveals this circular dependence as a core challenge for continual meta-learning. We approach this dilemma via Representation Classification Chains (RCCs), a JEPA-style architecture that disentangles these processes: variable inference and variable embeddings are learned by separate modules through Reinforcement Learning and self-supervised learning, respectively. Lastly, we demonstrate that RCCs facilitate compositional generalization to novel combinations of relevant variables. Together, these results establish a grounded setting for evaluating goal-directed generalist agents.

</details>


### [148] [Spectral-Aware Text-to-Time Series Generation with Billion-Scale Multimodal Meteorological Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27135)
*Shijie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个文本引导的气象时间序列生成统一框架，包含一个十亿级气象数据集MeteoCap-3B和一个基于扩散的生成模型MTransformer，通过频谱提示生成器实现语义控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本到时间序列生成方法面临两大挑战：(1) 缺乏大规模、物理一致的多模态气象数据集；(2) 现有架构忽视了天气信号的光谱-时间结构。这些限制阻碍了利用自然语言直观控制复杂多尺度大气动态的能力。

Method: 研究团队构建了MeteoCap-3B数据集，通过多智能体协作描述（MACC）管道生成专家级标注；同时提出MTransformer扩散模型，通过频谱提示生成器将文本描述映射为多频段频谱先验，再通过频率感知注意力机制引导生成过程。

Result: 实验结果表明：该方法在真实基准上达到最优生成质量，实现精准的跨模态对齐和强语义可控性；在数据稀缺和零样本设置下，下游预报任务有显著提升；且可泛化至通用时间序列基准。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了首个统一的文本引导气象时间序列生成框架，验证了基于频谱结构的扩散模型在气象领域及通用时间序列任务中的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Text-to-time-series generation is particularly important in meteorology, where natural language offers intuitive control over complex, multi-scale atmospheric dynamics. Existing approaches are constrained by the lack of large-scale, physically grounded multimodal datasets and by architectures that overlook the spectral-temporal structure of weather signals. We address these challenges with a unified framework for text-guided meteorological time-series generation. First, we introduce MeteoCap-3B, a billion-scale weather dataset paired with expert-level captions constructed via a Multi-agent Collaborative Captioning (MACC) pipeline, yielding information-dense and physically consistent annotations. Building on this dataset, we propose MTransformer, a diffusion-based model that enables precise semantic control by mapping textual descriptions into multi-band spectral priors through a Spectral Prompt Generator, which guides generation via frequency-aware attention. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art generation quality, accurate cross-modal alignment, strong semantic controllability, and substantial gains in downstream forecasting under data-sparse and zero-shot settings. Additional results on general time-series benchmarks indicate that the proposed framework generalizes beyond meteorology.

</details>


### [149] [ScoutAttention: Efficient KV Cache Offloading via Layer-Ahead CPU Pre-computation for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27138)
*Qiuyang Zhang,Kai Zhou,Ding Tang,Kai Lu,Cheng Li,Zhenyu Yang,Peng Xu,Jiguang Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ScoutAttention通过GPU-CPU协作计算和层提前预计算策略解决LLM长上下文推理中KV cache显存不足的问题，在精度损失仅2.4%的情况下实现比现有offloading方法快2.1倍的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在长上下文推理时面临严峻的GPU显存容量限制，KV cache的内存消耗严重制约了decode批处理大小。现有的KV cache卸载到DRAM的方案存在频繁GPU-CPU数据传输或大量CPU计算的问题，导致系统因I/O操作或CPU处理等待而产生较差的GPU利用率。

Method: 提出GPU-CPU协作的块级稀疏注意力机制，显著降低CPU负载；设计层提前(.layer-ahead)CPU预计算算法，使CPU能够提前一层开始注意力计算；配合异步周期性召回(asynchronous periodic recall)机制维持最小CPU计算负载。

Result: ScoutAttention在保持基线精度2.4%以内的同时，相比现有offloading方法实现2.1倍加速。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了长上下文LLM推理中的显存瓶颈问题，通过GPU-CPU协同计算和预计算策略平衡了计算资源利用与内存压力，为大模型高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models encounter critical GPU memory capacity constraints during long-context inference, where KV cache memory consumption severely limits decode batch sizes. While existing research has explored offloading KV cache to DRAM, these approaches either demand frequent GPU-CPU data transfers or impose extensive CPU computation requirements, resulting in poor GPU utilization as the system waits for I/O operations or CPU processing to complete.
  We propose ScoutAttention, a novel KV cache offloading framework that accelerates LLM inference through collaborative GPU-CPU attention computation. To prevent CPU computation from bottlenecking the system, ScoutAttention introduces GPU-CPU collaborative block-wise sparse attention that significantly reduces CPU load. Unlike conventional parallel computing approaches, our framework features a novel layer-ahead CPU pre-computation algorithm, enabling the CPU to initiate attention computation one layer in advance, complemented by asynchronous periodic recall mechanisms to maintain minimal CPU compute load. Experimental results demonstrate that ScoutAttention maintains accuracy within 2.4% of baseline while achieving 2.1x speedup compared to existing offloading methods.

</details>


### [150] [Preconditioned Attention: Enhancing Efficiency in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27153)
*Hemanth Saratchandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Transformer中标准注意力机制产生的病态矩阵问题，提出了一种名为"预条件注意力"的新方法，通过在每个注意力头中引入条件矩阵来改善矩阵条件数，从而提升基于梯度的优化器的训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制在Transformer中常产生条件数较大的病态矩阵，这是基于梯度的优化器面临的主要障碍，导致训练效率低下。

Method: 在每个注意力头中引入条件矩阵（preconditioning matrix），形成预条件注意力机制。该方法可作为多种注意力机制的即插即用替代方案。

Result: 理论分析表明该方法显著降低了注意力矩阵的条件数，产生了更好条件的矩阵，从而改善了优化效果。在图像分类、目标检测、实例分割、长序列建模和语言建模等多种Transformer应用中得到验证。

Conclusion: 预条件注意力作为一种简单有效的改进方案，可广泛应用于各种注意力机制，并在一系列Transformer任务中展现有效性。

Abstract: Central to the success of Transformers is the attention block, which effectively models global dependencies among input tokens associated to a dataset. However, we theoretically demonstrate that standard attention mechanisms in transformers often produce ill-conditioned matrices with large condition numbers. This ill-conditioning is a well-known obstacle for gradient-based optimizers, leading to inefficient training. To address this issue, we introduce preconditioned attention, a novel approach that incorporates a conditioning matrix into each attention head. Our theoretical analysis shows that this method significantly reduces the condition number of attention matrices, resulting in better-conditioned matrices that improve optimization. Conditioned attention serves as a simple drop-in replacement for a wide variety of attention mechanisms in the literature. We validate the effectiveness of preconditioned attention across a diverse set of transformer applications, including image classification, object detection, instance segmentation, long sequence modeling and language modeling.

</details>


### [151] [A Tight Expressivity Hierarchy for GNN-Based Entity Resolution in Master Data Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27154)
*Ashwin Ganesan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在"带类型的实体-属性图"上为基于 GNN 的实体解析建立了一套紧的表达能力分离理论：提出共指谓词 Dup_r（两同类型实体是否共享至少 r 个属性值）与 ℓ-环参与谓词 Cyc_ℓ，用四个定理证明每个任务所需的"最便宜"MPNN 架构与最优深度。核心结论是"最小架构原则"：Dup_1 只需反向消息传递（2 层，O(|E|)）；Dup_r (r≥2) 额外强制需要 ego IDs（4 层，O(|V|·|E|)），因为需要"跨属性身份关联"这一本质非局部的操作；环检测则需 ego IDs（ℓ 层，仅在标准分离实例上成立）。计算实验在 100 组随机初始化下验证了必要性（嵌入 ℓ2 距离恒为 0）与充分性（构造式 MPNN 输出正确）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实体解析（判断多条数据库记录是否指向同一真实实体）是数据集成、知识图谱构建与主数据管理（MDM）的核心任务，商用数据库动辄上亿条带类型的记录（姓名、地址、电话、邮箱、税号、股权关系），匹配错误会级联到客户分析、监管报告与欺诈检测。把所有可用架构增强（反向消息传递、端口编号、ego IDs）全上，会带来不必要的开销：ego IDs 使每层代价从 O(|E|) 升到 O(|V|·|E|)，端口编号需 O(|E| log|E|) 预计算。Egressy 等人只证明了"三者齐备可检测任意有向子图模式"的普遍性结论，既不区分哪些增强对具体任务是必要的，也不给出所需深度的下界。因此核心问题是：给定匹配准则，最便宜且可证明可行的 MPNN 架构是什么？作者同时论证了 GNN 建模实体解析的合理性：真实属性值含噪（"John Smith" vs "J. Smith"），哈希连接会漏配；而精确谓词 Dup_r 是软匹配函数在无噪极限下的特例，故表达力下界对任何合理软化推广都是 a fortiori 成立——不可表示的架构无论怎么训练都学不会。

Method: 形式化：(1) 定义带类型有向多重图上的"带类型实体-属性图"（节点类型分解为实体类型与属性类型，边由实体指向属性），以及二元节点分类任务 Dup_r(u)=1 当且仅当存在同类型实体 v 与 u 共享至少 r 个带类型邻居 N_typed(u)∩N_typed(v)；以及 Cyc_ℓ(v)=1 当且仅当 v 属于长度恰为 ℓ 的有向简单圈（允许边类型异质）。(2) MPNN 定义沿用 Hamilton 框架（仅沿入边聚合的多重集聚合 + UPDATE + READOUT），参照 Xu 等的 1-WL 上界与可注入和聚合引理（Lemma 5）；三种增强：Adaptation 1 反向消息传递（额外聚合出邻居）、Adaptation 2 有向多重图端口编号（区分入端口 2a/出端口 2b，性质 i–iii，作为对抗性资源：充分性须对所有合法编号成立，不充分性只需给出反例编号）、Adaptation 3 ego IDs（对指定中心节点加二元指示特征，需每节点跑一次前向）。(3) 证明策略为"必要+充分"双结构：必要性通过构造具体分离图对 G1/G2，并给出对任意深度/宽度/任意参数都成立的不可区分性归纳论证；充分性通过显式构造指定深度的 MPNN（消息内容、节点存储量、可实现性证明），逐层验证语义正确性。(4) 计算验证：对必要性，在 d=32 隐藏维、Kaiming uniform 初始化下采样 100 组权重，计算目标节点嵌入的 ℓ2 距离（无训练，单次前向）；对充分性，直接实现证明中的确定性聚合/读出逻辑并检查输出标签。

Result: 定理3（简单图 K_{2,1}）：反向消息传递对 Dup_1 既必要又充分，最优深度 2。必要性：实体节点 N_in(u)=∅，无反向传递时 h^(K)(u) 只依赖初始特征与架构常数，故分离图对（两实体共享属性 vs 单实体）不可区分；充分性：第1层前向让属性节点获得其 fan-in 剖面（各实体类型×边类型的多重集），第2层反向让实体据此判断是否存在同类型竞争者共享任一属性。定理5（多重图 K_{2,1}）：另需入端口编号（出端口不必要），深度仍为 2；原因是平行边会虚增属性节点的多重集计数，入端口（平行边共享端口、不同源不同端口）恰好恢复"不同源实体数"的计数能力。定理8（主结果，r≥2）：即使已有反向传递与完整端口编号，ego IDs 仍对 Dup_r 必要——存在 4 个实体、2r 属性、4r 边的简单无环二分图对，G1 中 u 与 v 共享全部 r 个属性，Dup_r(u)=1；G2 把 u 的 r 个属性"劈开"给 v1、v2（各 ≤ r−1），Dup_r(u)=0；两者度数-类型剖面完全相同，且可赋予合法端口编号使实体分成 class-1/class-2 两类且两类划分在两图中完全相同，从而任意深度下 u 的嵌入逐层相同。充分性：4 层 ego+反向传递 MPNN 足够且深度最优（3 层即使加 ego IDs 也不可区分），逐层计算 EgoTypes(a) → ov(v)=|N_typed(u)∩N_typed(v)| → maxOv(a) → 读出 [max maxOv ≥ r]，且该构造完全不需要端口编号（简单性保证每实体对每属性至多一条同类型边）。定理12（ℓ-环）：ego IDs 必要（即使有反向传递与完整端口编号），因为 G1=两个不相交的 ℓ-环与 G2=单个 2ℓ-环在端口编号下仍完全对称；ego IDs 单独（仅前向传播，深度 ℓ）在该分离对上的充分性成立，理由是闭合边本身把 ego 信号送回起点。实验验证：所有不充分架构在 100 组随机初始化、多个深度下 ℓ2 距离恒为 0.00；所有充分构造在分离图上给出正确标签（Thm8 覆盖 r=2,3,4，Thm12 覆盖 ℓ=3,5,7）。

Conclusion: 综合四个定理得到"最小架构原则"（Table 4）：每个实体解析任务在给定图类上都有唯一最便宜且充分的增强组合与最优深度，且可证明任何更廉价的替代方案均失败——K_{2,1}/简单图：反向传递、深度2；K_{2,1}/多重图：反向传递+入端口、深度2；K_{2,r}(r≥2)/简单图：反向传递+ego IDs、深度4（端口编号不必要）；K_{2,r}/多重图：反向传递+入端口+ego IDs、深度4；ℓ-环（标准分离对）：仅 ego IDs、深度ℓ。理论要点是：Dup_1 是纯局部计算（每个属性独立判定是否有两个同类型前驱），Dup_r 需要"跨属性身份关联"（验证同一实体同时出现在目标的多个属性处），这是本质非局部需求；ego IDs 带来的 O(|V|) 因子是任何 MPNN 方案无法规避的固有代价，而非特定架构的产物。另一个结构性对照是二分图与一般有向图的差异：实体-属性图所有边指向属性、无入邻居，故反向传递无条件必要；而环检测可借闭合边前向回流，故反向传递与端口编号可省。方法论上，必要性为对所有参数的普遍命题（随机采样提供经验证据，零距离印证其结构性），充分性为存在性命题（须给出见证权重，随机初始化下深层构造不分离属预期而非矛盾）。局限与未来工作：充分性构造依赖精确可注入聚合，有限精度与学习到的近似聚合的影响待查；环检测充分性仅在分离对上成立，所构造 MPNN 检测的是长度 ℓ 的闭合游走而非简单圈（一般图上二者分歧，如 3-环在 ℓ=6 时产生假阳性），通用的 Cyc_ℓ 计算仍需三种增强齐备（Egressy 等 Cor 4.4.1），反向传递与端口编号是否各自独立必要尚待证明；仅处理二元精确谓词，扩展到软匹配分数、属性扰动、高阶 K_{n,r}(n≥3) 及大规模基准验证均属开放方向。此外作者声明全流程采用 AI 辅助研究工作流（与 Claude Opus 4.6 迭代完成证明与实验）。

Abstract: Entity resolution -- identifying database records that refer to the same real-world entity -- is naturally modelled on bipartite graphs connecting entity nodes to their attribute values. Applying a message-passing neural network (MPNN) with all available extensions (reverse message passing, port numbering, ego IDs) incurs unnecessary overhead, since different entity resolution tasks have fundamentally different complexity. For a given matching criterion, what is the cheapest MPNN architecture that provably works?
  We answer this with a four-theorem separation theory on typed entity-attribute graphs. We introduce co-reference predicates $\mathrm{Dup}_r$ (two same-type entities share at least $r$ attribute values) and the $\ell$-cycle predicate $\mathrm{Cyc}_\ell$ for settings with entity-entity edges. For each predicate we prove tight bounds -- constructing graph pairs provably indistinguishable by every MPNN lacking the required adaptation, and exhibiting explicit minimal-depth MPNNs that compute the predicate on all inputs.
  The central finding is a sharp complexity gap between detecting any shared attribute and detecting multiple shared attributes. The former is purely local, requiring only reverse message passing in two layers. The latter demands cross-attribute identity correlation -- verifying that the same entity appears at several attributes of the target -- a fundamentally non-local requirement needing ego IDs and four layers, even on acyclic bipartite graphs. A similar necessity holds for cycle detection. Together, these results yield a minimal-architecture principle: practitioners can select the cheapest sufficient adaptation set, with a guarantee that no simpler architecture works. Computational validation confirms every prediction.

</details>


### [152] [GSR-GNN: Training Acceleration and Memory-Saving Framework of Deep GNNs on Circuit Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27156)
*Yuebo Luo,Shiyang Li,Yifei Feng,Vishal Kancharla,Shaoyi Huang,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向大规模电路图（EDA 拥塞预测任务）的深度 GNN 训练框架 GSR-GNN（Grouped-Sparse-Reversible GNN）。通过将 RevGNN 的"分组可逆残差"结构与"组稀疏非线性算子（GS-Op）"结合，并重构内存管理与前/反向执行流水线，在 CircuitNet 采样图上相对 RevGNN 实现最高 87.22% 的 GPU 显存节省、超过 35× 的端到端训练加速，同时 Pearson/Spearman/Kendall/R² 相关性指标基本不变（差异分别为 +0.0026/+0.0067/+0.0053/−0.023）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1）电路图规模持续膨胀（节点可达数万、边数千万级、节点度呈双峰分布），传统 3–4 层浅层 GNN 已达性能上限；而实测表明可训练的深 RevGNN（80 层 Pearson 0.4277 vs. 2 层 0.4096，GCN/SAGE 最优低于 0.3731 甚至为负）显著优于浅层模型，因此"深度不可行"的假设被推翻，问题转化为"如何高效训练深度模型"。2）RevGNN 虽在理论上把显存复杂度从 O(LND) 降到 O(ND)，但其基于 chunk 的 create-then-destroy 内存管理带来严重工程瓶颈：预留 17910 MB 而仅 9555 MB 活跃（约 50% 浪费）、单图 250 epoch 需 5.10 小时、100 层/384 通道在 10 张图上需约 46.4 GPU 小时与 30+ GB 显存，而 CircuitNet 全量有 10,370 张图。3）时间剖析显示可逆反向传播占 epoch 时间 71.6%（20.02 s），前向 20.9%（5.84 s），cudaMemcpy 7.6%（2.13 s），反向/前向比 3.43，导致 L>50 的深层模型训练时间额外放大 10¹–10² 倍，不适合需要快速迭代的 EDA 场景。4）同时 RevGNN 在约 70 层后四项相关性指标全部退化，说明需要一套针对领域定制的深度 GNN 训练框架。

Method: 框架层面：保留可逆残差分组结构以支持数百层训练，但重构层内数据流与内存管理，删除前向/反向中反复的 detach-copy-delete（storage.resize(0) 再 malloc）循环，改为"选择性 save-for-backward"，仅保存稀疏索引；执行流水线优化消除碎片化激活存储与冗余数据搬运，显存复杂度为 O(LNK)（K 为每节点保留元素数），但在实用深度范围内实际占用远低于 RevGNN。

算子层面：提出 Group Sparse 算子 GS(X,k)→(V,I)，作为 ReLU 的稀疏替代——每个节点仅保留 Top-k 显著特征值 V∈R^{N×k} 与其位置索引 I∈Z^{N×k}（CBSR 紧凑格式），前向消息传递只访问被激活的稀疏位置，反向则复用前向保存的索引 I 定位对应梯度，从而在不损失任务相关信息的前提下压缩节点嵌入。

块结构层面：设计 GSR-GNN Forward/Backward Block，内部携带相对稀疏消息传递；每层将输入按通道二分为两组，Group1 经 GS-Op→GSR-Forward→与 Group2 残差相加→Group2 再经 GS-Op→GSR-Forward→scatter 回填 Group1，最后 Concat（算法1）；反向（算法2）将梯度 Split 为两组，Group2 经 GS-Op 后用 GSR-Forward 重算得到 M1（再 GS-Op 得 V1/I1，GSRBlock 得 Z1），通过 M2=V2.scatter(I2)−Z1 恢复对应梯度，再分别经 GSR-Block-Backward 传回，实现"部分可逆 + 稀疏索引共享"的梯度恢复。

实验层面：在 AMD EPYC 7763 + 单张 NVIDIA A6000-48GB（CUDA 12.6、PyTorch 2.1.2、PyG 2.5.0）上，从 CircuitNet 随机采样 10 张代表性电路图（Table 1，含 FPU/RISC-Y 两类），不做子图划分，以拥塞预测为任务；对比 GCN、GraphSAGE 与官方 RevGNN；隐藏维度 256/384，层数 10–100（步长 10），GS-Op 的 K∈{4,8,16,32,64}，训练 250 epoch，评估 Pearson/Spearman/Kendall/R²。

Result: 精度：GSR-GNN 与 RevGNN 相比平均差异为 Pearson +0.0026、Spearman +0.0067、Kendall +0.0053、R² −0.023，即稀疏化几乎无损；GCN/SAGE 随深度增加剧烈波动甚至 OOM。速度：端到端加速随稀疏度反向相关——K=4、384 通道在 5324-RISCY-FPU-a-3 上超过 35×，K=64 在 6859-RISCY-FPU-b-3 上仍超过 4×；100 层/384 通道单图计时（Table 2）由基线 28.06 s（前向 5.84 s、反向 20.09 s、MemCpy 2.13 s）降至 K=4 的 1.52 s（0.36/1.16/0.001 s，18.4×），K=64 时最差仍有 9.1×，且 cudaMemcpy 时间几乎被消除（2.13 s → 0.001 s）。显存：最高节省 87.22%，活跃显存占预留显存比例达 98.6%（RevGNN 约 50% 为浪费）；在 876-R 与 5324-R 上平均分别少占约 2.58 GB 与 5.043 GB，100 层时仍低于 RevGNN；层数少于 30 时可在小于 8 GB 显存的 GPU 上训练。此外观察到电路图的节点度分布与结构模式与 Barabási–Albert 等通用图显著不同，且深 RevGNN 约 70 层后收益饱和并退化。

Conclusion: GSR-GNN 通过"可逆分组残差 + 组稀疏非线性算子 + 优化内存管理/执行流水线"的组合，使超深（数百层）GNN 在大型电路上可训练：显存占用大幅下降、训练提速一个数量级以上（最高 35×/30× 以上），且拥塞预测的相关性质量基本保持不变。结合深度收益在约 70–100 层趋于饱和的观察，文中建议通过"增加层数 + 降低稀疏度（减小 K）"的组合在显存、速度与精度之间取得平衡，从而让深度 GNN 真正适用于大规模 EDA 工作负载。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) show strong promise for circuit analysis, but scaling to modern large-scale circuit graphs is limited by GPU memory and training cost, especially for deep models. We revisit deep GNNs for circuit graphs and show that, when trainable, they significantly outperform shallow architectures, motivating an efficient, domain-specific training framework. We propose Grouped-Sparse-Reversible GNN (GSR-GNN), which enables training GNNs with up to hundreds of layers while reducing both compute and memory overhead. GSR-GNN integrates reversible residual modules with a group-wise sparse nonlinear operator that compresses node embeddings without sacrificing task-relevant information, and employs an optimized execution pipeline to eliminate fragmented activation storage and reduce data movement. On sampled circuit graphs, GSR-GNN achieves up to 87.2\% peak memory reduction and over 30$\times$ training speedup with negligible degradation in correlation-based quality metrics, making deep GNNs practical for large-scale EDA workloads.

</details>


### [153] [Online Learning of Kalman Filtering: From Output to State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27159)
*Lintao Ye,Ankang Zhang,Ming Chi,Bin Du,Jianghai Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了在部分观测线性动态系统中学习卡尔曼滤波的问题，提出了基于在线优化的统一算法框架。输出估计场景可达到对数遗憾界，状态估计场景在有查询限制时可达平方根遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡尔曼滤波在系统模型未知情况下的学习问题尚未得到充分解决，特别是在状态估计场景下存在理论困难。现有方法难以处理部分观测和未知系统模型的情况。

Method: 提出基于在线优化的统一算法框架，利用估计误差函数的条件强凸性质；为解决状态估计难题，引入随机查询机制使算法获得更优的系统状态信息。

Result: 输出估计场景达到O(log T)遗憾界；证明了状态估计场景中任何算法都无法达到T的次线性遗憾，但通过有限查询访问可达到O(√T)遗憾界。

Conclusion: 算法和遗憾界捕获了查询次数与遗憾之间的权衡，为有限观测条件下的在线学习问题提供了理论见解，数值实验验证了算法性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the problem of learning Kalman filtering with unknown system model in partially observed linear dynamical systems. We propose a unified algorithmic framework based on online optimization that can be used to solve both the output estimation and state estimation scenarios. By exploring the properties of the estimation error cost functions, such as conditionally strong convexity, we show that our algorithm achieves a $\log T$-regret in the horizon length $T$ for the output estimation scenario. More importantly, we tackle the more challenging scenario of learning Kalman filtering for state estimation, which is an open problem in the literature. We first characterize a fundamental limitation of the problem, demonstrating the impossibility of any algorithm to achieve sublinear regret in $T$. By further introducing a random query scheme into our algorithm, we show that a $\sqrt{T}$-regret is achievable when rendering the algorithm limited query access to more informative measurements of the system state in practice. Our algorithm and regret readily capture the trade-off between the number of queries and the achieved regret, and shed light on online learning problems with limited observations. We validate the performance of our algorithms using numerical examples.

</details>


### [154] [Hybrid Deep Learning with Temporal Data Augmentation for Accurate Remaining Useful Life Prediction of Lithium-Ion Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27186)
*Yun Tian,Guili Wang,Jian Bi,Kaixin Han,Chenglu Wu,Zhiyi Lu,Chenhao Li,Liangwang Sun,Minyu Zhou,Chenchen Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出混合深度学习模型 CDFormer（1D-CNN + 深度残差收缩网络 DRSN + Transformer 编码器），用于锂离子电池剩余寿命（RUL）预测，并提出包含高斯噪声、时间扭曲（time warping）、时间重采样（time resampling）的复合时序数据增强策略。在 NASA PCoE 与 CALCE 两个真实数据集上以留一交叉验证（LOOCV）评测，CDFormer 在 RMSE、MAE、RE 上整体优于 RNN/LSTM/GRU、DeTransformer、SA-LSTM、AttMoE 及自建变体（Baseline-FC、CNN-FC、CNN-Transformer）；相对最优基线 AttMoE 平均 RMSE/MAE/RE 降低约 24.6%/30.4%/25.9%。消融实验表明三种增强在数据量小、噪声大的 NASA 数据集上收益显著（组合策略 RMSE 降低 44.7%、MAE 降低 48.6%、RE 降低 43.9%），而在样本充足的 CALCE 上增益有限，甚至个别单增强使误差略升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂离子电池 RUL 预测是电池健康监测与数据驱动维护的核心，但现有方法存在三重局限：一是多尺度时序特征建模能力不足，难以同时刻画短时波动与长期退化趋势；二是多依赖单一变量输入（仅容量或电压），未充分利用电压、电流、温度等多传感信息；三是真实工况下噪声、负载波动与不规则充放电使模型鲁棒性与泛化性差。RNN/LSTM/GRU 受梯度消失/爆炸与长序列建模限制，DeTransformer 擅长全局但弱于局部细节，SA-LSTM 与 AttMoE（混合专家）结构复杂、调参代价高。此外锂电池公开数据集（NASA/CALCE）可用老化样本稀缺，小数据集极易过拟合，因此需要显式模拟传感器噪声与时序变异来扩充训练分布。

Method: 方法部分分两大块。（1）CDFormer 架构：输入为三维张量 X∈R^{N×C_in×L_in}；1D-CNN 通过沿时间轴滑动的卷积核提取局部时序模式（式1）；DRSN 模块由两层 Conv+BN+ReLU、自适应软阈值收缩与残差连接组成，阈值 λ 由全局平均池化 + 两层 FC（ReLU/Sigmoid）生成的阈值生成器给出，λ∈[0,1] 按通道自适应后沿时间维广播，实现数据驱动的去噪，通道数不匹配时用 1×1 卷积 + BN 短接（式2–10）；Transformer 编码器以多头自注意力 + 残差 + 层归一化 + 位置前馈网络建模长程依赖（式11–14）；回归头取最后时间步隐状态 z=h_T，经两层线性 + ReLU 输出标量容量预测 ĉ_t，NASA 数据集因噪声大在输出端加 ReLU 约束以避免负容量，CALCE 则不加以保留灵活性；EOL 判据为预测容量首次低于额定容量 70%。（2）复合时序数据增强：时间扭曲——对时间索引施加 U(−αΔt, αΔt) 扰动后线性插值生成新序列（式16–19）；时间重采样——按比例 ρ 随机抽取 ⌊ρT⌋ 个时间点后插值回原长度（式20）；高斯噪声——逐点加 N(0, σ²) 噪声（式21）。实验采用 LOOCV 划分电池，Huber 损失 + Adam（NASA lr=5e-4、CALCE lr=1e-3，β₁=0.9/β₂=0.999），早停 patience=15，L2=1e-3，batch=32，最大轮数 NASA 200 / CALCE 500；NASA 输入为平均电压/电流/温度/放电容量，CALCE 输入为放电容量、恒流充电时长、SOH；指标为 RMSE、MAE 及基于 EOL 循环数的 RE。

Result: （1）与 SOTA 对比：NASA 上 CDFormer 的 RMSE/MAE/RE 为 0.0671/0.0586/0.2365，相比最优基线 AttMoE 分别降低 20.8%/19.7%/17.0%；相比 DeTransformer 降低 22.2%/21.4%/9.4%。CALCE 上为 0.0179/0.0106/0.0877，相比 AttMoE 的 RMSE、MAE 降低 28.4%、41.1%，RE 相比最优 SA-LSTM 降低 20.1%。GRU 在 CALCE 上反而下降，作者归因于其简化门控难以建模长程退化依赖。（2）与自建变体对比：Baseline-FC（0.1952/0.1822/0.5572）、CNN-FC、CNN-Transformer 均劣于 CDFormer，CDFormer 在两数据集上取得最低 RMSE 与 MAE，但 RE 略高于个别变体（差异小，由更小的绝对误差抵消）。（3）消融（增强策略）：NASA 上组合增强最优（0.0371/0.0301/0.1327），相比无增强 RMSE、MAE、RE 分别下降 44.7%、48.6%、43.9%；各单一增强亦均有改善。CALCE 上增益有限，个别单增强（时间重采样）反而使 RMSE/MAE 略升，组合策略 RE 由 0.0877 降至 0.0817。（4）增强的跨模型迁移：NASA 上 Baseline-FC、CNN-FC、CNN-Transformer 施加增强后 RMSE/MAE/RE 均下降；而在仅用单一容量特征的 RNN、LSTM、GRU、DeTransformer、SA-LSTM、AttMoE 上增强几乎无影响（误差基本持平，仅 RE 偶有微降），说明增益来自多特征架构与增强的协同。（5）定性结果：预测-真实容量曲线更贴合，CDFormer 在 NASA 的 B0005/B0006、CALCE 全部电池的 RMSE/MAE 最优，能追踪突发容量变化。

Conclusion: CDFormer 通过 CNN（局部特征）、DRSN（自适应去噪与残差学习）、Transformer（长程依赖）的协同建模实现了多尺度退化动态的联合刻画，复合时序增强（高斯噪声 + 时间扭曲 + 时间重采样）进一步提升了在噪声与不规则采样下的鲁棒性，在 NASA 与 CALCE 两个真实基准上一致优于 RNN 系、Transformer 系及混合专家类基线，验证了「多特征融合 + 多尺度时序建模 + 抗噪增强」对提升 RUL 预测精度与 EOL 判定可靠性的有效性。局限与展望：CALCE 等大样本数据集上增强收益边际递减且个别单增强可能微增误差，说明增强更适用于小样本/高噪场景；RE 指标并非所有设置最优，模型解释性仍弱；未来将引入多模态传感数据，并探索 CDFormer 与物理模型、数字孪生融合以实现 SOH 与 RUL 的可解释联合估计。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of lithium-ion battery remaining useful life (RUL) is essential for reliable health monitoring and data-driven analysis of battery degradation. However, the robustness and generalization capabilities of existing RUL prediction models are significantly challenged by complex operating conditions and limited data availability. To address these limitations, this study proposes a hybrid deep learning model, CDFormer, which integrates convolutional neural networks, deep residual shrinkage networks, and Transformer encoders extract multiscale temporal features from battery measurement signals, including voltage, current, and capacity. This architecture enables the joint modeling of local and global degradation dynamics, effectively improving the accuracy of RUL prediction.To enhance predictive reliability, a composite temporal data augmentation strategy is proposed, incorporating Gaussian noise, time warping, and time resampling, explicitly accounting for measurement noise and variability. CDFormer is evaluated on two real-world datasets, with experimental results demonstrating its consistent superiority over conventional recurrent neural network-based and Transformer-based baselines across key metrics. By improving the reliability and predictive performance of RUL prediction from measurement data, CDFormer provides accurate and reliable forecasts, supporting effective battery health monitoring and data-driven maintenance strategies.

</details>


### [155] [Omni-Modal Dissonance Benchmark: Systematically Breaking Modality Consensus to Probe Robustness and Calibrated Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27187)
*Zabir Al Nazi,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Md Rizwan Parvez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 OMD-Bench：一个面向全模态（视频-音频-文本）模型的诊断基准，通过构造三模态完全一致的"锚点"输入，再以析因方式系统性地腐蚀各模态（8种组合），从而较少混杂地量化各模态贡献，并评估模型在证据冲突时"校准弃答"（calibrated abstention）的能力。在10个全模态模型上的评测显示：模型严重偏向文本（文本腐蚀掉点最大，音频Shapley值在7/10模型中为负）、弃答双向失准（k=2时过度弃答，k=3时严重漏弃答）、置信度持续偏高（60–100%），CoT虽改善弃答对齐但放大过度自信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态基准（OmniBench、AV-Odyssey、WorldSense、UNO-Bench、MMIR等）依赖自然共现的多模态数据，各模态所携带的信息既相关又不等量，因此观测到的"模态贡献"无法区分是真正的跨模态整合，还是对某一主导模态的依赖。此外，已有"缺失模态"鲁棒性工作研究的是输入缺席，而非模态主动提供冲突信息。论文借鉴Pearl的干预式推理逻辑，主张需要一种受控扰动（controlled perturbation）方法：先构造三模态在信息量上等价地编码同一概念的输入，再逐一替换以获得可解释的归因信号；同时需要在全模态场景下形式化并测量"校准弃答"（证据冲突时是否恰当拒答），这一能力此前仅在纯文本LLM中被研究。

Method: 构建OMD-Bench：以27个"锚点"（可被视频、音频、文本各自独立识别的对象/事件，如dog barking、thunder、clock ticking）作为三模态知识单元，要求满足三模态可识别性（人类单模态识别率视频94.5%/文本94.4%/音频93.1%，跨模态差距≤1.7%）、世界知识可接地性、跨锚点区分性三个标准。每个锚点约9.4个需两步认知（先感知锚点、再检索世界知识）的知识型问题，答案5选4：1个正确答案 + 3个同题异锚点干扰项 + 1个显式弃答选项，选项顺序随机化以抑制位置偏置。析因腐蚀协议：对255个种子问题各生成2^3=8种腐蚀条件（腐蚀向量c∈{0,1}^3，1表示该模态被替换为另一随机锚点的内容），得每split 2,040、共4,080个实例；分为真实split（视频Pexels、音频Freesound/Pixabay、人工撰写文本）与合成split（Sora/Grok/Gemini视频、AudioX音频）。真值由3名标注者按"结构化证据协议"判定（先分别识别每个模态呈现什么，再看哪一选项获得多数模态支持；证据冲突则选弃答），两人标注+第三人仲裁，标注者无需知晓事实答案（通过答案键映射）。评测指标：分条件准确率（k≤1为事实正确性，k≥2为按人类协议的正确应答）、弃答校准误差ACE（各k层模型弃答率与人类目标率之差的均值）、置信度校准（受限softmax RS/词元概率TP、平均预测置信度、ECE、RC-AUC）、模态依赖（归一化依赖度Δ=Acc(C000)-Acc(Cm)/Acc(C000)与Shapley值）。共评测10个模型（VideoLLaMA2、Phi-4、Qwen3o-Instruct/Thinking、MiniCPM-o 2.6、Uni-MoE-2、VITA-1.5、Gemini 2.0/2.5、GPT-4o-mini，后者经两阶段音频转写，故标注†），每模型2 split × 2 提示（零样本/CoT）共4种设置；另做开放式评测（去除选项，GPT-4.1-mini与GPT-5-mini双评审，32,640条判定）、Bootstrap CI、Wilcoxon/Friedman/McNemar/置换检验等统计验证。

Result: 1）模态依赖高度不对称：k=0平均准确率90.0%，k=1平均仅降9pp，但单模态腐蚀下文本腐蚀掉点最大（19.5–22pp），视觉中等（6–7pp），音频近乎为0（≤1pp）；Shapley值中文本占44–64%，音频在7/10模型中为负（仅音频干净C101准确率36.4%，低于全腐蚀C111），说明音频不仅无信息反而误导。2）弃答双向失准：所有模型弃答率随k单调上升但幅度错位——k≤2时人类几乎不弃答而模型常过度弃答，k=3时人类约98%弃答而无模型超过82%（最佳Gemini 2.5的ACE约15.6–16.6%，最差Phi-4/VITA-1.5约23.8%）；VideoLLaMA2与Phi-4几乎从不弃答。3）过度自信：k=2时平均准确率降至约61%而平均置信度仍≥78%，Gemini 2.5从99.7%仅微降至98.5%；CoT下8个模型中6个置信度饱和至近100%，ECE在CoT下恶化（Phi-4达37%）。CoT提升干净准确率（8/10模型，平均−0.8pp ACE改善）却使Gemini 2.0在k=3的弃答从81%跌至51%，即"更笃定而非更谨慎"。4）CoT在13/20模型-split组合中显著提升准确率，Uni-MoE-2在ZS下显著更优。5）开放式评测显示MCQ选项可"搭脚手"：k=0差距约25pp、k=2扩大至约60pp，但模态层级（文本≫视觉>音频）与k=2崩塌结论保持。6）错误分析归纳三类：E1错误事实答案（多因文本被腐蚀而模型顺从错误文本，即便正确识别了干净视频/音频；甚至存在零腐蚀下的知识/感知错误）、E2错误弃答（幻觉化干净模态的内容或知识缺失后拒答）、E3漏弃答（在全/多模态腐蚀下编造自洽叙事而不检测跨模态不一致）。模型极少检查模态间一致性。

Conclusion: OMD-Bench通过"先全模态一致、再析因腐蚀"的受控干预设计，提供了较少混杂的模态依赖归因与全模态校准弃答评测范式。实证表明当前全模态模型的"全模态"名不副实：文本主导、音频甚至有害，模型在证据冲突时既过度弃答又严重漏弃答，并保持近乎满分的自信；CoT只是把"更多正确答案"与"更高置信度"绑定，未解决校准问题。论文由此指出，需要在训练与推理策略层面平衡模态贡献、在冲突输入下校准不确定性，才能弥合"全模态整合承诺"与实际行为之间的差距。局限性包括：锚点仅27个且偏具体（不含抽象/文化特有/感知模糊概念）、腐蚀为整通道替换而非渐变退化（生态效度受限）、MCQ格式使结果应视为能力上界、文本为人工叙事体裁可能自带分布线索（文本优势与语言偏置难以完全解耦）、GPT-4o-mini为转写式且两模型缺token级logits导致置信度提取方法不可直接横向比较、材料仅英文且偏西方文化参考。作者同时声明该基准为诊断工具，不应作为安全关键部署的唯一认证标准，并讨论了双用途（脆弱性披露）问题。

Abstract: Existing omni-modal benchmarks attempt to measure modality-specific contributions, but their measurements are confounded: naturally co-occurring modalities carry correlated yet unequal information, making it unclear whether results reflect true modality reliance or information asymmetry. We introduce OMD-Bench, where all modalities are initially congruent - each presenting the same anchor, an object or event independently perceivable through video, audio, and text - which we then systematically corrupt to isolate each modality's contribution. We also evaluate calibrated abstention: whether models appropriately refrain from answering when evidence is conflicting. The benchmark comprises 4,080 instances spanning 27 anchors across eight corruption conditions. Evaluating ten omni-modal models under zero-shot and chain-of-thought prompting, we find that models over-abstain when two modalities are corrupted yet under-abstain severely when all three are, while maintaining high confidence (~60-100%) even under full corruption. Chain-of-thought prompting improves abstention alignment with human judgment but amplifies overconfidence rather than mitigating it. OMD-Bench provides a diagnostic benchmark for diagnosing modality reliance, robustness to cross-modal inconsistency, and uncertainty calibration in omni-modal systems.

</details>


### [156] [From Inference Routing to Agent Orchestration: Declarative Policy Compilation with Cross-Layer Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27299)
*Huamin Chen,Xunzhuo Liu,Bowei He,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Semantic Router DSL从单请求路由扩展到多步骤Agent工作流，通过非图灵完备的语言设计保证可验证性，从单一声明式源文件编译生成编排框架工件（LangGraph、OpenClaw）、Kubernetes配置（NetworkPolicy、Sandbox CRD、ConfigMap）、YANG/NETCONF载荷及协议边界门控（MCP、A2A）等多种目标，实现从推理网关到Agent编排再到基础设施部署的全链路策略统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决跨团队策略漂移问题：传统方案中推理网关、Agent编排和基础设施配置由不同团队维护，导致策略不一致。现有方案缺乏统一的声明式语言来协调这些层级，且无法保证路由穷尽性和分支无冲突性。

Method: 采用非图灵完备的DSL设计，利用其可判定性实现：1）编译时穷尽路由保证；2）无冲突分支验证；3）引用完整性检查；4）决策逻辑与审计追踪的结构耦合。通过共享信号定义实现单一编译步骤完成全链路传播。

Result: 成功将DSL从单请求路由扩展到多步骤Agent工作流全路径，覆盖推理网关、Agent编排层和基础设施部署层。编译生成多种目标工件并保持语义一致性。

Conclusion: 基于四大支柱（可审计性、成本效率、可验证性、可调优性）构建统一策略体系，在各层明确定义验证边界。非图灵完备设计是实现可判定验证的关键保障，使跨层级策略同步从协调问题变为编译问题。

Abstract: The Semantic Router DSL is a non-Turing-complete policy language deployed in production for per-request LLM inference routing: content signals (embedding similarity, PII detection, jailbreak scoring) feed into weighted projections and priority-ordered decision trees that select a model, enforce privacy policies, and produce structured audit traces -- all from a single declarative source file. Prior work established conflict-free compilation for probabilistic predicates and positioned the DSL within the Workload-Router-Pool inference architecture.
  This paper extends the same language from stateless, per-request routing to multi-step agent workflows -- the full path from inference gateway to agent orchestration to infrastructure deployment. The DSL compiler emits verified decision nodes for orchestration frameworks (LangGraph, OpenClaw), Kubernetes artifacts (NetworkPolicy, Sandbox CRD, ConfigMap), YANG/NETCONF payloads, and protocol-boundary gates (MCP, A2A) -- all from the same source.
  Because the language is non-Turing-complete, the compiler guarantees exhaustive routing, conflict-free branching, referential integrity, and audit traces structurally coupled to the decision logic. Because signal definitions are shared across targets, a threshold change propagates from inference gateway to agent gate to infrastructure artifact in one compilation step -- eliminating cross-team coordination as the primary source of policy drift. We ground the approach in four pillars -- auditability, cost efficiency, verifiability, and tunability -- and identify the verification boundary at each layer.

</details>


### [157] [Scalable Maximum Entropy Population Synthesis via Persistent Contrastive Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27312)
*Mirko Degli Esposti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GibbsPCDSolver方法，利用Persistent Contrastive Divergence（PCD）技术解决最大熵人口合成模型中精确期望计算的组合爆炸问题，实现从聚合人口普查数据生成合成人口样本，在50个分类属性规模下仍能保持0.010-0.018的相对误差，且有效样本量比广义raking方法提升86.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最大熵人口合成方法的核心瓶颈在于精确期望计算需要遍历整个元组空间cX，当分类属性数量K超过20时计算变得不可行，限制了其在高维人口数据上的应用。

Method: 提出GibbsPCDSolver方法，使用N个合成个体的持久池，在每个梯度步骤通过Gibbs扫描进行更新，提供模型期望的随机近似，避免了显式构造cX。该方法将计算复杂度从O(|cX|)降低到O(K)。

Result: 在K∈{12,20,30,40,50}的扩展实验中，GibbsPCDSolver在|cX|增长18个数量级的情况下仍保持MRE∈[0.010,0.018]。在Syn-ISTAT基准（K=15）上，训练约束MRE达到0.03，有效样本量Neff=N，而广义raking方法仅Neff≈0.012N，实现86.8倍的多样性优势。

Conclusion: GibbsPCDSolver成功解决了高维分类属性下最大熵模型的计算瓶颈，可在K=50规模保持精确度，同时产生高质量、多样化的合成人口样本，对基于智能体的城市模拟应用至关重要。

Abstract: Maximum entropy (MaxEnt) modelling provides a principled framework for generating synthetic populations from aggregate census data, without access to individual-level microdata. The bottleneck of existing approaches is exact expectation computation, which requires summing over the full tuple space $\cX$ and becomes infeasible for more than $K \approx 20$ categorical attributes. We propose \emph{GibbsPCDSolver}, a stochastic replacement for this computation based on Persistent Contrastive Divergence (PCD): a persistent pool of $N$ synthetic individuals is updated by Gibbs sweeps at each gradient step, providing a stochastic approximation of the model expectations without ever materialising $\cX$. We validate the approach on controlled benchmarks and on \emph{Syn-ISTAT}, a $K{=}15$ Italian demographic benchmark with analytically exact marginal targets derived from ISTAT-inspired conditional probability tables. Scaling experiments across $K \in \{12, 20, 30, 40, 50\}$ confirm that GibbsPCDSolver maintains $\MRE \in [0.010, 0.018]$ while $|\cX|$ grows eighteen orders of magnitude, with runtime scaling as $O(K)$ rather than $O(|\cX|)$. On Syn-ISTAT, GibbsPCDSolver reaches $\MRE{=}0.03$ on training constraints and -- crucially -- produces populations with effective sample size $\Neff = N$ versus $\Neff \approx 0.012\,N$ for generalised raking, an $86.8{\times}$ diversity advantage that is essential for agent-based urban simulations.

</details>


### [158] [Multimodal Forecasting for Commodity Prices Using Spectrogram-Based and Time Series Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27321)
*Soyeon Park,Doohee Chung,Charmgil Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEMF是一种用于多变量时间序列预测的多模态融合框架，通过结合频谱和时间表征来提高预测准确性。核心创新包括使用Morlet小波频谱图进行视觉变换器编码以提取频率感知特征，以及利用双向交叉注意力模块整合多模态信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列预测面临两大挑战：(1) 变量间复杂的交叉依赖关系，(2) 异构外部影响因素的存在。现有方法难以同时有效捕捉多尺度模式和跨变量相关性。

Method: 提出Spectrogram-Enhanced Multimodal Fusion (SEMF)框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 目标时间序列通过Morlet小波变换生成频谱图，由Vision Transformer编码器提取局部化、频率感知特征；(2) 外生变量（如金融指标、宏观经济信号）通过Transformer编码器捕捉时间依赖和多元动态；(3) 双向交叉注意力模块融合多模态表征，在保留各信号特征的同时建模跨模态相关性。

Result: 在多个商品价格预测任务上的实验表明，SEMF在多个预测 horizon 和评估指标上均优于7个竞争性基线模型。

Conclusion: 多模态融合与基于频谱图的编码方法能够有效捕捉复杂金融时间序列中的多尺度模式，证实了该方法在处理异构数据源和跨变量依赖问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Forecasting multivariate time series remains challenging due to complex cross-variable dependencies and the presence of heterogeneous external influences. This paper presents Spectrogram-Enhanced Multimodal Fusion (SEMF), which combines spectral and temporal representations for more accurate and robust forecasting. The target time series is transformed into Morlet wavelet spectrograms, from which a Vision Transformer encoder extracts localized, frequency-aware features. In parallel, exogenous variables, such as financial indicators and macroeconomic signals, are encoded via a Transformer to capture temporal dependencies and multivariate dynamics. A bidirectional cross-attention module integrates these modalities into a unified representation that preserves distinct signal characteristics while modeling cross-modal correlations. Applied to multiple commodity price forecasting tasks, SEMF achieves consistent improvements over seven competitive baselines across multiple forecasting horizons and evaluation metrics. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of multimodal fusion and spectrogram-based encoding in capturing multi-scale patterns within complex financial time series.

</details>


### [159] [Embedding Provenance in Computer Vision Datasets with JSON-LD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27348)
*Lynn Vonderhaar,Timothy Elvira,Tyler Thomas Procko,Omar Ochoa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于结构化图像来源信息（image provenance）的模式（schema），采用 JSON-LD（JavaScript 对象表示法-链接数据）格式，将溯源元数据直接嵌入图像文件内部，而非作为独立的文本文件保存，从而与既有标准（如 W3C PROV 等）保持链接与对齐，并保证溯源信息不丢失，提升系统的可维护性与可适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机视觉在工业中的普及使图像来源（provenance）的重要性日益凸显：溯源信息可用于追踪数据变更、理解下游模型的预期行为、辅助数据维护（合规检查、审计、复用）。但当前实践将溯源数据与图像分离存储（如额外的文本文件），导致关键描述信息丢失——例如图像采集设置、数据预处理步骤、模型架构或迭代过程等创建/编译参数往往未被记录。这造成图像与其来源之间关联脆弱，信息易失，系统可维护性与可复用性受损。

Method: 设计并提出一套新型图像溯源模式（schema），用 JSON-LD 表达以获得语义化的链接数据能力，并将其直接嵌入图像文件内部（而非外置为独立文本文件）。该模式受既有成熟标准启发并与之建立链接（如语义化的词汇/链接数据机制），从而对图像描述进行结构化组织：涵盖图像创建参数、采集设置、预处理流程、模型架构与迭代等维度。核心思想是维持视觉资源与其溯源描述之间的"直接连接"（intrinsically tied），使溯源随图像自身一同流转、复制与共享。

Result: 相较于外置的溯源文本文件，该方案带来两点显著优势：(1) 图像描述与一个健壮的、受标准启发并与之链接的模式保持一致；(2) 溯源信息与图像内在绑定，防止信息丢失，并改善系统质量属性，如可维护性与可适应性。文中强调维护视觉资源与其溯源之间的直接连接是其方法的关键收益。

Conclusion: 将溯源以 JSON-LD 形式嵌入图像并配合专门设计的模式，可在不破坏既有标准与生态的前提下，为工业界图像数据集提供可审计、可复用、健壮且自包含的来源管理机制。作者主张以"视觉资源与其溯源的直接绑定"作为该方案的中心原则，为后续图像来源标准化与工具链支持奠定基础。

Abstract: With the ubiquity of computer vision in industry, the importance of image provenance is becoming more apparent. Provenance provides information about the origin and derivation of some resource, e.g., an image dataset, enabling users to trace data changes to better understand the expected behaviors of downstream models trained on such data. Provenance may also help with data maintenance by ensuring compliance, supporting audits and improving reusability. Typically, if provided, provenance is stored separately, e.g., within a text file, leading to a loss of descriptive information for key details like image capture settings, data preprocessing steps, and model architecture or iteration. Images often lack the information detailing the parameters of their creation or compilation. This paper proposes a novel schema designed to structure image provenance in a manageable and coherent format. The approach utilizes JavaScript Object Notation for Linked Data (JSON-LD), embedding this provenance directly within the image file. This offers two significant benefits: (1) it aligns image descriptions with a robust schema inspired by and linked to established standards, and (2) it ensures that provenance remains intrinsically tied to images, preventing loss of information and enhancing system qualities, e.g., maintainability and adaptability. This approach emphasizes maintaining the direct connection between vision resources and their provenance.

</details>


### [160] [Active In-Context Learning for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27385)
*Wilailuck Treerath,Fabrizio Pittorino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Tabular Active In-Context Learning (Tab-AICL)：把表格基础模型 TabPFN 作为固定的上下文学习预测器，在主动学习循环中只迭代扩充「标注上下文」而非更新参数，并实例化四种采集规则（Margin 不确定性、Coreset 多样性、Hybrid 不确定性+多样性、Proxy-Hybrid 两阶段可扩展变体）。在 20 个分类基准、10 个随机种子、以 100 个标注内的归一化 AULC 为指标，TabPFN 类方法在 18/20 数据集上优于重训的 CatBoost-Margin/XGBoost-Margin，15/20 数据集取得最佳 AULC；TabPFN-Hybrid 相对最强 GBDT 基线在 8/20 数据集上经 BH 校正后显著更优，且 Proxy-Hybrid 将 TabPFN 打分开销降低约 1/α（≈20×）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格分类的主动学习在冷启动（极少标注）阶段收益有限，因为采集函数依赖的预测不确定性在极小训练集上不稳定、易失真。另一方面，TabPFN 等表格基础模型通过上下文学习（ICL）提供无需任务特定梯度更新即可获得的校准概率预测，天然适配低标注的冷启动场景。但把 ICL 嵌入 AL 循环存在两个实际障碍：其一，TabPFN 推理成本随上下文长度二次增长，对大候选池做全池打分代价高昂；其二，纯不确定性查询在噪声或类别不平衡的池上会「盯住」离群点而非信息量大的边界点，较为脆弱。已有工作只把 TabPFN 当作预测器做基准，尚未系统性地在 AL 循环内研究表格基础模型与采集策略的耦合。

Method: 形式化：设无标注池 U 与初始标注集 L0，每轮由采集规则选批量 Q_t（大小 B）交由 oracle 标注，标注上下文更新为 L_{t+1}=L_t∪Oracle(Q_t)，查询点从池中移除；预测器 M（TabPFN）参数固定，学习即「上下文优化」，目标是最大化给定标注预算 |L_t| 下的测试集性能。

四种采集规则：
1) TabPFN-Margin（不确定性）：按排序概率 p_(1)-p_(2) 取最小者。
2) TabPFN-Coreset（多样性）：贪心 k-center，迭代选择距当前已标注集最远的点；增量维护最近邻距离向量 D_min，内存 O(|U|)。
3) TabPFN-Hybrid：先按预测熵保留 top-N_cand 候选（N_cand=min(|U|, max(2B, ⌊|U|/2⌋)），再在其预处理特征上做 k-means（k=B），查询离各质心最近的点。
4) TabPFN-Proxy-Hybrid（两阶段可扩展）：用带类别权重的逻辑回归代理模型对全池按代理预测熵排序，保留 top-N_proxy（α=0.05，N_min=200、N_max=2000 截断），仅在短名单上计算 TabPFN 熵，再保留 N_div=min(3B,|S_short|) 做 k-means 多样性选取，TabPFN 评估次数约减少 1/α。

预处理统一：按元数据判定类别/数值（缺失元数据时整数且唯一值<20 视为类别），数值均值填补+StandardScaler，类别众数填补+OrdinalEncoder，所有统计量仅在训练池上拟合以避免泄漏。

实验：pool-based 批量 AL，70/30 分层划分，初始每类 1 个标注样本，预算 N_max=100（小数据集额外在查询原池 50% 时停止）；批次 B∈{5,10,15,20}；10 个种子重采样划分与初始化，报告 mean±std；主指标为归一化 AULC（等价于冷启动轨迹上测试 Cohen's κ 的均值），另报 N=100 处的最终 κ 与 ROC AUC（多类 one-vs-rest 宏平均）；显著性用配对 Wilcoxon 符号秩检验 + Benjamini-Hochberg FDR 校正（p_adj<0.05）。

基线：TabPFN-Random（被动，tabpfn v6.2.0，n_estimators=32）、每轮从零重训的 CatBoost-Margin 与 XGBoost-Margin（n_estimators=2000、lr=0.05、depth=6、early stopping 50）、以及配随机采集的半监督 Label Spreading（kNN 核 k=7、α=0.2）。数据集为 OpenML/UCI 的 20 个分类任务，超 10,000 样本者均匀下采样至 10,000。

Result: 主结果（表 II，B=10，归一化 AULC）：TabPFN 系方法整体在 18/20 数据集上优于重训 GBDT 基线，并在 15/20 数据集取得最佳 AULC；最强组合 TabPFN-Hybrid 相对 CatBoost-Margin 的配对 Wilcoxon 检验经 BH 校正后仅在 8/20 数据集上显著更优（Ionosphere、Balance-scale、Vehicle、Page-blocks、Seeds、CoverType、JM1、Steel-Plates，ΔAULC 约 +0.05~+0.20，p_adj≤0.012），而 CatBoost-Margin 在任一数据集上均未取得对 TabPFN-Hybrid 的显著优势。

方法间无普适赢家：Margin 在干净/结构化数据（Page-blocks、Glass）上强；Hybrid 在簇状几何数据（Vehicle、Balance-scale）上占优；Proxy-Hybrid 在含噪数据（Adult）上跨种子方差更窄、更稳定，但在 KC1、JM1、Phoneme 等上并非最佳。主动查询并非总是有益：在 Glass、Bank-Marketing、Tic-Tac-Toe 上 TabPFN-Random 匹配或超过主动规则；CatBoost-Margin 在 KC1、Ilpd 上仍具竞争力（树模型偏置在小预算下依然有效）。

最终步指标（表 V/VI，N=100）：TabPFN-Hybrid/Proxy-Hybrid/Margin 在多数数据集上取得最佳 κ 与 ROC AUC，如 Vehicle κ≈0.97–0.99、AUC≈1.000，CoverType κ≈0.47、AUC≈0.90；XGBoost-Margin 整体最弱（如 Ilpd AUC 0.636、Diabetes 0.723）。

计算与消融：Proxy 短名单将昂贵的 TabPFN 全池评估降低约 20×（α=0.05，截断未激活时），TabPFN-Hybrid 在结构化数据（Ionosphere）通常 N<50 标签即收敛并优于重训基线。批次消融（表 IV）显示 B 越小归一化 AULC 通常略好（B=5 常最佳），因上下文更新更频繁；但跨批次性能大体稳定，可选用较大批次摊薄标注开销。

Conclusion: 结论与局限：Tab-AICL 将表格基础模型的主动学习重述为「上下文优化」，在 20 个基准上验证 ICL 推理在低标注区间的归纳偏置优势——TabPFN 本身即强冷启动预测器，配合主动采集常能进一步提高归一化 AULC。但存在明确限制与强烈的数据集/制度依赖性：(1) 属小数据方法，推理随上下文长度二次增长并受最大上下文窗口限制，标注增多后收益饱和而成本上升；(2) 极早期对初始化敏感，类别不平衡或不利种子会在采集规则获得足够信号前扭曲轨迹；(3) 各采集规则有可预测的失效模式——不确定性易过度关注异常/噪声点，纯多样性会浪费标签在无信息区域，代理过滤引入可能失真的额外建模假设。实践建议：把 Tab-AICL 作为「冷启动模块」——用 TabPFN（可选叠加主动采集）在数十至数百标注内快速获得可用模型，待标注充裕且重训成本可接受时再移交 CatBoost/XGBoost。后续方向：自动判定从上下文推理切换到重训模型的准则；上下文压缩/蒸馏，把已获取上下文的信息迁移给轻量学生模型以缓解二次推理成本；基于早期迭代的采集规则元选择；并将方法扩展到回归与多标签表格任务。

Abstract: Active learning (AL) reduces labeling cost by querying informative samples, but in tabular settings its cold-start gains are often limited because uncertainty estimates are unreliable when models are trained on very few labels. Tabular foundation models such as TabPFN provide calibrated probabilistic predictions via in-context learning (ICL), i.e., without task-specific weight updates, enabling an AL regime in which the labeled context - rather than parameters - is iteratively optimized. We formalize Tabular Active In-Context Learning (Tab-AICL) and instantiate it with four acquisition rules: uncertainty (TabPFN-Margin), diversity (TabPFN-Coreset), an uncertainty-diversity hybrid (TabPFN-Hybrid), and a scalable two-stage method (TabPFN-Proxy-Hybrid) that shortlists candidates using a lightweight linear proxy before TabPFN-based selection. Across 20 classification benchmarks, Tab-AICL improves cold-start sample efficiency over retrained gradient-boosting baselines (CatBoost-Margin and XGBoost-Margin), measured by normalized AULC up to 100 labeled samples.

</details>


### [161] [Diagnosing Non-Markovian Observations in Reinforcement Learning via Prediction-Based Violation Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27389)
*Naveen Mysore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于预测的评分方法，通过随机森林和岭回归的组合来量化观察轨迹中的非马尔可夫结构。该方法在6个环境、3种RL算法、不同强度AR(1)噪声下进行评估，能够检测马尔可夫属性破坏，并在低维环境中发现了"反转现象"这一失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习算法假设观察满足马尔可夫性质，但现实世界的传感器经常通过相关噪声、延迟或部分可观测性违反这一假设。标准性能指标将马尔可夫破坏与其他次优来源混为一谈，导致从业者缺乏此类违反的诊断工具。

Method: 提出基于预测的评分方法：随机森林首先去除非线性马尔可夫兼容动态；然后岭回归测试历史观察是否在残差上减少了预测误差，超出当前观察所能提供的范围。所得分数有界于[0, 1]，无需因果图构建。

Result: 后验检测中，16个环境-算法对中的7个（主要是高维 locomotion 任务）在噪声强度与违反分数之间显示出显著的正单调性（Spearman rho高达0.78）；在训练时噪声下，16个对中的13个表现出统计显著的奖励退化。在低维环境中观察到反转现象：随机森林吸收噪声信号导致分数随真实违反增加而下降。

Conclusion: 该评分方法能够正确识别部分可观测性并指导架构选择，完全恢复了因非马尔可夫观察而损失的性能。为RL中马尔可夫违反的诊断提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning algorithms assume that observations satisfy the Markov property, yet real-world sensors frequently violate this assumption through correlated noise, latency, or partial observability. Standard performance metrics conflate Markov breakdowns with other sources of suboptimality, leaving practitioners without diagnostic tools for such violations. This paper introduces a prediction-based scoring method that quantifies non-Markovian structure in observation trajectories. A random forest first removes nonlinear Markov-compliant dynamics; ridge regression then tests whether historical observations reduce prediction error on the residuals beyond what the current observation provides. The resulting score is bounded in [0, 1] and requires no causal graph construction. Evaluation spans six environments (CartPole, Pendulum, Acrobot, HalfCheetah, Hopper, Walker2d), three algorithms (PPO, A2C, SAC), controlled AR(1) noise at six intensity levels, and 10 seeds per condition. In post-hoc detection, 7 of 16 environment-algorithm pairs, primarily high-dimensional locomotion tasks, show significant positive monotonicity between noise intensity and the violation score (Spearman rho up to 0.78, confirmed under repeated-measures analysis); under training-time noise, 13 of 16 pairs exhibit statistically significant reward degradation. An inversion phenomenon is documented in low-dimensional environments where the random forest absorbs the noise signal, causing the score to decrease as true violations grow, a failure mode analyzed in detail. A practical utility experiment demonstrates that the proposed score correctly identifies partial observability and guides architecture selection, fully recovering performance lost to non-Markovian observations. Source code to reproduce all results is provided at https://github.com/NAVEENMN/Markovianes.

</details>


### [162] [K-Means Based TinyML Anomaly Detection and Distributed Model Reuse via the Distributed Internet of Learning (DIoL)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27393)
*Abdulrahman Albaiz,Fathi Amsaad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对资源受限微控制器(MCU)的轻量级K-Means异常检测模型，以及一种名为分布式物联网学习(DIoL)的分布式模型共享工作流。核心思想是"训练一次，处处部署"(TOSE)，即在一台设备上训练的模型可以导出为可移植的文本表示，并在其他设备上直接复用，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物联网边缘设备上部署机器学习模型面临资源受限的挑战，传统的云端训练方式需要每台设备都进行模型训练，导致计算资源浪费和部署效率低下。需要一种能够在资源受限的微控制器上实现异常检测，同时支持模型高效共享和复用的解决方案。

Method: 1) 使用迷你冰箱的真实功率测量数据进行实验；2) 在设备端执行特征提取、聚类和阈值估计；3) 提出DIoL框架，将训练好的模型导出为便携式文本表示；4) 构建两设备原型验证TOSE方法，设备A负责训练，设备B直接使用模型进行推理。

Result: 实验结果表明：1) 异常检测行为一致；2) 解析开销可忽略不计；3) 独立运行和DIoL运行的推理时间完全相同。验证了"训练一次，处处部署"方案的可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的DIoL框架实现了跨嵌入式设备集群的可扩展、低成本TinyML部署，为资源受限环境下的边缘机器学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a lightweight K-Means anomaly detection model and a distributed model-sharing workflow designed for resource-constrained microcontrollers (MCUs). Using real power measurements from a mini-fridge appliance, the system performs on-device feature extraction, clustering, and threshold estimation to identify abnormal appliance behavior. To avoid retraining models on every device, we introduce the Distributed Internet of Learning (DIoL), which enables a model trained on one MCU to be exported as a portable, text-based representation and reused directly on other devices. A two-device prototype demonstrates the feasibility of the "Train Once, Share Everywhere" (TOSE) approach using a real-world appliance case study, where Device A trains the model and Device B performs inference without retraining. Experimental results show consistent anomaly detection behavior, negligible parsing overhead, and identical inference runtimes between standalone and DIoL-based operation. The proposed framework enables scalable, low-cost TinyML deployment across fleets of embedded devices.

</details>


### [163] [Conditional Factuality Controlled LLMs with Generalization Certificates via Conformal Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27403)
*Kai Ye,Qingtao Pan,Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 **CFC（Conditional Factuality Control）**：一个纯后处理（post-hoc）的共形框架，在 LLM 采样时用"特征条件"的接受阈值替代单一全局阈值，从而给出**函数类条件覆盖**（function-class conditional coverage）保证，而非仅边际覆盖；并推导带 PAC 稳定性证书的变体 **CFC-PAC**，保证条件失覆盖偏离目标不超过 O(√(log(1/δ)/N))。理论还证明在轻度假设下条件规则比边际 CP 在同等目标覆盖下期望预测集更小。实验（合成数据、GSM8K、TriviaQA、Flickr8k 视觉语言模型）表明 CFC/CFC-PAC 在各难度组上覆盖更贴合目标、预测集更紧凑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1) 现有针对 LLM 的共形预测（Best-of-N + 验证器/重排序式集合输出）普遍只提供**边际覆盖**并依赖单一全局阈值，对易提示过度覆盖、对难提示（如长数学题、罕见实体）系统性欠覆盖，且因阈值被迫在特征空间各区域折中而产生失准。2) 采样类推理策略（增加 test-time compute）虽提升准确率却不提供形式化可靠性保证，安全关键场景不可接受。3) 精确的点态条件覆盖在有限样本下不可达，只能采用 Gibbs 等人的函数类条件松弛（以线性基 Φ(·)^⊤β 为条件类）。4) 同时希望预测集合保持紧凑，使基于采样的推理仍具计算可行性。

Method: **问题设定**：提示 X，固定生成器 π，采样候选集 C(X)={Y_j}_{j=1}^M；验证器分数 V:𝒳×𝒴→[0,1]（越小越好）；正确性指示 A(X,y)。目标：输出集合 Ĉ_α(X) 满足 P(∃y∈Ĉ_α: A=1) ≥ 1−α。引入**潜在成功分数** S(X):=inf{V(X,y): y∈C(X), A(X,y)=1}（无正确候选时定义为 1），于是"集合含至少一个正确答案"⟺ S(X) ≤ λ(X)。

**CFC 算法（Algorithm 1）**：
- 在校准集 {(X_i, S_i)}_{i=1}^N 上，用 Gibbs 等人的**增广分位数回归**拟合特征条件分位：对候选测试分数 s∈[0,1]，解
  β_s = argmin_β [ (1/(N+1))Σ_{i=1}^N ρ_{1−α}(S_i − Φ(X_i)^⊤β) + (1/(N+1))ρ_{1−α}(s − Φ(X_{N+1})^⊤β) ]，
  其中 ρ_τ(u)=u(τ−1{u<0}) 为 pinball 损失；
- 定义 g_{X_{N+1}}(s)=Φ(X_{N+1})^⊤β_s，取**不动点阈值** λ̂_α(X)=sup{s∈[0,1]: s ≤ g_X(s)}（部署时无需在网格上反复求解式(4.2)）；
- 输出 Ĉ_α(X)={y∈C(X): V(X,y) ≤ λ̂_α(X)}。对易提示给更严阈值、难提示给更松阈值。全程不微调生成器与验证器，纯后处理。

**理论保证**：
- 定理4.1（条件覆盖）：在可交换性假设下，取 𝔽={Φ(X)^⊤β}，对任意非负 f∈𝔽 且 E[f(X)]>0，有 P_f(∃y∈Ĉ_α: A=1) ≥ 1−α（证明用增广目标在 f 方向的最优性 + 可交换性 + 不动点阈值单调性）。
- **CFC-PAC（Algorithm 2）**：在增广目标加 ridge 正则 (λ/2)||β||²，假设特征有界 ||Φ(X)||≤R、条件 CDF F_{S|X=x} 在 t 上 L-Lipschitz；由正则化 ERM 的稳定性得到替换单个校准点使阈值变化 O(1/N)，经 McDiarmid 不等式得 Q(D_cal) ≥ 1−α−ε_N(δ)，ε_N(δ)=O(√(log(1/δ)/N))；取 α_eff=α−ε_N(δ) 后以概率 ≥1−δ 得到 P(S ≤ λ̂_{α_eff}(X) | D_cal) ≥ 1−α。
- **效率分析（命题4.3、定理4.4）**：以 G_X(λ)=P(V≤λ|X) 表集合大小期望 M·E[G_X(λ(X))]；在假设（各 t 下 F_t 连续严格增、t↦F_t(λ) 非增、C_t(u)=G_t(F_t^{-1}(u)) 凸可微且具减差性质）下，用凸性支撑线 + Chebyshev 重排不等式证明"预言机"条件阈值 λ*(X)=q_α(X) 的期望集合大小严格小于任何常数阈值；若增广分位回归一致（sup_x|λ̂_{α,N}(x)−λ*(x)|→p 0），CFC 渐近继承该优势。

**实验设计**：基线为 TopK、ICP（全局阈值）、Learnt CP（学特征条件阈值但无精确共形校正，用于区分"学习"与"条件共形化"的贡献）；指标为 ECR（经验覆盖）、APSS（平均预测集大小，越小越好；<1 表示部分提示弃权返回空集）、GSC（分组覆盖的最小值）。数据：合成（难度 T∈[0,1]，M=50，α=0.10）、GSM8K（Llama-3-8B-Instruct，验证器 Qwen2.5-Math-RM-72B，前 5 个候选、T=平均验证损失、Φ=[1,T,T²]）、TriviaQA（答案分布熵+最大验证损失的校准定义特征与两分组）、Flickr8k（Qwen2-VL-7B-Instruct，每图至多 2 个缓存候选、同样的二次基）。

Result: **合成数据（α=0.10）**：CFC ECR 90.3±0.5 / APSS 15.53 / GSC 88.7；CFC-P 90.8 / 15.87 / 89.1；对比 ICP（90.2 / 16.71 / 57.4）、TopK（90.6 / 16.00 / 58.2）、Learnt CP（90.2 / 15.72 / 84.3）。最差组覆盖提升约 30 个百分点，同时集合更小；全 α∈[0.10,0.45] 扫描结论一致（命题中的严格不等式在 α=0.40/0.45 时变弱）。

**TriviaQA**：α=0.30 时 CFC ECR 68.4 / GSC 62.8 / APSS 0.88（ICP：69.5/49.3/0.90），CFC-P-F 70.0/69.2/0.99（目标 70%）；α=0.20/0.25/0.35/0.40/0.45 扫描显示 CFC 最省集合、CFC-PAC-FULL 最贴目标且子群下限最高。特征图消融：熵-线性 66.9/45.1/1.02、最大损失-线性 67.6/56.5/0.99、所选特征 68.4/62.8/0.88。

**GSM8K**：α=0.05 时 CFC ECR 94.82 / GSC 88.48 / APSS 2.35（ICP：95.09/79.85/4.73，Learnt CP：94.91/88.48/4.01），CFC-P-F GSC 88.79 / APSS 4.59；候选预算 5→20 时 ECR/GSC 几乎不变而 APSS 翻倍（1.49→3.79），故主文用 N=5。

**Flickr8k（VLM）**：α=0.03（目标 97%）时 CFC-P-F ECR 97.27 / GSC 95.21 / APSS 1.42（ICP 95.58/85.21/1.84），CFC APSS 0.99 但 ECR 95.81（因任务过易、常塌缩为单条字幕）；N=2、mean-loss、poly2 为最佳平衡设置。

**校准规模/预算消融**：合成上 N_cal∈{2000,5000,10000}、M∈{50,100,150} 时 CFC 真实覆盖稳定在 0.80 附近，CFC-PAC 系统性略高约 0.006–0.010，集合尺寸代价很小。

**需注意的局限**：定理4.1 是对校准样本期望意义下的条件覆盖（而非逐点条件覆盖）；CFC-PAC 的证书通过"双倍扣除"同一 ε_N 获得，形式上略显循环；实验部分 CFC 的 ECR 常略低于目标覆盖（且 APSS<1 表明存在弃权/空集，这在严格意义上与"集合含正确答案"保证相冲突）；效率结论依赖 oracle 阈值与较强的逐点一致假设；TriviaQA 的分组由校准集 92.5 分位定义，存在一定数据依赖选择。

Conclusion: CFC 用一个特征条件的连续接受阈值取代 LLM 共形控制中的单一全局阈值，在可交换性下获得函数类条件覆盖保证；CFC-PAC 借助 ridge 正则化与稳定性/McDiarmid 论证给出高概率有限样本证书（ε_N(δ)=O(√(log(1/δ)/N))）；效率分析表明在单调性/凸性/减差等温和假设下条件规则在同等覆盖下严格优于边际 CP，且 CFC 渐近继承预言机效率。实验显示该纯后处理层在合成数据、GSM8K、TriviaQA 与 Flickr8k 视觉语言场景中都能在提升最差子群覆盖的同时保持或缩小预测集，并可跨基础模型迁移。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) need reliable test-time control of hallucinations. Existing conformal methods for LLMs typically provide only \emph{marginal} guarantees and rely on a single global threshold, which can under-cover hard prompts, over-cover easy ones, and produce oversized prediction sets. We propose \emph{Conditional Factuality Control} (CFC), a post-hoc conformal framework that returns \emph{set-valued} outputs with \emph{conditional} coverage guarantees. CFC defines a continuous, feature-conditional acceptance threshold through augmented quantile regression on a latent ``success'' score, and deploys it through a fixed-point threshold rule at inference time. Theoretically, we show that CFC satisfies a conditional coverage guarantee under exchangeability and analyze its \emph{efficiency}, proving that, under mild assumptions on the score distributions, the conditional rule is strictly more sample-efficient than marginal conformal prediction at the same target coverage. We further derive a PAC-style variant, CFC-PAC, which shrinks the nominal risk level based on a stability bound, yielding a finite-sample certificate that the conditional miscoverage deviates from the target by at most $O(\sqrt{\log(1/δ)/N})$. Empirically, on synthetic data, real-world reasoning and QA benchmarks, and a Flickr8k VLM setting, CFC and CFC-PAC consistently attain near-target coverage across difficulty groups while using smaller prediction sets than CP and non-CP baselines.

</details>


### [164] [The Geometry of Harmful Intent: Training-Free Anomaly Detection via Angular Deviation in LLM Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27412)
*Isaac Llorente-Saguer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentBiopsy通过分析LLM残差流激活的几何结构来检测有害提示词，无需训练样本。基于200个安全提示词计算主成分，用径向偏离角θ的负对数似然作为异常分数。在Qwen两个系列的6个变体上均达到AUROC≥0.937的检测效果，且亚毫秒级延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM对齐技术（如ablation）可能无法彻底消除有害意图表示，亟需一种训练无关且能检测绕过对齐的有害提示词的方法。

Method: 使用200个安全规范提示词计算目标层激活的主成分，以新提示词相对于参考方向的径向偏离角θ作为特征，通过θ在正态分布拟合下的负对数似然作为异常分数，方向无关地检测偏离。

Result: 6个模型变体上，有害vs规范检测AUROC≥0.937；有害vs良性攻击性(XSTest)检测AUROC=1.000；每次查询延迟亚毫秒级。几何结构在ablation后仍保留。有害提示角向分布极窄(σθ≈0.03 rad)，比规范分布(σθ≈0.27 rad)紧一个数量级。不同模型家族有害提示占据相反的"环形"方向。

Conclusion: 有害意图的几何表示与下游生成式拒绝机制在神经上是分离的；有害提示词具有高度一致的角向分布特征；方向无关的评分规则是必要的，因为不同模型家族呈现相反的环形方向。

Abstract: We present LatentBiopsy, a training-free method for detecting harmful prompts by analysing the geometry of residual-stream activations in large language models. Given 200 safe normative prompts, LatentBiopsy computes the leading principal component of their activations at a target layer and characterises new prompts by their radial deviation angle $θ$ from this reference direction. The anomaly score is the negative log-likelihood of $θ$ under a Gaussian fit to the normative distribution, flagging deviations symmetrically regardless of orientation. No harmful examples are required for training.
  We evaluate two complete model triplets from the Qwen3.5-0.8B and Qwen2.5-0.5B families: base, instruction-tuned, and \emph{abliterated} (refusal direction surgically removed via orthogonalisation). Across all six variants, LatentBiopsy achieves AUROC $\geq$0.937 for harmful-vs-normative detection and AUROC = 1.000 for discriminating harmful from benign-aggressive prompts (XSTest), with sub-millisecond per-query overhead.
  Three empirical findings emerge. First, geometry survives refusal ablation: both abliterated variants achieve AUROC at most 0.015 below their instruction-tuned counterparts, establishing a geometric dissociation between harmful-intent representation and the downstream generative refusal mechanism. Second, harmful prompts exhibit a near-degenerate angular distribution ($σ_θ\approx 0.03$ rad), an order of magnitude tighter than the normative distribution ($σ_θ\approx 0.27$ rad), preserved across all alignment stages including abliteration. Third, the two families exhibit opposite ring orientations at the same depth: harmful prompts occupy the outer ring in Qwen3.5-0.8B but the inner ring in Qwen2.5-0.5B, directly motivating the direction-agnostic scoring rule.

</details>


### [165] [Kempe Swap K-Means: A Scalable Near-Optimal Solution for Semi-Supervised Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27417)
*Yuxuan Ren,Shijie Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Kempe Swap K-Means的基于质心的约束聚类启发式算法，通过Kempe链交换和受控扰动机制，在保持高计算效率的同时实现接近最优的聚类划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束聚类中的must-link（必须链接）和cannot-link（不能链接）约束给传统聚类算法带来了挑战，需要在满足约束条件的同时避免陷入局部最优，现有方法在求解质量和计算效率之间难以兼顾。

Method: 采用双阶段迭代框架：分配阶段利用Kempe链交换在约束可行空间中细化当前聚类；质心更新阶段计算最优簇质心。为增强全局搜索能力，在更新阶段引入受控扰动以跳出局部最优。

Result: 实验结果表明，该算法在保证高计算效率和可扩展性的前提下，能够获得接近最优的聚类划分，在大规模数据集上的聚类准确率和算法效率均优于当前最先进的基准方法。

Conclusion: Kempe Swap K-Means算法通过结合Kempe链交换与受控扰动策略，有效解决了约束聚类中的局部最优问题，为大规模约束聚类提供了一种高效且准确的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel centroid-based heuristic algorithm, termed Kempe Swap K-Means, for constrained clustering under rigid must-link (ML) and cannot-link (CL) constraints. The algorithm employs a dual-phase iterative process: an assignment step that utilizes Kempe chain swaps to refine current clustering in the constrained solution space and a centroid update step that computes optimal cluster centroids. To enhance global search capabilities and avoid local optima, the framework incorporates controlled perturbations during the update phase. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that the proposed method achieves near-optimal partitions while maintaining high computational efficiency and scalability. The results indicate that Kempe Swap K-Means consistently outperforms state-of-the-art benchmarks in both clustering accuracy and algorithmic efficiency for large-scale datasets.

</details>


### [166] [The Geometric Cost of Normalization: Affine Bounds on the Bayesian Complexity of Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27432)
*Sungbae Chun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明LayerNorm通过均值中心化将数据限制在通过原点的线性超平面，从而将后续权重矩阵的局部学习系数(LLC)降低m/2；而RMSNorm通过投影到球面完全保留LLC。这一差异在训练前就已由数据流形几何决定，且对于非零曲率流形，LLC下降是二进制的——仅平直流形才会导致下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解LayerNorm与RMSNorm在模型复杂度上的理论差异。两种归一化方法的几何约束不同，但这种差异对模型复杂度的精确量化影响尚未被系统证明。

Method: 1. 理论分析：证明LayerNorm的均值中心化步骤通过将数据限制在通过原点的线性超平面，将后续权重矩阵的局部学习系数(LLC)降低精确的m/2；2. 几何阈值条件：对于余维1流形，LLC下降是二进制的——任何非零曲率（无论符号或幅度）都足以保留LLC，仅仿射平坦流形导致下降；3. 有限样本分析：有限样本下该阈值呈现平滑交叉，其宽度取决于数据分布中实际经历曲率的比例；4. 仿射对称性扩展：证明Softmax单纯形数据与显式下游偏置配对时产生相同的m/2 LLC下降；5. 实验验证：使用wrLLC框架进行受控单层缩放实验。

Result: 1. 严格证明了LayerNorm通过均值中心化将LLC降低m/2，而RMSNorm完全保留LLC；2. 建立了LLC下降的几何阈值条件——曲率的存在与否决定了下降是否发生；3. 揭示了有限样本下从二进制下降到平滑交叉的转变机制；4. 发现Softmax单纯形数据的"走私偏置"效应。

Conclusion: LayerNorm与RMSNorm的几何差异具有可量化的理论后果，LLC的差异在训练前就已由数据流形几何决定。这一发现为理解现代归一化技术的理论性质提供了严格框架，并揭示了数据几何与学习复杂度之间的本质联系。

Abstract: LayerNorm and RMSNorm impose fundamentally different geometric constraints on their outputs - and this difference has a precise, quantifiable consequence for model complexity. We prove that LayerNorm's mean-centering step, by confining data to a linear hyperplane (through the origin), reduces the Local Learning Coefficient (LLC) of the subsequent weight matrix by exactly $m/2$ (where $m$ is its output dimension); RMSNorm's projection onto a sphere preserves the LLC entirely. This reduction is structurally guaranteed before any training begins, determined by data manifold geometry alone.
  The underlying condition is a geometric threshold: for the codimension-one manifolds we study, the LLC drop is binary -- any non-zero curvature, regardless of sign or magnitude, is sufficient to preserve the LLC, while only affinely flat manifolds cause the drop. At finite sample sizes this threshold acquires a smooth crossover whose width depends on how much of the data distribution actually experiences the curvature, not merely on whether curvature exists somewhere.
  We verify both predictions experimentally with controlled single-layer scaling experiments using the wrLLC framework. We further show that Softmax simplex data introduces a "smuggled bias" that activates the same $m/2$ LLC drop when paired with an explicit downstream bias, proved via the affine symmetry extension of the main theorem and confirmed empirically.

</details>


### [167] [Interpretable Physics Extraction from Data for Linear Dynamical Systems using Lie Generator Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27442)
*Shafayeth Jamil,Rehan Kapadia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"李生成元网络"（Lie Generator Networks, LGN）：把线性动力系统的状态转移矩阵 A 参数化为"反对称矩阵 S 减正对角矩阵 D"的结构化形式，从而把动力学求解从数值积分换成矩阵指数化（李群一参数子群）。稳定性与耗散性由架构本身保证，而非通过损失函数显式惩罚。在 100 维稳定 RLC 梯形网络系统辨识任务上，普通基于导数的最小二乘辨识会得到不稳定特征值，无约束 LGN 虽稳定但谱（物理特征）不正确，而 LGN-SD 能完整恢复全部 100 个特征值，特征值误差比无约束方案低两个数量级以上，并给出可解释的极点、自然频率与阻尼比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性动力系统（控制理论、信号处理、电路分析的分析骨架）具有基于状态转移矩阵的精确闭式解，但在参数需从数据反推（系统辨识）的场景下，近年来的神经方法虽具柔性却牺牲了物理保证：Neural ODE 用数值积分近似轨迹，可能违反物理不变量（如能量守恒）；能量保持型架构（正交/Hamiltonian 结构）又无法原生表示真实系统必需的耗散。核心动机在于：既然系统本身是线性的，学习过程为什么还要是非线性的？作者主张应当把解析结构（李群/矩阵指数）作为归纳偏置直接编码进网络，使"由积分转向指数"在构造层面保留结构。

Method: 核心构件是结构化生成元 A = S − D，其中 S 为反对称矩阵（对应旋转/守恒分量，通过 S = (K − Kᵀ)/2 参数化以保证反对称性），D 为正对角矩阵（对应耗散/衰减分量）。前向传播不使用 ODE 求解器，而直接计算 x(t) = exp(A t) x(0)（一参数子群/Taylor 展开或 scaling-and-squaring 技巧），从而在连续时间域上给出解析线性流；时间常数/时间变系统通过对生成元加入时间依赖或分段常数处理，形成统一框架覆盖线性保守（纯 S）、耗散（S−D）与时变三类系统。变体包括：无约束（unconstrained）LGN（仅靠学习到的 S、D 施加弱约束）与 LGN-SD（在参数化/正则上显式强制耗散与物理一致的谱结构），后者针对系统辨识任务从数据中反推 S 与 D。评测在合成的 100 维稳定 RLC 梯形网络（电路级联，含真实极点、固有频率、阻尼比）上，比较基于导数的最小二乘辨识、无约束 LGN 与 LGN-SD 的特征值恢复精度。

Result: 在 100 维稳定 RLC 梯形网络实验上：（1）标准基于导数的最小二乘系统辨识可能给出不稳定特征值（尽管真实系统稳定），完全失去物理意义；（2）无约束 LGN 得到稳定但物理上错误的谱（特征值位置/频率/阻尼与真值不符）；（3）LGN-SD 成功恢复全部 100 个特征值，其平均特征值误差比无约束方案低两个数量级以上。恢复的特征值可直接解释为系统极点、固有（自然）频率与阻尼比，这类可解释物理量是黑盒网络无法提供的。

Conclusion: 通过用矩阵指数化替换 ODE 积分，并把反对称—耗散结构直接编码进网络参数化，LGN 实现了"结构按构造保留"的线性系统学习范式：稳定性与耗散性来自架构而非损失函数的软惩罚，从而在系统辨识中同时保证数值稳定与物理正确性，并提供面向电路/控制工程的可解释谱信息。论文主张，当底层系统为线性时，学习器应继承其解析结构（李群结构）而非用通用非线性近似器替代，从而兼顾柔性与物理保证。局限与展望在于：方法本质上限于线性（可含时变）系统族，对强非线性系统不适用；消融与更复杂网络（如含耦合RLC、多时间尺度、噪声与数据量有限情形）下的鲁棒性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: When the system is linear, why should learning be nonlinear? Linear dynamical systems, the analytical backbone of control theory, signal processing and circuit analysis, have exact closed-form solutions via the state transition matrix. Yet when system parameters must be inferred from data, recent neural approaches offer flexibility at the cost of physical guarantees: Neural ODEs provide flexible trajectory approximation but may violate physical invariants, while energy preserving architectures do not natively represent dissipation essential to real-world systems. We introduce Lie Generator Networks (LGN), which learn a structured generator A and compute trajectories directly via matrix exponentiation. This shift from integration to exponentiation preserves structure by construction. By parameterizing A = S - D (skew-symmetric minus positive diagonal), stability and dissipation emerge from the underlying architecture and are not introduced during training via the loss function. LGN provides a unified framework for linear conservative, dissipative, and time-varying systems. On a 100-dimensional stable RLC ladder, standard derivative-based least-squares system identification can yield unstable eigenvalues. The unconstrained LGN yields stable but physically incorrect spectra, whereas LGN-SD recovers all 100 eigenvalues with over two orders of magnitude lower mean eigenvalue error than unconstrained alternatives. Critically, these eigenvalues reveal poles, natural frequencies, and damping ratios which are interpretable physics that black-box networks do not provide.

</details>


### [168] [GIFT: Bootstrapping Image-to-CAD Program Synthesis via Geometric Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27448)
*Giorgio Giannone,Anna Clare Doris,Amin Heyrani Nobari,Kai Xu,Akash Srivastava,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GIFT框架，通过几何反馈将测试时计算转化为高质量训练样本，解决CAD程序生成中视觉几何与符号程序对齐的数据稀缺问题，包含软拒绝采样和失败驱动增强两种机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前方法在设计复杂度增加时无法可靠学习生成可执行CAD程序，原因在于缺乏将视觉几何与程序语法对齐的多样化训练数据。现有微调方法依赖有限监督数据集或昂贵的后训练流程，导致系统脆弱。

Method: GIFT框架包含两个机制：(1) 软拒绝采样(GIFT-REJECT)保留超出精确匹配的多样化高保真程序；(2) 失败驱动增强(GIFT-FAIL)将接近正确的预测转化为合成训练样本。模型通过将推理时搜索成本摊销到参数中获益。

Result: 减少80%推理计算量，mean IoU提升12%，无需额外人工标注或专门架构即可与更复杂的多模态系统竞争。

Conclusion: GIFT通过几何反馈自举高质量训练样本，有效缓解数据稀缺瓶颈，为可扩展的生成式CAD设计提供了数据高效解决方案。

Abstract: Generating executable CAD programs from images requires alignment between visual geometry and symbolic program representations, a capability that current methods fail to learn reliably as design complexity increases. Existing fine-tuning approaches rely on either limited supervised datasets or expensive post-training pipelines, resulting in brittle systems that restrict progress in generative CAD design. We argue that the primary bottleneck lies not in model or algorithmic capacity, but in the scarcity of diverse training examples that align visual geometry with program syntax. This limitation is especially acute because the collection of diverse and verified engineering datasets is both expensive and difficult to scale, constraining the development of robust generative CAD models. We introduce Geometric Inference Feedback Tuning (GIFT), a data augmentation framework that leverages geometric feedback to turn test-time compute into a bootstrapped set of high-quality training samples. GIFT combines two mechanisms: Soft-Rejection Sampling (GIFT-REJECT), which retains diverse high-fidelity programs beyond exact ground-truth matches, and Failure-Driven Augmentation (GIFT-FAIL), which converts near-miss predictions into synthetic training examples that improve robustness on challenging geometries. By amortizing inference-time search into the model parameters, GIFT captures the benefits of test-time scaling while reducing inference compute by 80%. It improves mean IoU by 12% over a strong supervised baseline and remains competitive with more complex multimodal systems, without requiring additional human annotation or specialized architectures.

</details>


### [169] [FlowRL: A Taxonomy and Modular Framework for Reinforcement Learning with Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27450)
*Chenxiao Gao,Edward Chen,Tianyi Chen,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对扩散/流策略强化学习的统一分类框架，并开发了模块化JAX代码库和标准化基准测试，以解决该领域缺乏统一视角和高效工具的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流模型在策略表示中具有出色的灵活性，但在这些策略上进行高效强化学习仍具挑战，因为缺乏显式对数概率用于策略梯度估计。现有方法分散，缺乏统一视角，阻碍了领域发展。

Method: 1) 提出RL与扩散/流策略的综合分类法；2) 开发模块化JAX开源代码库，利用JIT编译实现高吞吐训练；3) 在Gym-Locomotion、DeepMind Control Suite和IsaacLab上进行系统化基准测试。

Result: 提供扩散策略方法的严格对比分析，为从业者选择合适算法提供指导。

Conclusion: 本文为理解和算法设计建立了清晰基础，提供了高效研究工具及生成模型与机器人领域的算法指南。

Abstract: Thanks to their remarkable flexibility, diffusion models and flow models have emerged as promising candidates for policy representation. However, efficient reinforcement learning (RL) upon these policies remains a challenge due to the lack of explicit log-probabilities for vanilla policy gradient estimators. While numerous attempts have been proposed to address this, the field lacks a unified perspective to reconcile these seemingly disparate methods, thus hampering ongoing development. In this paper, we bridge this gap by introducing a comprehensive taxonomy for RL algorithms with diffusion/flow policies. To support reproducibility and agile prototyping, we introduce a modular, JAX-based open-source codebase that leverages JIT-compilation for high-throughput training.
